__________________________________________

As it turned out my pokemon had for all intents and purposes, claimed a part of the large dome building as their own territory. I was directed to follow the largest of the sprawling rivers leading away from the centre of the area, into a large sprawling and dense forest area filled with a wide variety of berry trees. It opened up into another smaller pooling of water at the very end inside what appeared to be a clearing in the middle of the forest area, stationed right in front of a large rocky plateau.

And everywhere as far as my eye could see I saw my pokemon. I saw Golbat hanging upside down from a tree beside Butterfree, Beedrill buzzing above, zooming drills through the air. Persian and my dozens of Meowth were lounging practically everywhere. Poliwrath was on the banks of the river trading blows with Shiftry as Krabby rested off to the side of them watching them fight with keen interest, while throughout the small lake they'd claimed I saw dozens of Squirtle and Wartortle.

"!" A chirping squeaking chitter of excitement drew my eyes upwards at the very top of the rocky plateau and found Raticate and Golem staring down at me.

"Gol!" Golem beamed and waved his arms at me happily and excitedly, and was joined a moment later as Clefable waddled over to peer down at the top and wave to me as well.

Raticate wasn't content with that though-

And the ball of brown fur outright launched himself from the very top of the rocky plateau, leaping straight down towards me. He was a blur of motion-

But I'm Jord and I lift Gyarados' for shits and giggles.

So my arms came up and caught him a moment later, and if I grunted and lost my balance momentarily because of the sheer speed he leapt down at me with, well shut up.

"Heya mate I've missed you!" I laughed as Raticate wasted no time in thrusting his fuzzy whiskered face against mine and nuzzling me for all he was worth.

"Ratataicate!" he babbled against my face.

The commotion Raticate made drew the attention of all the other pokemon around us. And honestly, it was a wonder I wasn't caught before, I had literally walked through dozens of my Exeggutor who had barely glanced at me.

Still, even as eyes lit up and dozens of pokemon rushed over to me, while others stayed back and wearily eyed me such as the Squirtle and Watortle, I noticed something about the rat pokemon in my arms.

"Have you gotten bigger buddy?" I laughed, lifting him high up into the air above my head and examining him.

"Ratta!" he nodded his head as an affirmative bobbing up and down in my grasp.

I thought so, he was definitely a decent bit heavier than I remember as well. In the short time we've been apart, he has to have grown by at least four or five inches!

"You've been really working hard, huh?" I brought him back down and hugged him to my chest, "All of you have, I saw on my pokedex." I looked around at the pokemon who had all approached me.

Mostly those who had been on my squad or switched around, those who stayed away where ones I hadn't really interacted with yet beyond catching.

Shiftry smirked and gave me a single nod, while Poliwrath gave me the Zyzz flex and a twin pair of thumbs up. I lowered Raticate to the ground at my feet, but no sooner did I do that, did a familiar buzzing noise come from behind me and Beedrill landed atop my back, perching his massive lance arms over my shoulders and hugging himself to my back-

And the earth rumbled ominously as Golem dropped from the rock plateau, followed by the dainty floating figure of Clefable slowly following down behind him.

"When did you get so clingy?" I groused with a roll of my eyes at Beedrill, even as I pat one of his lances and greeted Golem with a fond pat on the head, "Weren't you the silent confident badass type like Shiftry? Or did you miss me that much?"

"Bzzz!" Beedrill buzzed and smacked his head against the back of my own lightly, before pulling back-

Just in time for Golbat's massive bulk to perch atop my shoulders, "Really?" I rolled my eyes as he smacked me across the cheek with his massive, long tongue and chittered in amusement.

One by one I was greeted by my pokemon, Persian strolling up halfway through and rubbing up against my side with a purr and plopping down at my side when I took a seat on a large boulder I was directed too as if it were a throne.

As I did, a familiar massive crab pokemon hopped up beside me and greeted me with a happy wave of one of his claws, "Hey Krabby, doing good?" I greeted him.

"Kookee!" he thumped me on the shoulder and beamed. It was just a light tip, but it throbbed. Man he had some real power behind those claws. He really was massive too.

I can't wait to see the kind of monster he'll be when he evolves.

"Good to hear mate." I bumped my fist again his claw, it was more than twice the size of my fist.

As I pulled back, there was a rustle in the tree above me, and Snivy dropped out of the tree to land on my shoulder and lay herself across it in a lazy yet somehow graceful lounge.

I need to learn that, it had a feel of such grand lazy contemptuous arrogance that it would be perfect for not only showing off, but pissing people off.

Basically right up my alley way.

Good thing I planned on bringing her along with me when I set out again soon.

"Oh yeah, have a seat why don't you." I rolled my eyes in greeting.

"Sni." Snivy sniffed, giving me a lazy lidded look and smirk.

I should learn that too. Like yeah I plan on being a better person and all, but again, baby steps, and I never promised to be a better person to the likes of Cross. I will only use this power of aggravating arrogance once I obtain it, on people who deserve it.

Speaking of starters who belonged to other trainers previously though, "Any idea where Raboot is?" I asked.

Snivy nodded simply, before one of her vines reached up into the tree she just leapt down onto me from, and came back down a second little pulling along with it an embarrassed looking Raboot.

"Woah," I blinked as he was simply dropped swept over him as he was dropped into my lap by Snivy, "Nice of you to drop by." I greeted him.

The white fur of his face flushed a bit red somehow, no idea how and he grunted, giving me a raised hand in greeting, "Boot."

Awkward little guy huh? Not that I blame him. How could he develop social skills being around a friendless, maidenless weirdo like Goh after all? It's no wonder I not only stole his starter, but his childhood friend as well.

And plan to steal his dream while I'm at it too.

Hmm, maybe I should beat Mew with Raboot and record it?

Thoughts for later.

"I saw you learned Flamethrower while you were here," I said, before reaching down and covering the entirety of his small head with my palm and rubbing it gently, "Good job, you've been working hard huh? You'll be a real beast soon mate."

"Ra..." he ducked his head to avoid my gaze bashfully, the cool facade he tried to display melting away in moments as he avoided my gaze. A shy guy for sure despite outward appearances.

"Mrrawo." Persian gave a little purring chuckle of amusement as he eyed the rabbit in my lap, who glared at him sullenly.

Chuckling myself I continued patting Raboot's head and turned my attention to the feline pokemon by my side, who while sitting beside my reached almost chest level, even with me sitting on a small boulder as a seat.

Persian as a species really were a lot bigger than most thought.

"So how's the place been treating you?" I asked, "You and your group been settling in alright?"

Persian merely gave me a smiling, raised brow smirk and butt his head against my knee, laying his head across my knees beside where Raboot was resting.

Very casual, and said all that needed to be said.

"Good to know mate," I nodded, "Will the Meowth be alright without you? I was thinking of bringing you along when I next set out. Alongside Raboot, Snivy and Krabby here." I nodded at the other three pokemon lounging around me.

Krabby perked up noticeably, "Kookee?" he pointed a claw at himself.

"Hell yeah you're coming," I nodded, "You were super strong in the battle against Milotic without any training at all. I can't wait to see how strong I can train you to be."

I didn't know it was possible for red shelled crabs to blush, but somehow Krabby managed it, bubbles foaming at his mouth, before he pumped his claw in happiness.

"Mraow..." Persian purred thoughtfully, before simply nodding his head at my question.

"Good to know." I said, I'm planning on taking quite a few pokemon out with me. Putting aside my fossil pokemon that I don't really plan on training for battle besides the one Kabutops and Aerodactyl, they'll all be coming with me regardless. They're leverage to get old man Oak to get my place built up good and proper as quick as possible.

But battler wise, that was Persian, Raboot, Snivy, Krabby and Kabutops.

Pidgeot, Charizard and Fearow are all of course coming. They are my flight team after all. and parallel to them are my swimming team Milotic, Gyarados and Blastoise. Which brings the total up to eleven.

Raticate and Golem are coming for sure. Raticate has put in some real work lately and deserves it, and Golem is needed for a few of my plans to go through.

...There's a pair of legendaries I can get easy access to and might be able to bring down in one fell swoop with Golem's explosion. They were powerful, incredibly so. But were glass cannons.

Actually.

I wonder, what about Moltres? I had planned on avoiding it for now, but I know the rough area of where a Moltres nest is, actually not that far off of Pallet Town. It was in the mountains near the edge of Professor Oak's land. I'd generally not thought much about it because I was dead set sure that me and my pokemon couldn't do anything to it right now. After all, it had traded hands with Ash's Infernape and had the upper hand.

...But after that battle with Lance, I'm not so sure. I might actually be able to do it.

Especially with Altaria on my squad now.

...Which was another I was bringing along, alongside Aerodactyl.

Which was fifteen right then and there of main battlers. Over twenty in total with the squad of fossil pokemon.

Not to mention you know-

Togepi, Dragonite and Rotom.

And I want to bring one of my Exeggutor along as well to see if I can train it to take advantage of its three heads like Lance did with his Hydreigon.

Which made nineteen active battlers.

Thank fuck I don't have a carry limit anymore because fucking hell man.

I'm gonna need to stock up on a lot of pokemon food huh? Good thing I'm loaded now.

Well, while I'm still here, I'll need to pick out what Exeggutor I want to use. And see about having Butterfree and my other grass types teach it some attacks. Like the powder attacks, Psychic, Sunny Day, Solar Beam, Teleport.

That kind of thing.

As always, the grind never waited.

_______________________

I ended up sticking around the Pokemon Center for another three days. There was quite a few things I had to do before I set out after all.

Plus I was waiting on my gauntlet from Silph Co being made and transferred over to me.

For one, I had to teach all my pokemon Tera Blast.

Well, all the pokemon I would actively be using in battle, so it wasn't counting the other massive groups of pokemon I had like the Meowth, the squirtle and wartortle and the Exeggutor beyond the strongest one I'd picked out.

…Well I say strongest, but I honestly just picked out the one that had the most moves already known.

There wasn't much personality between the Exeggutor for me to narrow down anything else. They were rather…well not dull, but easy going pokemon as a whole.

They had rather prodigious mental capabilities though, and the one I picked out as my main Exeggutor at the very least, picked up things very fast.

In just three days it picked up a bunch of attacks from my other pokemon. The spore type attacks actually weren't needed to be learned, it had already known them, as it had Psychic.

To start with, Exeggutor had already known: Hypnosis, Stun Spore, Sleep Powder, Poison Powder, Barrier, Psychic, Stomp, Barrage and Leech Seed.

Sunny Day, Solar Beam and Teleport however were picked up quite quickly. And beyond that, was one more move that would be very vital going forward that I had Exeggutor work on with Golem.

Explosion.

Just thinking about it made me shudder in excitement. A barrage of status ailment powders from each head at once. One head to protect with Barrier, another to harass with Psychic and the last to slap hard with Leech Seed.

Exeggutor would be a horror to battle.

And if he was ever on the backfoot and going to go down? Boom! Explosion!

Exeggutor will become the ultimate fuck you pokemon.

_____________________________________________

"Damn, it looks so cool."

A laugh left my throat as I looked down into the opened box sitting atop my bed for the duration of my stay in Viridian City.

A gleaming metallic silver gauntlet sat perched before my eyes within the confines of the box. It had just arrived not long ago.

And it looked amazing.

It was designed to reach up to my elbow and was all sleek, shiny and are I say elegant? At the back of the fist of the gauntlet, there was a screen that according to the instructions would allow me to keep track of all that was contained within it and other such things.

For instance, it was designed to be able to slot together with a pokedex, or even phones.

And in the middle of the palm there was a spherical indent that would open up to allow items to be absorbed inside it and stored. It truly was a marvel to look at.

Lifting it up, I eyed the inside of it and couldn't stop the grin from spreading across my face as I beheld the soft velvety inlaid insides. It was made specifically to be comfortable while I wore it.

I slid my hand into it and clenched my fist, metallic clad fingers curling easily and seamlessly. No stiffness at all.

The craftsmanship on this thing is incredible.

"Alright then." I licked my lips and reached for my belt where twenty seven literal shrunken pokeballs hung from my belt.

Nineteen active battling and training pokemon.

Pidgeot, Charizard, Fearow, Blissey, Milotic, Blastoise, Gyarados, Raticate, Golem, Persian, Raboot, Snivy, Krabby, Kabutops, Aerodactyl, Altaria, Dragonite, Togepi, Exeggutor.

Eight tag longs in the form of my other Kabutops, the two Kabuto, the two Omastar, the two Omanyte and that spunky female Wartortle of mine. the fossil pokemon would be sticking with me for now until Casa De Jord is fully built.

Which according to Professor Oak would only be a month. Man that old man worked fast when he wanted to research pokemon.

And finally, the one I just plucked from my belt.

"Come on out Rotom." I released the pokemon from the confines of its pokeball in a burst of silvery light.

"Kekekekekekkekeekke!" a chortling snicker met my ears immediately as Rotom appeared before me.

The little electric ghost pokemon had barely materialised before it started zipping around the room at blurring speeds.

If it weren't for the fact I was so used to pokemon battling now and training my body to the brink, I wouldn't even be able to see the little twit moving it was so fast.

Surge was right about one thing.

Rotom as a whole were irritating little shits.

Though he had told me they calmed down a lot more when they entered a piece of technology and gained a more solid body. Something about them being purely ethereal as a ghost type meant they were in a state of constant motion like electricity itself.

I don't really get it, Surge explained it to me but it went mostly over my head. I suppose I could call him and get him to explain it to me.

He'd left last night, alongside Professor Cerise and Chloe.

…And I'd gotten the phone numbers of all three of them. I'd struck up an odd sort of friendship with Surge, and he'd even offered me the chance to come train with him when I had the time, he'd told me he was interested in seeing how I would do getting put through the same kind of soldier boot camp training he'd went through.

And honestly if I weren't so busy I'd have taken him up on the offer. There were bound to be loads of training techniques in such a training course that would help me a shit ton.

Professor Cerise was pretty self explanatory. He wanted the chance to study a lot of my pokemon when I had the time.

And Chloe? Well, she had a little bit of soul searching to do. To decide on what she really wanted to do with her life going forward. That, and we'd slept together every night she'd been in Viridian City.

So exchanging numbers was kind of self explanatory…again.

On that topic, Professor Oak and Delia had left just this morning as well. Before they got waylaid by my 'discovery' of the living fossil pokemon, they'd been on their way to Porta Vista for a little holiday and to meet up with Ash and Gary to check in on them.

Well either way, as it was before he'd left I'd had a talk with him, Professor Oak that was. Specifically about the home being built for me and my pokemon.

I'd been inspired by the massive dome building my pokemon had been living within here at Viridian City. A Terrarium Biodome it was called, Biome for short. A massive building terrarium where the insides were build specifically to reflect certain climates and habitats for all kinds of pokemon, multiple different ones side by side throughout the insides even.

I wanted one.

Luckily for me, Professor Oak was the right guy to talk to if I wanted to get one. With his help, it was surprisingly easy to purchase the amount of land I wanted.

So twelve million pokedollars went down the drain instantly right there to purchase a hundred and fifty acres of land, bringing the total amount of land I own up to a hundred and sixty acres.

And then it cost another eighteen million for the biome.

Which was surprisingly cheap. But then, in this world where pokemon could build entire houses in a day and the league going out of their way to endorse trainers, maybe I shouldn't have been surprised.

Either way, I was brought down to just over a hundred and thirty million pokedollars in total, and I'd have a property ranging for one hundred and sixty acres and a biome that would cover roughly one hundred acres of that land.

Which was perfect since it left plenty of space for any pokemon that didn't want to live inside the biome.

I held my hand up, "Rotom, come." I commanded, holding my gauntlet clad fist out into the open air.

The zipping bolt of electricity that was Rotom froze to a halt mid-air a few feet away from me-

And before I could even look it in the eye, the little electric ghost buzzed out of existence, forming into a bolt of electricity that zipped towards me and slammed into the gauntlet atop my fist like a lightning bolt to a lightning rod.

And before my eyes, the gauntlet began to shift and change. Its already sleek form rippled around my arm and fastened perfectly around my limb and the shining silver bled and darkened into a crimson red.

"Huh." I blinked, "Eat your heart out Tony Stark." I snorted.

It almost looked like I was wearing one of the Iron Man gauntlets. Even the indent in the middle of the palm lit up with a soft glow akin to the repulsors.

And at the back of the fist, the screen lit up with a pair of spherical blue indents forming at the top of it that formed the shape of eyes.

"Rotom Gauntlet is online!" a mechanical voice chirped from the gauntlet.

"Everything alright Rotom?" I asked, holding the gauntlet up to my face and looking into the eyes now at the back of my fist.

"All systems are running perfectly," Rotom chirped, "This is interesting, I have never felt so calm and at peace before. Trainer Jord, are you perhaps a genius?"

"Yes." I nodded humbly.

"Systems indicate a lie going by your pulse," Rotom hummed, "Well no matter, genius or fool, Rotom Gauntlet is ready to serve!"

Rude.

As expected of my pokemon I suppose.

No matter.

"Did you learn any new attacks from possessing the gauntlet?" I asked curiously.

"Data indicates that I have indeed learned a new attack, but am incapable of scanning myself to find out which." Rotom replied, "Data also indicates a change in typing, but again, lack the software or hardware needed to scan myself fully."

I hummed, reaching into my pocket and pulling out my phone, "The gauntlet is supposed to be able to connect to a pokedex or phone," I pointed out and held it up before the gauntlet, "Store this inside and see if you can get access to the pokedex app inside, it should be able to tell you."

"Roger that!" Rotom chirped.

The indent in the middle of the palm glowed brighter, before suddenly the phone in my hand was slurped inside.

The way it warped as it shrank inside, made it look like an odd noodle.

I stared at the gauntlet for a few seconds, waiting.

"Connection complete!" Rotom relayed after those few seconds, "Pokedex scan indicates that Rotom Gauntlet is an Electric and Fighting Type pokemon. The ability remains levitate. Known moves: Shock Wave, Thunder Wave, Confuse Ray, Shadow Ball, Double Team, Tera Blast and Mach Punch."

Hmm. Mach Punch Huh? And as I predicted, it really did become an electric and fighting type.

Still, that was somewhat disappointing, I was hoping for Aura Sphere or something.

Well, Mach Punch isn't so bad I suppose. Like Crabhammer it is a pretty interesting attack with two variations. One clads the fist with energy for a physical blow, the other allows to fire off a long range fist of force and energy.

Hmm, I wanted to try something.

"Rotom, Double Team." I ordered.

I wanted to see if-

"Roger!" Rotom chirped. The gauntlet on my hand shimmered, before spreading across my body-

And a second later, multiple gauntlet clad versions of me were spread about the room.

A massive grin spread across my face, "Oh yeah, I can work with this," I guffawed. Shaping my fingers into a gun shape I levelled my finger at one of the clones, "Thunder Wave." I ordered, keeping it low powered while inside.

The gauntlet on my fist lit up with electricity and a thin bolt leapt forth from my pistol shaped fingers and pierced through the illusionary clone, making it burst apart.

______________________________________________________________________

# 167: Chapter 166

Sorry for not updating on here lately guys, it's just kind of slipped my mind. With everything going on, and trying to rebuild my shit on Subscribestar...and dealing with a new puppy, it's been a whole thing.

But there's a bunch of stuff up on my subscribestar if you guys want to read ahead. For stuff like this, Systematic Shinobi, Stacking The Deck, Bleached Invader, and a ton of other stories

_________________________________________

I had a list of things to do after finally leaving Viridian City behind. And as I soared into the air atop Pidgeot's back once more, there was a big smile on my face.

Mostly because of the put upon sigh Nurse Joy had given me alongside an eye roll when I gave her a cheeky hug and told her to be ready to receive even more crazy pokemon.

Which was a lie because beyond a few, most of the pokemon I catch I'll keep with me for the first while before sending back. And it will only be a month before my own place is ready.

'…Granted, I'll need to see about finding trustworthy people to employ.' I mused. It wasn't like I could just stay there all the time after all.

Still, I had a bunch of things to do before I left Kanto, that I plan on taking care of all today.

I had to hit up Fennel Valley and find that Tyranitar. I had to pay a visit to Misty, Ash and Brock, to drop off Wartortle and those Leaf Stones. I had to call Hilda, she'd only sent me a few texts over the past few days and told me to call her for a real talk when I wasn't as busy.

I'll call her when I leave Kanto.

…And hell, before that I want to pay a visit to that Moltres and try my luck.

Still…

'Moltres, huh?' I hummed.

A legendary pokemon.

And I'm on my way to Fennel Valley too.

I'd specifically kept it out of my thoughts before because I hadn't liked my chances so far, but…if I can take a Moltres, then couldn't I possibly take it as well?

It being the Regice I knew lived in the ruins of Fennel Valley. Specifically, the Regice, Brandon caught during Battle Frontier just prior to his final battle with Ash Ketchum.

Could I take it?

That Regice is on another level compared to Regileki and Regidrago. Sure, they were powerful as hell, but they were glass cannons. An explosion from Golem would deal tons of damage to them and leave them vulnerable.

Regice on the other hand?

That thing took tens of dozens of attacks from Ash's Pikachu at the end of Battle Frontier when it hadn't even been trained by Brandon yet. Dozens of Iron Tails, Thunderbolts and even Volt Tackles.

As I said, it was on a completely different level from Regileki and Regidrago.

…But, just like Regileki and Regidrago, it is in an enclosed space. Making it easy to land attacks.

And not only do I have Golem, but I have Exeggutor as well. Sure, Exeggutor isn't on Golem's level, but the trio of spores to paralyse it, put it to sleep and poison it would swing things in my favour.

And then explosion for massive damage.

And then Golem to stall it, land some hits and then go for another explosion. It would be slowed down, sluggish and taking constant damage before the two explosions came into the picture.

Wouldn't that weaken it well enough for Charizard to come in and finish it off? Regice might have been incredibly powerful as a legendary but it wasn't a champion tier pokemon considering when Ash beat it.

Speaking of champions though-

'If I do go ahead with it, this will cause another massive stir.' I mused. Would Lance be able to quash that before it became an issue?

Probably not.

Honestly, it wasn't like Regice was the only one I know the location of either. While I don't know where the Registeel that Brandon caught is, I do know where the Regirock he caught is as well.

Though that one was more out of the way for me than Regice.

"Whatever, I'll think about it more after I catch Tyranitar." I mused.

Delegating all my problematic thoughts to future Jord sure is blissful.

Fennel Valley really wasn't that far away from Viridian City at all. All in, it took only a half an hour before we were circling above the area.

It was a beautiful place I had to admit.

The valley itself was surrounded on all sides by dozens of conjoined mountains. It was a range of mountains forming together in a sphere, cutting off from most of the outside world beyond one road that lead through.

Within the confines of those mountains was a land of sprawling green and crystal lakes and rivers intersecting all throughout and disappearing into the mountains themselves.

And atop the tallest peak of the tallest mountain, was an ancient ruin of what once might have been a castle, but was now no more than mausoleum to time.

And in that ruin, lay a slumbering Regice just waiting for someone to one day come and awaken it.

But that wasn't for me at this split moment, maybe in a little while.

For now though.

'Now where are you.' I mused as Pidgeot cruised through the air above the mountains.

And he wasn't the only one.

Fearow, Dragonite, Charizard, Aerodactyl and even Altaria. Each of the pokemon I had with me capable of flight was out searching for a big green rocky godzilla wannabe.

"Togepri!" Togepi who was nestled in my arms giggled joyfully as Pidgeot dipped down into a small dive, passing over a cave alcove mid-way up one of the mountains to peek for Tyranitar.

But no luck.

"Dude!" A geodude within the cave tossed me a wave.

"Hey." I waved back as we passed by.

After a while, I directed Pidgeot to perch atop one of the mountains to wait for my other pokemon.

To see if they had any luck.

And after a while, they came back, one by one. First Aerodactyl, then Altaria. They were followed by Dragonite and then Charizard.

They had no luck at all.

'Maybe it isn't here?' I mused thoughtfully. Timeline wise, it would actually be a couple years before Ash and co would have ran across it. So perhaps it just hadn't turned up here yet?

That would suck.

If it wasn't here, the only other Tyranitar in the wild I know about is one on Mount Silver and one in Alola.

Both are problems. One was watched over by Pokemon Rangers if I remember correctly.

And the other was stupid far away in Alola.

I sighed, honestly I-

"Row!" a sharp familiar cry made me look up to see Fearow zipping towards us, a great big smug grin on his long beak.

…And he was followed by a full flock of other Fearow? A good ten to twelve of them I'd say.

---

---

---

# 168: Chapter 167

So yeah, it's been a real long ass time since I uploaded on here. Honestly, I just kind of forgot to upload here? Sorry about that. My life has just been pretty hectic. I haven't stopped writing at all mind you, slowed down a little but, but picked up again in speed recently. And if you wanna read advanced chapters, you can check them out on my Subscribestar, same name I use on here,- 0Jordinio0

Also wanna thank you guys who got in contact with me about the dudes who keep plagarising my stories, like that one dude who keeps re-uploading To Go Even Further Beyond every time he gets reported and the story taken down.

_______________________

He landed before Pidgeot, Togepi and I moments later, his new…friends? Landing beside him a few seconds later.

Looking at him beside them all, he positively towered over them. He had practically twice the mass of any of the other Fearow.

Yeah, that's my try hard bird!

"Make some new friends mate?" I grinned at him, raising an eyebrow. Does he want me to catch them?

I'd planned on slowing down on that kind of thing, catching big groups of the same pokemon like a collector ever since I had my epiphany with Surge. But, if Fearow wanted to bring his friends along and they were in agreement, then I would.

Fearow being happy is more important after all than me being a better person.

"Feeeerow!" he puffed his chest out.

Looking them over, there were eleven in total not counting my Fearow.

"Any luck?" I asked Fearow, though my hopes for Tyranitar were already rock bottom.

No matter. It wasn't that big of a deal I suppose.

"Row row Fearow row!" Fearow shook his head and barked back, before gesturing to his new buddies, "Row row fee!"

"Fearow claims he had no luck finding the Tyranitar you seek," Rotom offered from my hand, almost making me jump in surprise, my eyes widening, how could I forget Rotom is a pokemon and could understand them. And can now translate for me! "But as he was searching he came across this flock of Fearow and sought information on Tyranitar's where abouts. They refused to give out such information and came to blows, Fearow quickly defeating the leader of the flock…and now they have bowed to Fearow as their new leader."

Huh.

It hasn't even been a half an hour and my Fearow had literally taken over another flock casually while searching for something for me.

That's one of my try hards for you. Being an epic try hard even when not meaning to.

I shook my head, "So you want to bring them along then?" I asked Fearow, looking them over.

While they weren't as big as my Fearow, they weren't exactly small little things either. And there weren't any Spearow among them. I'm not exactly sure why.

They could be useful I suppose. A team of flying bird scouts.

"Fearow row row." Fearow sighed, before shrugging his wings and nodding.

"Fearow claims that while it wasn't his intention at first, he has to take responsibility for accepting the battle in the first place," Rotom relayed, "Though if you do not want to bring them along, he will send them away."

I rolled my eyes, "Nah if you want to take responsibility, I'll catch them," I shrugged, again Fearow's happiness is important. It sure is hard trying to be a better person, "Though they won't be able to come along with us, you'll be able to see them when we visit, more so when our new home is built."

Fearow nodded, before looking over at the assembled flock of Fearow and barking out a few things at them.

And then they began giving crowing cheers from their long beaks, wings raised in celebration.

Huh.

I guess they really like Fearow?

I sighed, "It's too bad about Tyranitar I suppose."

As I said that, Fearow turned back to me and blinked, before his eyes widened and he began to wave his wings frantically, "Row row fearow fearow row!"

A frantic apology?

Really, there's no need to feel that bad about not finding Tyranitar and getting caught up with-

"Fearow apologises for not stating so sooner, but he demanded all the flock of Fearow knew about the Tyranitar you seek before accepting his place as the leader of the flock," Rotom cut his thoughts off, "He has already found where the Tyranitar is nesting."

…Oh.

Huh.

I grinned, hopping off Pidgeot's back, and holding Togepi in one arm I wrapped Fearow in a big hug, "Well done mate!" I laughed, "Look at you go, taking over another group of Fearow and finding Tyranitar at the same time! you're amazing mate!"

"Row!" Fearow beamed joyfully.

"Geot!" Pidgeot huffed, rolling his eyes and flicked his long mane of hair back.

_________________

As it turned out, Tyranitar had taken up residence in a cave on the opposite side of the valley from the ancient ruin where Regice resided.

Whether that was intentional or not to avoid the legendary pokemon I couldn't say.

Nor did it really matter.

After catching the flock of eleven Fearow and returning…Fearow. I hopped back up on Pidgeots back and we made our way over to where my new flock had told me where Tyranitar resided.

And it wasn't long before we found it.

It was truly massive. A towering behemoth well over ten feet tall, made up of purely muscled rocky plate that was lounging over a massive boulder lazily, directly until the suns warm rays.

It narrowed its eyes at me and Pidgeot as we landed a good ten feet away, but didn't tense up at all. It didn't feel threatened at all.

As expected of a Pseudo Legendary.

"Tyranitar," I pointed to it with a grin in greeting, "I've come to catch you."

I'd already recalled Togepi for now.

Tyranitar scoffed as it pushed itself up off the rock it was tanning on, rolling its slitted eyes at me and Pidgeot.

This is a good time for one of my pokemon to make their first official battle debut and not just training battles with my other pokemon.

I pressed a few buttons on the back of my Rotom Gauntlet and a moment later, a gleaming ebony black luxury ball deposited into my hand from the gauntlet.

"Let's go, Blastoise!" I said, releasing the pokemon from within the luxury ball in a burst of bright light.

---

---

# 169: Chapter 168

Changed the cover art.

___________________________

"…Blast…" Blastoise sighed in resignation as he appeared before me and beheld his towering opponent. The behemoth of a Tyranitar had a good five feet on him in height.

Tyranitar on the other hand smirked cockily and pointed one massive green clawed hand towards Blastoise. A spiral of purple and black power erupted into existence, a dark pulse attack roaring towards Blastoise in the blink of an eye.

I didn't need to say or command anything.

Blastoise immediately retreated into his shell, which then gleamed as Withdraw came into effect. The Dark Pulse slammed into his shell-

And bounced off, ricocheting back towards the Tyranitar who didn't blink an eye and back handed the spiralling beam of black and purple casually into the sky.

"Rapid Spin." I commanded.

Blastoise's shell made a great impression of a beyblade - the new and improved Draciel - as his shell began to rotate at rapid speeds and he zipped through the air like an artillery shell towards the behemoth of a Tyranitar.

Clawed hands caught Blastoise mid-air, his shell grinding against Tyranitar's hands. Tyranitar was pushed back a good four or five metres before it dug its clawed feet into the ground and stopped itself from behind pushed back, and slammed Blastoise into the ground, its mouth opening and a familiar crackling dark sphere of powering humming into existence within its maw.

That was fine.

"Hydro Pump!" I shouted next.

With a grunt, Blastoise appeared from his shell on all fours, head level with Tyranitar's stomach as he was held down.

As were his massive shoulder cannons that appeared out of his shell as well.

Massive twin jets of finely compressed water cannons erupted from the shoulder cannons, each capable of crumpling a double decker bus in an instant and slammed into Tyranitar's stomach with bone crunching force.

The Tyranitar roared in pain as its armoured stomach was tore into by the massive water jets and it was forced to let go as it was sent flying back through the air.

Casual look on its face gone, Tyranitar directed an angered howl and didn't stop its attack.

It unleashed the Hyper Beam it was charging towards Blastoise, in the form of a massive black beam of pure destruction.

Before it could hit though, Blastoise was already moving, retreating into his shell and spinning out of the way.

At the same time Tyranitar slammed into the ground feet first over twenty feet away, hardly phased by the point blank Hydro Pump.

Or so it would seem.

With a snarl, it slammed its fist into the ground and the mountain rumbled. Massive spires of rock erupted from where its fist impacted, travelling in a road towards Blastoise.

Blastoise could have easily dodged the Stone Edge with Aqua Jet. But, I didn't call for that.

Instead a beam of red light washed over Beyblade Blastoise as I returned him to his gleaming black pokeball and quickly switched on the fly to another in less than a second.

Such was the utility of my new Rotom Gauntlet.

"Let's go, Snivy." I declared, releasing my second pokemon. It was time to test her out.

I released her upwards, allowing her to materialize upon the top of one of the huge rocky spires elegantly, "Sunny Day, babe." I clicked my fingers.

Snivy represented my bond with Hilda. And what better debut for my elegant little grass snake, than to down a Pseudo Legendary?

Snivy narrowed her eyes, not even missing a beat as her green snout upon her head raising in excitement as the heat intensified with a flash of light, and an orb of pure solar energy erupted from her body, shooting high into the air above and the clouded sky above us burned away in the span of mere seconds.

All that was left was a blazing, harshly hot sun above us that the moment it was established, born from her power and lent its energy to her, "Solarbeam!" I ordered, "Right through the rocks, twist your head!"

"Sni!" she cried out in confirmation, her little mouth stretching wide open like the serpent she was, and in all of a split moment, unleashed a golden beam of solar power multiple times the size of her body. It ploughed through the first few rocky spires in the blink of an eye, before she swept her head to the side-

The beam angled diagonally, sweeping through each of the rocky spires at different levels, and also sweeping across the length of the mountain.

A roar of pain sounded out moments later.

And as the debris cleared from the rocky spires being demolished, it was to show Tyranitar, half way towards me from the left - looking like it was planning to flank Blastoise initially - arms crossed over its face to protect itself, and arms simmering from where it most likely blocked the hit from Solarbeam.

It shook its arms out with a gruff huff and cocked its head, looking up at Snivy, and just stared for a moment. Then it raised a brow, and scoffed, shaking its head in amusement.

"...Sni!?" an aggrieved spit shot from Snivy's little mouth, now back to normal as she glared at the Tyranitar, that just totally disregarded her.

Tyranitar smirked and lifted one clawed hand, and showed off two of its claws held a miniscule few centimetres apart. And then wiggled its claws mockingly at Snivy and laughed.

Well that just wasn't nice.

It didn't see Snivy as a threat at all because of her small size. Silly considering just a moment ago it had roared in pain from blocking her Solarbeam.

Oh well, it would come to regret that-

"SNIVY!" an angered shriek erupted from Snivy's mouth as she glared in fury down at the Tyranitar.

And then her body exploded.

Into glowing, blinding light.

...Huh.

Before my eyes she began to grow and change. Her body doubled in size and length, stubby arms and legs elongated, a crest akin to a small crown appearing atop her head, and her spade-tail ballooning.

# 170: Chapter 169

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

________________________

And then the light burst apart to reveal-

"Servine!" my newly evolved pokemon declared with gusto, never losing the glare in her eyes at all. I looked over at Tyranitar, to find it frozen stiff.

Huh, was it that shocked by her evolving? honestly, not that I was expecting it either, this is our first battle together for crying out loud. But then, I suppose she'd been with Hilda for over a year. And she'd spent a few weeks around my pokemon and taking part in our training. And then continuing to train with my other pokemon at the Viridian City Pokemon Centre...

Well...fair enough.

"Notice, pokememon Snivy has evolved into Servine," Rotom Gauntlet spoke up from my fist, "Through evolution, Servine has acquired the attacks Mirror Coat and Glare."

Wait, what!?

Oh, that was why it was frozen stiff!

With a shout, my newly evolved Servine leapt into the air. A swirling vortex of green glowing leaves erupted into existence around her and started rapidly rotating around her body. then, she thrust her newly evolved and longer arms down towards Tyrantar, and the glowing leaves congregated together and spiralled downwards towards Tyranitar in the form of a roaring hurricane of razor sharp energy leaves.

Frozen stiff by Servine's Glare attack, it had no way to protect itself, and Tyranitar was peppered head on with the leaf Hurricane as its entire body was tossed up into the air like a ragdoll, and carried through the air to slam into the cave side atop the mountain with thundering force, a great big crater forming within the wall.

Well alright then!

"Quick, before it recovers girl!" I shouted, getting her attention as she landed back atop the rocky spire created by Tyranitar, "Solarbeam, again!"

"Ser!" she beamed with a wide grin and a nod. Her head turned towards Tyrantar once more, jaws unhinging and spreading wide open as a crackling orb of golden solar power hummed into existence.

And was fired a split moment later.

The size of the beam was a good fifty percent bigger than the one she'd fired before evolving too! That was incredible growth!

Tyranitar was in the midst of breaking out of its crater with a grunt when the golden Solarbeam slammed into it and sent it careening backwards, straight into the side of the mountain it was slammed against originally. It didn't even have time to react before the massive golden beam of solar grass energy washed over it and crushed it back into the mountainside.

Servine gave a coquettish little scoffing smile as she cut off the power of her attack after a few seconds and hopped off the rocky spire to land elegantly in front of me, a proud loom upon her face.

"Bit too early to celebrate don't you think?" I raised a brow at her, and looked over at the Tyranitar that sat in a slump within the crater, head bowed and eyes half shut.

It looked mightily dazed.

"Ser." Servine shrugged and sent a smug look Tyranitar's way.

Only to blink as the Tyranitar huffed and lifted its arms to grasp the edge of the crater it was lodged within, and pull itself out with a mighty heave that crumped the stone in its hands.

It stared daggers at Servine, and then craned its neck to the side, a cracking sounding echoing, and then it turned its head to the other and repeated it.

That was some stamina this thing had. A Rapid spin, albeit blocked, a point blank super effective Hydro Pump to the stomach, a super effective Solarbeam, a super effective Leaf Storm and another super effect Solarbeam and it was still chugging along.

'Nice.' I thought.

I liked that endurance.

It pointed a clawed finger at Servine, before suddenly its body was suffused with a familiar draconic green glow.

Dragon Dance!

"Servine, Vine Whip!" I ordered.

With a shout, she spun, twin vines erupting from her shoulders and lashing out, crossing the distance in the blink of an eye, but just as they were about to hit, Tyranitar moved.

It was almost a blur as the green behemoth dodged between the vines and then leapt high into the air.

It crossed the distance just a breath or two, casting a shadow over the both of us from above, a wide grin on its face, before its own maw opened wide open.

And a titanic flame in the shape of a star exploded into existence down towards us.

No time to dodge!

My heart leapt into my throat.

But-

Vines retracting in the blink of an eye wrapped around me and threw me to the side, just as the Fire Blast smashed into our, or now just Servine's position with earth shaking force, kicking up an explosion.

"Mirror Coat!" I shouted, even as I landed on my side and quickly rolled to my feet.

The smoke kicked up by the explosion was obliterated as a second star of fire, twice the size of the first erupted back upwards towards the airborne Tyranitar and smashed it out of the air.

It slammed into the ground a few feet away from Servine, who was burned quite badly and had fallen to a knee, panting deeply, before slumping to her side.

'Shit, that Fire Blast was amped by our Sunny Day!' I realised, fists clenching.

Unlike Servine, Tyranitar didn't stay down at all and though it was horribly bruised, battered, bleeding and even smoking with burns from its own attack being returned at double the power, it bore a victorious smirk as it raised a fist into the air above Servine, icy blue energy shimmering over its fist.

It wound up, before unleashing its Ice Punch attack-

Only to go frozen stiff.

No pun.

Blue electricity crackled over its form.

The origin?

My gauntlet clad fingers in the shape of a gun, "You aimed at me mate," I smirked, "I wasn't gonna get involved but you aimed at me too and tagged me in, fair is fair."

My Thunder Wave paralysed it before it could finish its attack.

"Now Shock Wave!" I shouted, opening my palm. A fat crackling bolt of lightning seared from my palm and washed over Tyranitar's body a second later.

It was a rock and dark type now after all, no longer ground type and immune to electric attacks.

Tyranitar snarled in pain, legs giving out beneath it as it dropped to its knees. And I took my chance, dashing towards it.

"Mach Punch!" I ordered next and threw my fist out in a punch towards it. From my fist, leapt a massive red ethereal gauntlet near equal in size to the Tyranitar itself.

And the massive quad effective fist bowelled into it, sending it flying where it hit the ground and literally bounced three times before slamming into another cave wall.

I looked to Servine who was looking at my wide eyed while on her side, "Solarbeam, one more time girl!" I told her, before cocking my fist back, "And let's go with another Mach Punch!"

Servine rolled onto her other side without much prompting, golden light shimmering into existence within her mouth for a third Solarbeam.

At the same time I threw my fist out in a punch through the air.

Before Tyranitar could shake itself off again, it was mere prey to a gleaming golden beam of solar power and an ethereal fist of pure fighting energy.

Tyranitar roared in pain.

And then slumped down on its face.

It didn't get up again.

"Luxury Ball." I commanded, holding my palm out. The indent on my gauntlet popped open and spat a gleaming ebony luxury ball into my grasp.

And a split moment later, it was sailing through the air, clonking the unconscious Tyranitar on the head, before popping open and sucking it inside.

Once, twice, thrice it shook, the golden middle of the luxury ball glowing a searing crimson red.

Before-

Plink!

Success.

Tyranitar was captured!

# 171: Chapter 170

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

__________________________

A little while after the battle with Tyranitar, I sat leaning against one of the rigid cave walls atop the mountain.

Servine lay resting across my lap, while a gentle pink healing light suffused her body, being emitted from Blissey's hands…paws.

Bit by bit, the wounds across her body faded away, and before long, she was back to full health.

A little burning was no match for Blissey's power!

…Even if it was from a sun enhanced Tyranitar.

"Thanks girl, amazing work as always." I thanked the mvp of my squad, lifting her gleaming luxury ball up.

"Bliss." she chirped back happily as she was enveloped by red light and slurped up inside by the power of noodle light.

I could go some noodles right about now actually.

Hmm, curry for sure.

'After I'm done here.' I nodded to myself, as I reached for Servine and stroked her back, "Feeling better?" I asked the grass type serpent.

"Ser…." she huffed, rolling her eyes as if annoyed.

But didn't move from my lap, and in fact pushed herself into my stroking hand.

She was a bit tsundere this girl.

I smiled, "You did great," I praised her, "A little cocky there midway through and should have kept blasting Tyranitar while it was down, but you did good either way."

"Servine, ser." Servine smugly preened under my praise and stroking hand.

I laughed, "Besides, not that I can say anything about anyone being cocky."

She gave a little giggle, wide serpentine mouth spread in a big smile to match my own.

I stroked her gently for a few minutes, before giving her a sitrus berry as a reward for a good job and returning her to her pokeball.

I stood up, stretching out my arms, before holding my gauntlet clad fist up, "Alright Rotom, talk to me," I said, "What are we working with?"

"Citation needed." Rotom replied black.

I snorted, "Don't be a smartarse, you're not actually a robot," I said, rolling my eyes, "Tyranitar, give me an overview on its data."

Speaking of robots though. I wonder if I could enhance my Rotom Gauntlet even further if I caught a Porygon and made it go inside as well? Especially if I evolved it all the way up into a Porygon Z.

…And I kind of know where to get a Porygon actually.

Assuming they're not being sold at the Celadon Game Corner, like in the games.

"Hn. Very well," Rotom replied blandly, "Gender: Male. Ability: Sandstream. Height: 12.02. Weight: 1102 pounds. Attacks Known: Dark Pulse, Hyper Beam, Stone Edge, Iron Defense, Ice Punch, Dragon Dance, Fire Blast, Dragon Claw, Crunch, Earthquake, Giga Impact. Initial scan estimates in the range of twelve to fifteen years old. Data indicates Tyranitar has no preference for short or long range combat and is proficient in both practices."

Huh, that was more detailed than I was expecting. And it knew a crap ton of attacks, and a lot of powerful ones at that. In fact, not a single one of those attacks were low tier like Scratch or something.

…As expected of a pseudo legendary I guess?

And it sure was a big boy.

"How big is your average newly evolved Tyranitar?" I asked.

"The average Tyranitar stands in the range of 6.7 to seven feet tall. All data suggests that if this Tyranitar is trained like your other pokemon and undergoes similar growth, it will quickly grow to become double the size of average and beyond." Rotom replied.

Hmm, was that so?

Good to know this big boy, would become an even bigger boy, just like Charizard, Pidgeot and the others.

One day I want Pidgeot to be even bigger than those Pidgeot on that giant island in the Orange Islands.

That was a weird place actually now that I think about it. Only two species of pokemon lived on that island. Pidgeot and Rhydon, and they were all giant.

'Actually, it's weird just how many giant pokemon there are in the Orange Islands.' I hummed in thought. There was that island of giants I just thought of, that giant magikarp that simped for the tanned Nurse Joy, and the giant Bulbasaur stuck in that sewer because it was too big to leave, after being abandoned there by its trainer.

…Soon buddy, soon.

"Incoming call." Rotom's voice broke me from my thoughts.

"Hmm?" I looked at my gauntlet, "From who?"

"Hilda White." Rotom replied, succinctly.

I blinked.

Then smirked, "Took her long enough." I chuckled, I expected her to call like an hour after I battled Lance.

She hadn't until now.

I'd been expecting the earful she wanted to give me more talking all sappy about her on live television rapidly, not three days later.

For shame Hilda.

"Alright accept call." I ordered, twisting my hand around so the screen on the back of my fist was facing me.

"Acknowledged." Rotom said, before the screen blinked and changed.

The screen lit up to show what appeared to be a living room, with Hilda sitting down. I could only see her upper body, clad only in her white under top, no black vest in sight, nor her hat and her fluffy chocolate hair was loose and hanging, no ponytail holding it up.

"Hello, lover." I greeted her with a grin.

I was met with a deadpan look and a raised brow, "Are you sure that fits?" she snorted sarcastically, "I didn't put a ring on it after all, right?"

I resisted the urge to laugh.

"Well, I mean you didn't," I shrugged casually, "You kind of noped out to go back for your important family stuff before we could talk about whatever is between us. And you didn't exactly give me a time frame or want me to go with you."

Hilda winced, "Well when you say it like that , it sounds pretty bad."

# 172: Chapter 171

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

"Not really, it's fine, I get it," I did laugh this time, "It's not like I blame you or anything, I wouldn't have been bragging about you if I did now would I? I just said it as it is."

"Well…that's good to hear I guess," she sighed, shoulders slumping, but smiling back nonetheless, "Still, did you have to say it all live like that? You realise people will be able to figure out who I am if they check your trainer profile right? Traded pokemon are still in the records and linked, so anyone checking your profile can easily follow it to mine."

Huh, I did not know that actually.

Something to keep in mind for later.

"Eh, free advertisement," I replied cheekily, "Though, I remembered my promise, beyond talking on the camera I didn't let them interview me. My first time is reserved for you." I winked.

"As charming as always aren't you?" Hilda snorted, before shaking her head, "Honestly Jord, I was joking when I told you not to get into trouble without me, but I think you took it as a challenge. How did you even find those fossil pokemon? Never mind two Dragonite?"

"Psychic powers." I shrugged.

Time to start laying down that cover story. I can't go on forever just catching loads of rare and powerful pokemon without explanation.

Hilda blinked, "Wait…really!?" she sat up straighter, her fluffy chocolate hair bouncing, it made me remember how it felt in my hands, I wanted to run my fingers through it again, "You're a psychic?"

"Was," I 'corrected', "I wasn't born one or anything, but I came across these weirdo's a few months back before I came to Kanto. Dressed in these funky uniforms, all blue and white with stupid looking hoods. They had this psychic pokemon I'd never seen before, caged it up and had these weird devices that were stopping it from using its power. I set it free and its power that had been blocked off all exploded out of it, and a bunch of it seeped into me. I passed out, and when I came to the weirdo's and the psychic pokemon was gone, and I started getting visions for a while."

I clicked my fingers over the screen, "Learned to control them after a bit, used them to find out where a bunch of powerful and rare pokemon were," I continued, "They completely faded away a few weeks before I became a trainer, but I'd pretty much memorized everything I saw by that point. I suppose all that built up energy the pokemon let out was used up with the visions."

For a moment, Hilda just stared, "That…explains so much, here I was just thinking you were blessed by Arceus or the original dragon or something."

"I am blessed though," I replied maturely, sticking out my tongue, "Blessed by the awesome that is being me. I mean, you should know well, you got to feel my blessing all night long."

"Again, charming as ever Jord, that's what I love about you," she snorted, "Still…look…" she looked away, kind of awkwardly.

My eyebrows raised, interest forming, "Oh?"

She sighed, "…Rosa made things clear, so look," she turned back to face me on the screen, and gave me a smirk that would have looked more at home on my face, a cocky arrogant thing, It was kind of hot actually, "Leaving you hanging, wasn't fair. So for now, run wild as you please. But when I'm done with this nonsense over here and come back, I'm gonna lock your overly horny cocky arrogant ass down and put that ring on you, got it?"

Well, that….I was not expecting.

I felt the temptation to swoon.

"Or you know, I could just come back to Unova and help you, skip the middle man and you can get with the ringing right away," I smirked back at her, "Then we can break a few beds while I blow your back out."

Hilda laughed, "Such a romantic aren't you? It's a wonder I'm not swooning right now," she replied, before shaking her head, "But no, now that I know about your visions, it makes it even more important I don't pull you away from all that. Don't worry, these idiots Team Plasma they're calling themselves, they're just idiots, nowhere near as threatening as Team Rocket or something, especially with Dragonite on my side. I'll deal with these idiots quick and help Rosa get her Eevee back, and then I'll come find you and before you know it, you'll be putting me through a bed frame and leaving an imprint of me in it." she winked.

"…That's hot." was all I could really say to that.

"I know right?" Hilda's smirk widened, "Still, that's all I wanted to say, and just wanted to check up on you. So I'm gonna go, it's too tempting to just say yes to your offers to have you come here. Love ya, bye."

And then before I could reply, she screen blipped out as she hung up on me.

Huh.

…..Huh.

Huh…?

"Huh!?" I blinked.

I looked up at the sky and nodded, "….HUH!?"

I didn't hear that wrong right?

"Rotom, I didn't hear that wrong right?" I asked.

"Hear what wrong?" Rotom asked.

"That last bit, she totally said 'love ya', right?" I asked, bobbing my head like a car bobble head.

"Correct, the human female known as Hilda White did indeed say those words." Rotom confirmed.

Huh.

I licked my lips, "Alright then," I nodded. Somehow, I'm feeling pretty good right now, better than good even. Motivated. Strong. Awesome. A little bit proud too, "Fuck it we ball team! Let's go catch a fucking legendary!"

Oh Regice, buddy buddy buddy. You are not ready for the Jord train coming your way.

"Choo choo!" I pumped my fist, before jumping to my feet. With a word, a gleaming black luxury ball was in my hand and then I was sprinting forward.

And jumped straight off the mountain top, "Choo choo!" I cheered, releasing Pidgeot from the expensive ebony pokeball and landing atop his back.

# 173: Chapter 172

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

______________________

The totally manly bellow - and not at all girly shriek- I released upon realising what I did when I jumped off of the mountain, drew a roll of the eyes from Pidgeot.

But as befitting of my starter, beyond said eye roll and a barking scoff, he barely even broke stride as he caught me upon his back and ferried me through the sky over to the other side of Fennel Valley.

As it was, my good mood was not at all bothered by what I did - progress? - and not long later, I was dropped off in front of the massive mausoleum that was home to a legendary pokemon.

As I returned Pidgeot to his pokeball and looked up at the ancient building, I couldn't help but whistle. It was really impressive looking. A grand stair case led up to a hallway opening before the main door, held up by massive pillars and in between each one were huge metal braziers inscribed with images of pokemon.

'Weird though, shouldn't this place be a historic monument?' I wondered. One would think places like this would be cordoned off from the public.

…But then, this world is rife with things like this. Has it got to the point where they just don't care anymore?

That didn't seem right considering those archaeologists Ash ran into during that point who were looking into Pokemopolis.

….Actually, if I remember right, the Regi trio Brandon caught, were all from ruins dealing with Pokemopolis, also known as Pokelantis. Including that psychotic evil king that tried to possess Ash when Brandon caught Regirock.

Actually, that ruin wasn't far from here actually. It wasn't far off from Pewter City.

And wait, the ancient temple ruins with the sealed giant Alakazam, Gengar and Jigglypuff aren't far off from Pallet town.

Hmm.

"Rotom, display a map of Kanto." I said, holding up gauntlet up.

"Acknowledged." Rotom replied, the screen on the back of my fist lighting up and displaying the map of the region.

'Right, i'm right here.' I thought, placing my finger over Fennel Valley on the map. Then, winding my finger up in a circle shape to just before Pewter, my finger stopped on where a small village was, home to a ruin just a little ways away from Pewter.

And then continuing on I wound it further down still in the circle shape, keeping it a perfect circle shape…and directly across from Fennel Valley with Viridian City between them, was another area home to a ruin.

'If Regice is here and Regirock is at the one near Pewter City, then this ruin I'm pointing to, must be where Registeel is.' I cupped my chin in thought.

Then, this obviously dictates they were all set up by the same civilization. And considering the ancient evil kings plan was to use un-named legendary pokemon to rule the world…Did that mean his pokemon squad was: Regice, Regirock, Registeel, the giant Alakazam, the giant Gengar and the giant Jigglypuff?

No wonder Ho-oh had to personally get involved and smite his ass.

Logic would probably dictate that somewhere in that circle between Pallet, Virdian and Pewter, there would be three other ruins. One for Regieleki, one for Regidrago and one final one for a Regigigas.

Assuming the guy found them as well.

Maybe a pentagram within the circle?

'Well, no matter.' I shrugged it off for now. I already know where to find a Regieleki and Regidrago, together in the same ruin in Galar.

And I know where to find a Regigigas in Sinnoh.

…Two of them actually.

Not that I plan on going anywhere near a Regigigas for the foreseeable future.

…The five other Regi's though, might not be a bad idea. While I do plan to calm down a lot on how I go about things.

Legendary pokemon that are easy to find and won't cause any natural disasters if I capture are another thing entirely.

Especially since, while I'd prefer to leave everything up to Ash, he's the chosen one after all, if things do go fubar…being able to call upon the Regi's would be a massive boon.

'Well, we'll see after I deal with Regice.' I thought. Regidrago and Regieleki are one thing, they have a shit ton of fire power, but are glass cannons. The three Regi's Brandon caught though? Were not only powerhouses, but absolute tanky units.

I made my way up the grand staircase into the ancient ruin, and beyond the light streaming in from the open doorway, it was pitch black within.

A musty dank scent assaulted my nostrils. It reminded me of the cavern where my fossil pokemon were, smelling like the underground.

I wonder if this is what miners smelled when they went deep underground to mine coal and such?

The deeper I walked, the darker it got, until I could barely see a few feet in front of me, "Rotom, give me some light." I said, holding my hand up.

A second later, the circular indent within the palm of my gauntlet, lit up with a bright illuminating light, like that of a torch.

'That's better.' I thought and continued on my way, deeper into the ruin. With the light from my gauntlet, I got a much better look at things.

As I went deeper, I passed by small archways leading into empty stone rooms, filled with nothing but dust, rocks…and even a few small skeletons that were probably long dead pokemon.

Whatever were in these rooms before, had long since been picked clean over the centuries.

Finally, I came upon an absolutely massive archway that lead into a huge open cavernous room.

Massive pillars lined the walls, leading up to a grand staircase at the far end of the room. Where at the very top, sat a huge throne, and atop it, a gargantuan statue of a man dressed in robes, holding a huge stone sceptre and a giant crown atop his head.

"So this was the evil king huh?" I hummed, shining my light upon it, "God he was one ugly fuck huh?" I snorted.

He had a thick heavy brow, pronounced bum chin. A massive, fat flat nose. Massive buggy looking eyes. And his face as a whole, was wide and fat, his cheeks pronounced with blubber.

# 174: Chapter 173

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________________________

…God he looked like a stereotypical evil king too.

"Now that's just sad." I shook my head.

Weirdly, there was a small empty throne placed right in front of the statue on its throne.

Did that mean he sat on the little throne while that giant stone version of him loomed over everything on its own throne?

What an ego this dude had, and that's coming from me of all people!

I eyed the throne thoughtfully, "…It's made of stone, but it looks really well done." I hummed, looking it over.

Plus those jewels.

It was wide, with a high back arch. The back rest of it delicately carved with a roaring Charizard head inscribed within the stone. And there was a head sized ruby sitting at the very top of the back arch, with two fist sized ones a the front of the arm rests.

I've no idea why they weren't stolen either though….

Nor did I care.

A beat.

"Hey Rotom, could you store that throne within your storage space?" I asked, "Just…for reference."

Look, that guy didn't deserve a throne.

And maybe I didn't either. But like, finders keepers losers weepers. And I'm a better person than him.

Right?

Okay look, I just like the idea of being able to summon a grand throne and sitting atop it at a moments notice. It was a power move.

Also badass!

And it had a Charizard on it!

Absolute cunt the evil king might have been, and ugly as sin. But, he had decent taste.

"I can yes." Rotom replied, and didn't judge.

He was an electrical ghost spaz after all, he wouldn't give a lick about an ancient ruin like this.

"Alright…cool," I nodded, before walking over and placing my hand on the throne, "Store this throne please buddy."

"Acknowledged." Rotom replied.

And a moment later, the throne was slurped up inside the gauntlet like a big wet noodle.

With that taken care of, I looked around, "Regice should be behind some kind of wall puzzle." I said aloud, holding my hand out and shining the light from my gauntlet around the room.

A moment later, I found it. On the far side of the room.

"There you are." I grinned and made my way over, looking upon it. It was almost akin to a jigsaw puzzle, made up of twelve stone slabs. Three in a row with four rows in total.

They weren't images, but rather each one was inscribed with some sort of language I had no experience with at all.

Alas, I am not a geniur nor an intelligent well learned pokemon archaeologist like Brandon.

What I am though, is a meat head.

And a bit of a thug.

That was why, I brought out Golem and pointed at the wall, "Smash through that wall will you buddy?" I smirked, before ordering, "Brick Break."

"Gol!" Golem grinned back, before saluting me-

And then he turned and leapt towards, one arm lighting up with bright power, before he brought it down upon the wall.

The wall shattered like paper mache from the force of the blow, debris going flying as Golem carved straight through the puzzle and wall in one blow.

Stepping in with Golem at my side, I shined my light upon the room, and my smirk widened, "Not hard at all to find, were you?" I said.

Because there it was. A huge humanoid shaped beast, made of shining blue crystalline ice, standing stock still and sentry at the top of a huge set of stairs, while behind it lay a huge chest of some form.

Treasure maybe?

Did the evil king leave Regice here to guard his treasure? What a moron, Regice was the real treasure!

A vibrating humming nose came from Regice a second later, dot yellow eyes lighting up brightly-

"Flamethrower!" I commanded Golem quickly.

And not even hesitating, Golem opened his mouth and unleashed a mighty bevy of flame that converged over the legendary ice type.

"Get up there with Brick Break, get in close!" I continued before Golem even finished using his Flamethrower attack.

Hearing my words, Golem began to run forward, not letting up on his flamethrower attack at all-

Before cutting it off at the base of stairs as he leapt into the air, arm once again glowing with the power of Brick Break.

The flames died away to reveal Regice no worse for wear, standing in the same spot. And as Golem came down like a destroying meteor to drive his Brick Break attack into the top of its head, Regice simply lifted one arm and caught the blow, easily stopping it and holding Golem aloft.

It began to glow, ice particles forming around it, forming into a truly massive orb of crackling freezing energy in front of it. The sheer size and speed it was formed at, was incredible.

But-

"Explosion!" I shouted.

I saw Golem smirk as his body immediately began to glow bright white, and then…

*Crackaboom*!

The entire mausoleum shook like an earthquake was rumbling through the mountainside it was atop, the world going white and all sound being drowned out as Golem detonated himself.

When the world stopped shaking and returned to normal, it was to reveal the entire staircase had been blown away, alongside the treasure chest.

It was lucky I ducked behind the wall made at the 'entrance' of the room or I'd have gotten caught up in it as well.

All over the floor I saw sparkling golden coins, jewel encrusted rings, necklaces and more. Massive gems of varying colours spread all around.

Golem, having fallen unconscious was sizzling from the explosion he unleashed, and Regice?

Regice was covered in burn marks and body smoking, smashed halfway into the wall. I wasted no time, fingers forming into a pistol shape, "Thunder Wave!" I shouted, and a thin crackling bolt of blue leapt from my fingers and washed over Regice, paralysing it.

Electricity crackled over its form as its arms that were reaching up to pull itself out of the wall, slowed noticeably.

And I wasted no time, recalling Golem and getting the pokeball of one of my newest captures, and the one inspired by Lance, "Let's go Exeggutor!" I released my grass and psychic type.

I made sure to let him out of his pokeball far ahead of me, so he was very close to Regice.

Practically point blank, "Middle Sleep Powder, left Poison Powder, right Leech Seed!" I quickly ordered.

"Exeg!"

New to the squad or not, Exeggutor was quick on the ball. Two of his heads opened their mouths, and twin clouds of spores erupted, one a venomous purple, the other a sparkling blue.

While the other? Spat out a simple seed.

At point blank, Regice who had just pulled out of the wall had no chance to dodge, and it slumped over asleep a moment later as the sleep powder took effect.

Then its body glowed purple with poison.

And the seed that hit its body, burst apart forming into huge vines that spread all over its body, lighting it up with crimson energy just like that of an Absorb attack as its health began to get drained.

"Now, Explode!" I ordered again, ducking out of the way once more behind the wall at the entrance.

And once more, the world turned white, the sound was drowned out and the mausoleum shook as Exeggutor detonated himself at point blank range, just like Golem before him.

When it was safe, I peeked back in to find Exeggutor laying on his side, out cold like Golem was before, body sizzling.

And laying beside it was Regice. Its huge crystalline body was littered in injuries and burn marks, and the purple glow of poison and crackling red energy being sucked out of it by Leech Seed hadn't stopped at all.

Yeah, it definitely wasn't down yet.

And I'm not giving it a chance to recover. Because I know it can. After all, I know for a fact this Regice knows Rest.

If I give it the chance to, this thing will outlast every pokemon I have.

'Time to finish it off with the big guns.' I thought, recalling Exeggutor and transferring his shining expensive ebony black luxury ball for another, "Let's go, Charizard."

In a burst of bright light, the winged flame tailed behemoth thundered into existence beside me, a roar of challenge erupting from his throat.

I cocked my fist back over my shoulder, winding it up to put all my power into it, "Blast Burn Charizard," I commanded, followed by, "Mach Punch!"

And then both of our fists glowed, and together, we unleashed a pair of devastating punches.

From my fist, leapt a massive ethereal crimson red gauntlet that tore through the air towards Regice, at the same time Charizard smashed his fist through the floor of the mausoleum and the ground rent and split apart, searing orange heat tearing it apart.

The huge fist slammed into Regice, smashing into its body with boulder cratering force.

And then the ground erupted beneath it, in a gigantic explosion of heat and energy that was so powerful, it literally blew a hole straight up through the roof hundreds of feet up.

"Luxury ball me." I said aloud over the loud ringing in my ears from the Blast Burn attack from Charizard.

A shiny black luxury ball was deposited in my palm, and I waited for the debris from Charizard's attack to clear.

And even then, I didn't wait until it did totally.

The second the smoke and debris cleared away enough for me to catch sight of Regice's body, I threw the ball.

The luxury ball bounced off its form, before cracking open, red light escaping it and enveloping Regice's body, before sucking it inside.

It snapped shut, golden middle glowing bright red as the ball hit the ground and began to roll back and forth.

Once!

I lifted my hand, palm wide open, prepared to unleash another attack with Rotom Gauntlet.

Twice!

"Get ready." I ordered Charizard at my side, tensing up.

.

..

…

….

…..

Three times!

….Ping!

I breathed a sigh of relief, shoulders relaxing and arm dropping, "…Well damn…" I uttered, staring blankly at the luxury ball laying innocently within the absolutely ruined room, covered in craters and sizzling burn marks.

Not to mention all the treasure scattered about everything.

"I just caught a legendary." I blinked and noted blankly.

….Huh.
