# 175: Chapter 174

So...been a while huh? To be honest, I completely forgot I had a webnovel. I was reminded by someone of the fact I did yesterday, or was it the day before? Well, either way. I finally remembered to upload here again. But well, good news at least for you guys I suppose, I've updated a ton for my main stories.

____________________

"Regice. Gender: None. Ability: Ice Body. Height: 15'04. Weight: 2108 pounds. Attacks Known: Explosion, Icy Wind, Charge Beam, Ice Beam, Blizzard, Hammer Arm, Focus Punch, Hail, Hyper Beam, Rest, Lock On, Zap Cannon, Earthquake, Ancient Power. Data indicates Regice has no preference for short or long range combat and is proficient in both practices. Though, data does indicate Regice excels in battles of attrition."

Well, that was something already. I stared at Rotom Gauntlet whom had just relayed the abilities of my new Regice to me. I wet my lips, cocking my head to the side and nodding.

Yep, it was super lucky I jumped Regice. Beyond that whole slew of powerful attacks, if it got Hail up, it would recover passively. And then it would have rest as well. And with how tanky it was….

And Lock on was scary. As soon as it used it, its attacks would follow the target, even if they dodged like heat seeker missiles.

I shuddered at the thought of that with it using Hyper Beam. Or worse, explosion.

"Tor…!" a happy groan of relief drew my attention from my gauntlet, I looked over towards the pokemon I currently had out. We were back outside the ancient building where I caught Regice, I sat atop the stairs, while to my side Charizard rested happily and proudly, being the one to finish off Regice and all.

At the bottom of the stairs, both Exeggutor and Golem sat, Blissey between them both as she released the ethereal pink energy of Heal Pulse over their bodies.

They were already more or less, full recovered. A testament to their endurance and durability, and of course Blissey's highly potent Heal Pulse. She was very experienced with it.

She used it every day, dozens of times, multiple times a day after all. And ever since she evolved, not only had her stamina increased massively, but so too had the potency of her heal pulse.

"Doing alright lads?" I called over.

They looked over, Exeggutor gave blinks of six eyes, before just happily chanting his name, wiggling his head back and forth. He was just a happy, chill guy I suppose.

"Golem!" Golem on the other hand, flashed me a full thumbs up and beamed, eye smiles and all, big wide grin on his face.

He had been very happy ever since he woke up when I let him back out of his ball to get healed. He had taken me sending him back to the Pokemon Centre pretty hard after he lost to Surge's Alolan Golem, and threw himself into training. And it definitely showed. But mostly, he was just very proud of himself for being the one to launch the opening salvo on a legendary pokemon, and debatably, dealt the most damage.

Regice might have blocked that Brick Break, but it still took the hit. Flamethrower, Brick Break, then a point blank explosion?

He did very well.

I flashed a thumbs up back, before standing up, stretching my arms out and hopping down the stairs.

"Alright then, if you guys are good, I'll leave you out for now just in case," I said, "To be honest, I'm not really expecting any problems from Regice, Tyranitar might be another story."

"Bliss, bliss." Blissey waved her stubby hands airily, waving it off as not a problem, while Exeggutor just gave me a trio of grins.

Golem stood, nodding eagerly, while Charizard gave a rumble as he stood up from where he was laying on the stairs and stomped his way down the steps with a single nod, coming to a stop beside me and crossing his arms.

Menacingly.

Charizard was a big guy after all. No less in stature than Tyranitar, though both were a bit dwarfed by Regice.

Alright then.

In that case, I'd start with whom should be the easier of the two. I grinned, "Rotom, Regice's ball please." I said.

"Roger." Rotom replied, and a moment later the gleaming luxury ball containing my newest capture and first legendary pokemon popped into my palm.

Hmm, actually, now that I think about it. People are probably going wild again after seeing my newest addition to my trainer page.

…I think I'll not contact anyone like say, Lance or Nurse Joy unless they call me first.

Yep yep, that sounds like a plan.

I nodded at my own genies before holding the ball up, "Come on out, Regice." I said, before releasing the legendary beast within.

…Actually, that is three legendary dogs nickname right?

Oh well.

In a burst of bright silvery light, Regice's towering robust icy blue form appeared in front of us. It was covered in wounds, and there was a sickly purple glow seeping off its form from where Exeggutor poisoned it earlier.

Despite that, it stood tall.

Silent.

…Completely silent. And just stared at me, my pokemon around me tense and ready to jump into action at any moment.

The sad thing was though, that wouldn't happen at all. Because Regice? And all the Regi's actually, were more like automatons given life and their purpose, was to follow orders.

And now that I had captured it, it would just obey me and any order I gave.

I flicked my tongue over my teeth at the thought, it rankled me. The Regi as a whole, basically didn't have free will. Or at least had been used as such for so long, such a thing was just instinct to them now.

"Hey Regice, I'm Jord, and I'm your new trainer," I introduced myself, stepping forward and looking up into its six yellow dot eyes, "Nice to meet you."

It didn't move at all, but its yellow dot eyes did light up slightly, "Dododon." a humming, vibrating 'don' sound escaped it,

And that was that.

I won't lie, I'm actually a little disappointed.

I don't like that.

I don't like it at all. It was amazing to see how my pokemon expressed themselves, all their little ticks and traits.

Regice was just blank.

…Can I do something about that?

I can try I suppose.

I nodded, before turning to Blissey, "Hey girl, can you heal Regice with Aromatherapy and Heal Pulse?" I asked her.

Blissey who I noticed had an almost dismayed look upon her face at the reaction of Regice, or lack thereof, nodded and stepped forward, a determined look upon her face, "Bliss!"

It didn't take long for Blissey to completely heal Regice back to full health, and before long, his...its body was a bright shining gleaming icy blue once more, no wounds or status effects hindering it at all.

While she healed it, I tried to think of a way to help it, but, as expected, nothing came to mind in only a few minutes.

…There would no simple solution to dealing with a hundreds of years old problem. Though, I suppose at least I can start by letting it out of its ball, and ordering it to do what it wanted, or what came to mind? Follow any impulses it had?

It would be a start at least.

In that case-

With Regice healed, I returned all of my pokemon to their luxury balls, except Regice itself.

"Mind lending me a hand mate?" I asked the legendary ice type, the best place, would be asking, not ordering, and allowing for refusal, "You don't need to if you don't want to. Just that I captured a Tyranitar just before you, and need to meet him face to face outside of battle and settle things. I'd just like your help in case he attacks me."

For a few moments, Regice just stared at me blankly, and I began to get a little dismayed at the thought I really would need to order everything.

But then, slowly Regice inclined its huge ice pillar like head forward ever so slightly, "Dododon."

Well, alright then.

"Thanks mate." I pat it on its bulky icy arm, pausing as I noticed just how frigid its body felt. Odd, for how cold it was, I didn't feel any of that until I touched it. Some kind of genetic body field kind of thing that kept its body temperature locked to it, to conserve any or something, maybe?

Putting that out of mind, I had Rotom spit out Tyranitar's shiny new ball into my palm, and then, I pressed the button in the middle and released the pseudo legendary contained within.

Tyranitar appeared before me, light dying down immediately to show off his behemothic, battered form.

He blinked sluggishly, before gaining focus as he saw me, his eyes narrowing at me,, "Rrrrrrr…" he gave a grumbling roar, before wincing.

And then huffing.

And giving me an expectant look.

Well, so far so good. He hadn't launched himself at me like Dragonite did at least. I wasn't looking forward to laying out another pseudo legendary on his ass.

"Bet you won't be underestimating small opponents much anymore huh?" I couldn't help but needle him just a little bit though.

Tyranitar's eye twitched, claws flexing-

Before he breathed out fully through his nose, spreading his arms wide and way higher in height than Servine was, and then pointing at me, and smirking with a shrug.

"It was technically three on one yes," I agreed, "You're pretty strong. What if I told you though, that Servine was one of my newest pokemon and I've barely trained her?"

Tyranitar reared back as if struck.

"Yup," I grinned, "I've got far stronger pokemon than her…like this guy." I jabbed my finger over my shoulder at Regice.

Looming menacingly.

Tyrantar blinked, as if just realising Regice was actually there, before goggling and stepping back warily.

Hmm, yeah he was probably well aware of Regice huh?

"As you can see, I caught him too, it wasn't all that hard despite how incredibly strong Regice is," I boasted lightly, playing myself up, "How would you like me to make you just as strong as Regice. No, in fact, how would you like to become even stronger than he is now?"

I very much specified the 'now', because I for sure plan on Regice becoming stronger and stronger.

Tyranitar eyed Regice for a few moments, before turning to look at me sceptically.

"I mean, I caught Regice didn't I?" I shrugged in return. I didn't bother going into the fact that one on one, Regice would cream any pokemon I have right now, even Pidgeot or Altaria would lose. I can only estimate right now, but I'd say Regice while not on the level of Lance's Dragonite he started off our battle with, not that far off it either.

If I said Lance's weakest Dragonite was level two hundred and fifty, then Regice was level two hundred.

And Pidgeot would be around level ninety.

Of course levels aren't really a thing, but those are the estimates I would give them if they were.

Tyranitar eyed me dubiously for a few moments, before simply rolling his eyes and shrugging his shoulders and accepting.

"You're a suspicious guy huh? I respect that." I nodded, "Then since you're fine with this, pop a squat will you? I'll get those injuries of yours dealt with."

Tyranitar shrugged…before flopping down on his side in a lazy sprawl, the ground shaking as he did from his sheer weight, and propping his head up on one arm and yawning.

Well, okay then.

He was a lot more chill than my Dragonite at least.

With that said, I turned to Regice and pat his arm thankfully, "Cheers a bunch for your help big guy," I said, "It turned out you didn't need to do anything beyond stand there and look tough, but thank a bunch anyway."

Regice cocked its icy pillar head to the side as if examining me, before silently returning to its standing position and saying nothing or doing anything at all beyond that.

I withheld a sigh.

And to think, all the Regi's are going to be like this.

# 176: Chapter 175

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

__________________

…Should I even catch them? Sure, their power would be incredible to add to my repertoire of pokemon, and would be a big big help in dealing with world ending shenanigans when, not if, they happen.

But…am I equipped and able enough to do something for them all? I'm struggling to picture even handling Regice and trying to breathe some life into it and give it meaning beyond orders.

Would I be better off just leaving them where they are?

But then…that might run the risk of others finding and capturing them. This world, isn't just the anime alone after all. I've noticed elements from the games and even the manga. I can't rely on my anime knowledge alone, that they'd still just be there after a time.

I mean, Brandon for instance is on the hunt for the Regi's specifically. He's an alright guy and all I suppose, but he only wants them because he wants to collect them as a history nut.

…Yeah, ironic and a bit hypocritical coming from me. But again, the only hypocrite I like is myself. And well, I'm trying to turn over a new leaf at least.

But, I'm not really sure if Brandon will be trying to give them the will to live beyond orders like I want to do with Regice.

…But could I handle doing it for five Regi's?

Like, I like myself a whole lot, and think I'm pretty awesome. But that might be beyond even my abilities.

Definitely something I'll need to think on before I leave Fennel Valley.

I sighed, sitting down cross legged in front of Regice…before remembering I needed to bring out Blissey to heal Tyranitar.

__________________

With how tough Tyranitar was, both in general strength and well…species, it wasn't long before he was healed back up into tip top shape by Blissey.

As it was, I returned both the pseudo legendary and my legendary healing angel - cuz she totally was - to their balls, and rested atop the stairs of the ancient building, lazily looking up into the clear blue skies above.

Standing at the base of the stairs, Regice was an ever watchful sentinel. I needed to think.

And he was decent company.

Didn't say much though.

…Or anything beyond 'dododon' when I asked something….when he deigned it a good enough thing to reply to, or needing a reply I suppose.

An hour had passed now since Tyranitar was healed, and in that time, my mind had been wandering, planning, questioning, backtracking, re-planning, procrastinating…almost falling asleep.

My mind had moved in circles, processing the situation around Regice and the other Regi's, and debating not only the pro's and cons of going after them and if I should.

But could I handle them?

And the answer I came to?

I don't know.

But…at the very least I can try. And, I'll have plenty of time to do it. I do have plans to become immortal after all.

Or well, un-aging at least. Eternal youth? A store of life force that simulates such a thing?

Something along those lines.

Though, I suppose me going after them does depend on one thing. I turned my head and looked at the towering form of Regice at the bottom of the stares, who just stood there silently and staring at me, not having budged an inch.

It was weird…I'd found through trial and error so far, that Regice was 'guarding' me. That was basically, the Regi's purpose, guards who obeyed orders. And since I caught it, Regice's new self assigned purpose, was guarding me and obeying my orders.

"Hey, Regice." I spoke up, calling over to the massive ice type.

Regice remained silent, not moving at all, but its yellow eyes did light up ever so slightly, something I'd noticed it did to show it was paying attention.

"I'm thinking of going after your brethren," I replied, "Not Regigas, but I know where Regirock is, and the general area of Registeel. And I know where a Regielki and Regidrago are. Do you have any problems with that? Any reason why I shouldn't? If you don't want me to, I won't."

In the end, after everything, I delegated.

I left it all up to Regice.

Its eyes light up a little brighter, and then…in the most movement I'd seen of the legendary ice sentinel beyond the the battle where we jumped it silly, it lifted is massive blocky ice arms-

And shrugged, "Dododon."

Regice, either did not understand the question of why I was asking for permission for such a thing, or just didn't care.

Alright then, let me try a different approach then.

"Would you be fine battling them and helping me catch them?" I asked, "You don't need to if you don't want to." I stressed.

I stood up and hopped down the stairs to stand in front of him, looking up at his towering form that towered over me by a near factor of three, "But like you, they're very strong, and I only managed to beat you by getting the drop on you, and if we gave you time, you'd have recovered and outlasted us. So would you help me capture your friends as well?"

For a moment, Regice was still and silent, before its eyes lit up and it inclined its massive ice pillar head in a single nod, "Re…gice, Regigigice."

I blinked, before my eyes flew wide open at the exclamation of its name, like a normal pokemon! Its voice was vibrating and buzzing like someone humming with their teeth clicking together.

But that was definitely its name!

My jaw dropped and a smile spread across my face, well alright then!

"Rotom, can you translate?" I asked, looking to my gauntlet eagerly. I'd tried to get him to translate the 'Dododon's, but not even Rotom was capable of that. They apparently weren't a pokemon language that could be spoken by anything that wasn't another Regi.

"I can, not much to translate though," Rotom replied, "Regice simply said, that it has no issue with you capturing its brethren and has no issues in facing them in battle. Its tone is blank, with no emotion I might add. Though I detect, a hint of approval of some form? I cannot be sure though. Respect maybe? If nothing else I can easily infer the ancient king Regice once served, was a…what is the word you like to use? Ah yes, a right twat. So even for an emotionless thing like this, you are a far step up."

Oh…I got a little excited over nothing then huh? I thought there was more to it.

But well, it was definitely a start. Even a tiny hint of approval, was better than absolute nothing. And it gave me a little bit of hope, that for sure there was indeed emotion in that big icy body.

I grinned happily at Regice and pat it on his front, ignoring the biting cold that seeped into my fingers, "Thank you Regice," I told it truthfully, "And I didn't get the chance to say this yet, but welcome to the family, let's get along alright mate?"

"Dododon." Regice replied.

…Well, can't all be winners, "We'll get there mate, we'll get there." my patting turned consoling.

I held my hand out and had Rotom spit out Regice's luxury ball, "Alright mate, you take a test right now and relax inside your ball alright? I made sure to pay a lot of extra to make sure you and all my other pokemon could live in comfort when you're inside your balls." I smiled at it, before returning him to his ball.

"Well, that happened." I sighed, storing Regice's luxury ball away in my gauntlet.

You know, I thought when caught my first legendary, it would be much more hype. And well, don't get me wrong, I'm elated, excited even and very happy.

I just never imagined I'd be….what-

A therapist?

Basically yeah, the closest thing I could liken to, was becoming Regice's therapist to help him regain his emotions and live a good fulfilling life. And would be doing so with all the other Regi's.

Was this karma maybe?

If it is, it was totally deserved.

I did collect pokemon like I was the next gen Lawrence the third. I let my ego and arrogance get the better of me.

I forgot what pokemon, not the creatures in this world, but the entirety of this universe actually meant to me.

Though you'd think after dealing with Goh I'd have earned a little bit more of a better karma scale.

Maybe Goh was so shit, karma itself didn't want to even acknowledge he existed? In which case, fair enough karma.

Shaking my head, I put that out of mind for the moment. It was about time to get a move on.

My time table just got a lot busier for the day. I was planning on visiting Misty to give my female Wartortle to her, and give some stones to her, Ash and Brock, maybe chill with them a little bit and such, before going after Moltres and then leaving Kanto behind.

But now, I'm on the Regi hunt.

I wonder if I could even do it all today? Well, not the two in Galar, but Regirock and Registeel. I know where Regirock is, but Registeel I only know the vague area.

Perhaps Regice would know?

But yeah, even with Pidgeot's speed while flying, could I get from here to Pewter, catch Regirock, then over towards indigo from Viridian and find the ruin Registeel was in and catch it?

It would be tight for sure.

Hmm, actually, that gives me an idea.

I held out my hand and had Rotom deposit Exeggutor's luxury ball into my palm, and then in a burst of light I let the massive grass and psychic type out.

I almost winced in guilt when I saw that two of Exeggutor's heads were fast asleep, and the only one awake, gave me a sleepy, "Tor…!" in greeting.

He'd put some real work in earlier against Regice, however brief the battle had been, he'd expended a lot of energy.

"Tired buddy, sorry about bringing you out," I apologized, rubbing his side, feeling the bark-like quality of his body, "I was wondering, would you be able to teleport us back to Viridian City? In the battlefield outside the pokemon centre we did some training in?"

While training him to learn a bunch of attacks over the last few days, we'd paid multiple visits to said battle field.

Exeggutor gave a sleepy yawn, before nodding, "Exeg…u….tor." he replied, before his body began to glow.

The glow spread from him to me in the blink of an eye from where I was touching him, and a feeling of weightlessness spread through my body for a split moment.

And suddenly, I wasn't in Fennel Valley anymore, but the battlefield outside the Viridian City Pokemon Centre.

I blinked…and just stared.

That was my first time teleporting.

'So smooth.' I almost goggled, and would have for a little longer if Exeggutor didn't give another sleepy rumble at my side.

Quickly thanking him after shaking myself out of my stupor, I returned Exeggutor to his luxury ball, before storing it away in the gauntlet.

"It's too bad you can't learn teleport, huh Rotom?" I mused almost blankly, it was just so sudden to be here, I was still a little dazed to be honest, "I wouldn't need to bother Exeggutor and it would be incredible to use in a fight."

"But you'd have no problem bothering me!?" Rotom sounded like he was pouting.

"After the amount of times you zipped around like a lunatic over the last few days before that gauntlet arrived? Yes," I snorted, "But beyond that, doesn't that gauntlet have some super battery and energy generator thing that could power an entire building easily? Your own energy is amped by it now right?"

"Pfft, it's the principle of the matter, but yes, so what?" Rotom was definitely pouting now, somehow, "Either way, I am utterly incapable of learning teleport. But I do have a solution if you want to hear~" his voice became very sly all of a sudden, like a sleazy care salesman.

"Oh?" I raised a brow.

"Perhaps if you got those devilishly skilled and strong hands of yours on a Porygon, we could make it work, they can learn teleport according to my database," Rotom continued, all the while complimenting me suddenly. It made me blink actually, why would he be so gung ho for it? "There's plenty of space in this gauntlet for a porygon, and they can learn many many attacks I cannot. And of course, it would also boost the strength of the gauntlet, and my energy stores and processing power even further!"

Ah, he just wanted to boost his own power and other abilities he now had access to as the Rotom Gauntlet.

Wannabe sly little thing wasn't he?

I almost laughed at him trying to butter me up. 'Little wanker.' I thought fondly.

"I don't really have a problem with that, I just only know where two are, and they're already owned by someone else, so unless I can convince them to give me one, we're shit out of luck," I replied with a shrug, "Well, I suppose they might be getting sold at the Celadon Game Corner?"

They did in the games at least.

"I will check right away!" Rotom chirped back.

"You do that mate," I snorted, "First though, gimme Fearow's ball eh?" I said. May as well give Pidgeot a little rest as well, "No wait, in fact, give me Aerodactyl's."

I'd been leaving him to his own devices with Altaria the past few days, but I should take some time to bully check up on how he was doing. Maybe have a little heart to heart, give some advice where it was due and such things?

# 177: Chapter 176

___________________

When he was let out of his luxury ball, Aerodactyl stretched out his wings, before stiffening up and looking around, head whipping back and forth wildly, eyes scanning the area warily.

When he saw only me, Aerodactyl let loose a deep sigh of relief, tension all but evaporating from his massive rocky form.

I smirked, "Expecting someone a lot fluffier?" I teased.

Aerodactyl sniffed and looked away, embarrassedly.

He did not like Altaria at all.

Not that it stopped them from getting on the hanky panky train multiple times a day, it was just that Altaria was the dominant one when it came to that from what I had….overheard.

They were quite loud.

And it seemed Altaria amazon dommy mommy'd him.

"You should train harder like Dragonite and the others then," I walked over and pat his massive jaw, "Once you're stronger she won't be able to toss you around as she pleases."

That wasn't to say Aerodactyl did not train, but his ingrained arrogance, made it so he put much less effort in than Dragonite and others. And it was beginning to show.

After all, originally when I caught him, Aerodactyl was decently stronger than Dragonite, but now Dragonite had begun to pull ahead. Not that Aerodactyl noticed.

He was too busy getting reverse mating pressed into submission by Altaria.

"…Aero…" Aerodactly's wings slumped in defeat, and he simply nodded.

Damn, no push back at all, Altaria had well and truly gotten to him. Did this count as pussy whipped?

Shaking my head, I let out the extra saddle within Rotom's storage space, and went about securing it to his body, making him give me a curious look, "I decided I'd try flying on your back for a change," I explained, as I fastened a large sturdy buckle, "That alright with you?"

Aerodactyl merely shrugged his wings, uncaring.

Eeesh.

Before, he definitely would have thrown a hissy fit. My plan for Altaria to mellow him out and soften that gaping pride of his had worked in one way at least.

It wasn't long later, that I was once again soaring through the clouds, this time upon Aerodactyl's back.

He had a little trouble at first, adjusting to flying with the saddle and me atop it. But, thanks to his sheer size, it wasn't any real trouble for him.

"Good work, you adjusted quick." I pat his tough grey back hide in praise.

"Ro!" Aerodactyl smugly barked, side eyeing me.

Out and about with no Altaria in sight, it didn't take him long to get some peppy arrogance back to him.

"Text message received." Rotom suddenly spoke up.

"Hmm?" I raised a brow, "From who? Gary?" he was the one who I texted the most, though he'd been suspiciously silent since just after the battle with Lance.

I'm assuming I lit a fire under his ass and he was training hard…or spending a shit ton of money on more TM's.

'Rich bastard.' I thought fondly.

"Unknown number." Rotom replied simply.

Well alright then. Odd, but it it might be Professor Oak or something, they could probably get his number quite easily.

Honestly, I had been expecting a call from Nurse Joy or Lance or something since I caught Tyranitar and Regice, but nothing yet.

Odd that.

Lifting the gauntlet, I looked at the screen on the back of my fist, "Bring up the text message." I said, and a moment later, the screen switched to it.

I blinked as I found myself staring at an image. An image of a pair of fat juicy pillowy round ass cheeks, black lacy panties stretched taught. A slender back clad in a white tank top, fluffy voluminous brown hair held in a simple messy ponytail as the owner of said lovely ass stretched her arms over her head.

A familiar pair of juicy ass cheeks.

A pair I'd buried my face between not that long ago after all, in Vermillion City.

'Check it out, a full moon in the morning!' was written as as caption under the image, with a love heart emoji and a smoochie kissie face emoji as well.

It was a very nice image-

But…

'Who the hell is this, and how did you get this picture?' I replied to the text. I had a decent idea of who it was, but I could be wrong.

And if I was, and this was some pervy cunt stalking Hilda and taunting me like this then fuck it. I'm going straight to Unova and killing whatever cunt it is.

'I'll give you one guess, and a hint to go with it!' was their reply only ten seconds later, followed by an image.

My eyebrows raised as a familiar blue and white long sleeved shirt came into view, massive tits even larger than Hilda's own stretching it out, and going by the indents in the middle of them…she wasn't wearing a bra.

'So that pink thing she wears underneath it isn't a sports bra?' I thought idly, taking in her monumental…assets.

Rosa really had it going on.

This I was not expecting.

For a few moments, I honestly wasn't sure what to think, never mind respond. 'What brought this on?' I texted back, 'Not that I'm complaining, that's two images I'll happily keep, you and Hilda are looking good as always.'

'Heh!'

Was all she sent back, but even as simple as it was, and simply in text form, it radiated smugness.

She did have that kind of energy about her I remember. Cocky and spunky, and with little shame form what I remembered of the time I talked to her on the phone when I sent that Dragonite to Hilda.

'I'm shooting my shot. You said you're still open to offers live remember?'

I raised a brow. Rosa was firing into me? But then, why did she send a picture of Hilda first?

Like, she was gorgeous, hot beyond belief. Which granted was obvious, she was very similar in looks to Hilda. Only, more athletic, and with even bigger tits despite being about a half a year younger than Hilda from what I knew of her.

'Was my dick that impressive?' I shot back.

'Yes,' she replied simply, 'That thing was massive. So how about it? Wanna take a shot with the superior cousin?'

Damn, she was blunt as hell.

Ironic coming from me.

…Yes, yes I wanted a shot at that. But…my mind drifted back to only a few hours ago, when Hilda called me.

She told me to go wild as I pleased with girls, but the next time she saw me, I was getting locked down.

Considering Hilda was specifically back in Unova to help Rosa, well that route was closed off wasn't it?

Because, the simple fact of the matter was, I wanted to lock in with Hilda. Because, beyond how gorgeous she was, beyond how amazing the sex had been with her.

I really actually like Hilda.

So that was why, with a heavy heart and disappointed balls, I replied, 'No can do. Gorgeous as you are, and as amazing as it would be, the next time I see Hilda is when it gets serious.'

I sighed as I sent the text rejecting her. It felt wrong to reject such a babe, but alas Hilda was worth the sacrifice.

'Oh, I know, who do you think made her lay it all out on the table?' was the reply I got from Rosa not long later.

I blinked.

…What?

'You?' I replied, 'Why though? If you're interested, why make it harder by pushing Hilda to go for me harder?'

I was genuinely confused.

'I didn't say I was gonna steal you now did I?' was her reply, alongside a smug smirking emoji, 'Come on now, a guy like you, are you going to turn down getting a second absurdly gorgeous hot beyond belief babe of a second girlfriend?'

It was lucky I wasn't drinking anything or I'd have spat it all out over Aerodactyl's back. Dealing super effective damage.

And probably making Aerodactyl fall out of the air, where we would crash hard into the ground and I would die gruesomely.

Such was my shock.

'…Explain.' I replied.

'What's to explain?' Rosa replied in practically the blink of an eye, 'A lot of girls are going to be after you now after your showing against Lance, and they won't care if you're in a relationship already or not. I'm surprised you haven't had gold diggers from all over swarming you with how blase you were about bedding girls and being single. I'm interested too, and I have the advantage. Hilda is the front runner, all I had to do was wear my silly cousin down into letting me take second place.'

I stared blankly at the screen for a few moments. Surely not? There was no way Hilda would be fine with that…right?

…But then she did tell me I was free to go wild right, until I saw her again at least? That wasn't normal….or was it normal here?

I mean Gary had a full cheerleader squad he was banging, and they seemed totally fine with it.

I know the League was big on expanding the human populace, but polygamy?

I hadn't come across anything like that.

'That sounds too good to be true.' I replied.

'Well believe it buddy!' Rosa fired back.

…I dunno.

Well, there was a simple way to check.

'And if I call Hilda right now, she'll tell me she's fine with this?' I replied once more.

This time, it took a good few moments before Rosa replied, nearly a minute.

'…Don't do that. I might have jumped the gun a little and I'm still in the process of wearing her down, but it'll happen, trust me!' Rosa replied, 'You just have to say yes when I do. Don't act like you wouldn't want me and Hilda in your bed whenever you want, we both know you wanted to grab me by my twin tails and bend me over as soon as we saw each other on that video call a while back.'

…Well she wasn't wrong.

But this conversation in of itself was dangerous if Hilda ever saw it and she was not amendable to it.

So, I need to lock it off right now.

And do what any smart person would do in my position.

'…Take it up with Hilda. I'll leave it up to her.'

Delegate, delegate and delegate. Foist all responsibility on Hilda. The true path of a genius.

She replied a second later.

With a simple smirking confident looking emoji.

'=)'

…Well alright then. That, I was not expecting at all. Can't say I'm not interested though in how that will turn out.

Ganbare Hilda.

Can't say I'm not hopeful she'll manage to convince Hilda either. I'm only a man after all.

_________________

Rosa looked up from her phone with a smirk, 'As expected, he was pretty easy to convince.' she thought.

Not that she blamed Jord, what guy would say no in his position? It was smart of him though to side step any of the accountability by making her have to convince Hilda and refusing to commit himself.

She looked up from the phone, watching as Hilda scuffled around the bedroom getting ready for the day. They'd be heading out today to start cracking down on those goons from Team Plasma and start looking for her Eevee.

The cunts.

Her cousin hadn't even noticed her taking a picture of her fat ass as she got ready, to send to her beau.

The kid had wanted to fuck her no doubt, but locking him into a relationship when she barely knew him? That would be hard.

Luckily, she had an in with Hilda who was already half in love with Jord, if not more so despite the way she denied it.

She probably didn't even realise how often she scrolled through her pictures in her phone and stared at images of them together and sighed wistfully.

It honestly made her feel guilty that because of her, Hilda had come back to Unova when she could be living it up with Jord and having a whirlwind crazy action packed romance.

'Fucking Team Plasma.' she cursed in her head.

As it was though, she wanted him too. She wasn't in love with him, but that could come later. She liked his cocky shameless attitude though. And she absolutely adored his skill and ability as a trainer, he was amazing. And the casual way he gave away a Dragonite worth so much to Hilda, just to make sure she was safe?

What straight girl wouldn't swoon a little? You didn't get much greater in romantic gestures than that.

The only thing that topped something like that, would be if he fought like…a rampaging legendary pokemon for her or something.

So to put it bluntly, she wanted him. But she didn't want to take him from Hilda. As much as she loved teasing her and getting a rise out of her older cousin, she loved her dearly.

So that left her with the option, of both of them getting with Jord. And she would make it happen.

She'd yet to fail at achieving anything she truly put her mind to and this would go the same!

She just had to wear Hilda down. Take care of Team Plasma, save Eevee and then they could go together and lock Jord down with a pair of rings and rock his world.

A perfect plan indeed.

_____________________

# 179: Chapter 178

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

Just about caught up everything I have for Systematic Shinobi for the moment, so will be actively uploading what I have for this now.

_____________

It didn't take all that long, even with Aerodactyl's slower flight speed to reach our destination.

Not far from Pewter City, Aerodactyl touched down in front of a massive mausoleum. It was a near perfect replica of the one he saw in Fennel Valley, only this one was deep within the Viridian Forest. Trees and Foliage had grown wild around it, blotting it almost from view.

The home of Regirock.

Or…more like prison really.

As soon as I landed on Aerodactyl's massive form, dozens of pokemon roosting or lounging in the area made a break for it into the deeper forest. Mostly a bunch of caterpie, but a few pidgey in the trees followed suit.

"Good work mate." I returned Aerodactyl to his ball after I stepped off of his back, storing it back inside my gauntlet before making my way inside the huge abandoned ruin.

Even the inside was identical to the one if Fennel Valley. Right up into the main chamber containing a replica of that ugly ass statue of the evil bastard king, throne and all.

Luckily, I already had the other throne so I didn't need to bother with that this time around.

…Not that I should have in the first place, but god it was just so gaudy I couldn't help myself.

Shaking my head, I looked away, my gaze zeroing in on the puzzle on the wall to the far side of the massive main chamber.

…Now how to go about this?

All out water offensive? I could lead with both Gyarados and Blastoise, smashing Regirock with Hydro Pump before it even wakes.

But no, super effective or not, it would just shake it easily because of the sheer gulf in power between them.

I suppose I'll just rout it the same way I did Regice, but with both Exeggutor and Golem at the same time. Laying it flat with the explosions and status effects were the way to go.

They'd both already fought today, but I'd had them healed up by Blissey. Still, they'd expanded a lot of energy against Regice.

So after I take care of Regirock, I'll call it a night and let them rest for the night, and leave Registeel until tomorrow morning.

I could most likely do it without Exeggutor and Golem, especially with Regice on my side now, but setting up with the two of them, would make it much easier to bring them down.

A normal full on battle would most likely bring down the Mausoleum down on top of my head, destroying the ancient building in total.

With that thought in mind, I grabbed both of their gleaming black luxurious luxury balls from my gauntlet storage, and released them both.

Both appeared in bursts of silver light beside me, "Same as before you two," I told them, "Only this time, at the same time."

Exeggutor gave a sleepy trio of murmurs and nods, while Golem grinned and flashed me a thumbs up.

I pointed at the wall, "Break this one down as well with Brick Break, Golem." I ordered.

"Gol!" he nodded and walked forward, both myself and Exeggutor trailing behind him as he wound up his arm, now glowing with power and ploughed straight through the puzzle wall, as he had done earlier with the first one.

Smoke and debris wafted around us as we stepped into the chamber containing Regirock.

Like the one from earlier, it was empty save for a towering plateau of stairs rising high up above us, into a wide platform where the slumbering form of Regirock waited, alongside another large treasure chest.

I watched as its orange eyes flickered with golden light, "Un!…Un!…Un!" its slumped over form straightened up as it began awakening.

"Exeggutor go! Middle Leech Seed, Left, Sleep Powder, Right Poison Powder!" I commanded, lifting my gauntlet clad fist, "Golem, Sand Attack its eyes, blind it! Rotom, Thunder Wave!"

My pokemon leapt into action. The second the commands left my mouth, they burst forth.

From the left and right heads of Exeggutor, huge clouds of spores erupted from their mouths, glittering spores of cyan blue and deep royal purple. At the same time, Golem roared and tore one of his arms through the ground and tossed forth a literal wave of sand up amongst them, creating a crescent over Regirock's massive rocky form that cast a shadow upon it.

At the same time the middle spat forth a seed like a bullet, it pierced through the spores and sand both hitting the legendary rock type square in the face, before exploding into thick vines that wrapped tightly around Regirock's body, lighting up its body in shocks of crimson red that began to rapidly sap its energy.

"Un!" It made to spread its massive thick powerful rock arms out and tear the vines from its body, but before it could the amalgamation of spores and sand washed over its body,

Sand caked it from head to toe, covering its face, at the same time it slumped over falling asleep once more and its body bubbled with a venomous purple glow as poison took root.

And to hammer it in, a thin bolt of blue electricity erupted from my gauntlet clad palm, striking the now sleeping Regirock, paralyzing it.

Asleep, paralyzed, poisoned, eyes covered in sand and its energy now being rapidly drained by Leech Seed.

All before it even barely got the chance to really wake up and see what the hell was going on.

I am such a bad guy.

"You know what to do!" I continued, winding my fist back, even as I stepped back out of the room, taking cover behind the walls "Both of you, Explosion!"

"Golem!"

"Exeggutor!"

With cries of affirmation, both of them rushed forward and up the winding plateau of stairs, bodies beginning to glow a bright white with trembling crackling power.

And a second later, my vision went white as they erupted in twin thunderous explosions as soon as they reached the top and got beside Regirock, even hidden behind the walls as I was, and my hearing was drowned out as the world thundered and shook around me, the entire mausoleum shaking as if it were going to fall apart from the explosive force.

_____________

Rosa linked her fingers together and chuckled ominiously within a dark shadowy room, the only light, casting on her face, illuminated by the moonlight streaming in from the secure window, where none would see, "All according to cake, kukukukuku!"

Then a thump on the door made her yelp and jump.

"Rosa will you get out of the bathroom already!" Hilda huffed from outside, "You've been in there making weird giggles for an hour already, you better not be masturbating again!"

# 180: Chapter 179

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

'Note to self, two explosions at once is a bit much for old places like this.' I thought, blinking spots from my vision and stepping back through, even as my ears rang.

The plateau of stairs was just gone and I caught sight of all three of them, alongside the treasure chest itself falling from the air, a massive cloud of dust and debris swirling around them.

In my free hand, I held both of Golem and Exeggutor's luxury balls and quickly returned them before they could hit the ground, before stepping forward, hand that I'd wound up in preparation in advance, whipping forward in a punch.

A massive ethereal fist erupted from my gauntlet clad fist, Mach Punch attack slamming into Regirock's airborne form before it could hit the ground, carrying the massive rocky beast through the air where it smashed hard into the wall, cratering it.

And poor Regirock could do naught by lay there asleep and take it, even as poison and leech side sapped its energy continuously.

Quickly, I had Rotom slurp up their balls, before depositing the luxury ball of my newest and most powerful pokemon into my grasp.

"Let's go, Regice!" I declared, summoning forth a legendary pokemon for the first time ever into battle.

"Dododon!" Regice vibrated beside me as its towering glacial form appeared in a burst of silvery bright light from the luxury ball in my hand.

Six golden glowing dot like eyes settled on the prone form of its brethren, Regice floating slightly above the ground.

"Hammer Arm, let's go!" I clicked my fingers towards Regirock and commanded.

"Dododon!" Regice replied, and belying its massive size, Regice outright glided rapidly across the room, winding up a massive glacial arm glowing a bright white with power.

And brought it down atop the chest of the prone Regirock. The sheer force of the blow, lifted the massive rock legendary up off the ground despite it being a downward strike and smashed it even deeper into the wall, tripling the size of the crater and outright blasting a hole straight to the outside, Regirock's bulky body practically bouncing across the forest floor and landing prone outside.

A massively super effective strike.

"Don't let up!" I rushed behind Regice as the legendary followed Regirock outside, gliding out over the grass, "Hit it with Ice Beam, freeze it over before it can wake up!"

"Regiiiice!" the legendary cried out in response, making my eyes widen slightly in surprise.

I stopped and stared as Regice lifted both bulky icy arms up, twin spheres of icy blue energy forming in its palms, before erupting in an onslaught of jagged beams, a trio of massive jagged icy blue beams from each arm, slamming into Regirock feet first.

Its huge rocky feet froze over in seconds, and then Regice jerked its arms up trailing the beams up over Regirock's legs, encasing them in freezing cold blocks of ice, before continuing up over its torso freezing it whole, before finally reaching the head.

In seconds, Regirock's body was completely frozen over.

I couldn't help but wince.

I sure am ruthless.

Asleep, poisoned, being drained by leech seed, paralysis, sand in the eyes, twin explosions, a super effective mach punch, a super effective hammer arm while it was immobile from a legendary…and now frozen over completely with twin ice beams from said legendary.

This is the second legendary I've treated like this today.

I'm not sure how I feel about that, but nothing for it.

I stepped forward, walking around Regice's massive form, the ice type legendary not lowering its arms at all, ready and able to attack at a moments notice if Regirock broke free.

Such a diligent guy.

"Luxury Ball me, Rotom." I said, holding my palm out.

"Roger." Rotom replied, the slat on my palm opening up and spitting out an empty, shrunken gleaming black luxury ball.

Which I wasted no time in pressing the golden button in the middle of, enlarging it and tossing it at the prone beaten and frozen form of Regirock.

The gleaming expensive black poke ball tinked against the thick ice encompassing Regirock's form, bouncing up into the air above it as it clicked open and red light erupted from within, spreading over the frozen body of Regirock and slurping it up like a wet noodle.

Moments later, the luxury ball fell to the forest floor, golden centre glowing a thrumming crimson red as the ball began to shake back and forth.

Once.

…Twice.

…Thrice…!

Click!

A deep exhale of breath left me at the successful capture. Unlike before, my legs didn't turn to jelly beneath me, nor did the excitement threaten to overwhelm me like it had with Regice.

The reality of what the Regi's were and their mental state made it so I couldn't enjoy it as much.

But I couldn't deny the smile it put on my face.

"Two down, one to go," I laughed lightly as I walked over, picking up the gleaming expensive ball and held it up in my palm, looking it over, "Or well, three technically I suppose."

Since there was still Regieleki and Regidrago, but they were a foregone conclusion at this point.

I stared at it for a few more moments, before having Rotom store the luxury ball away for the moment and turned to the towering form of Regice.

It had lowered its arms with Regirock captured, and now simply waited for me to order it in whatever way I saw fit.

God it was going to be an uphill battle going forward with these guys.

Shaking my head, I walked over and placed my hand gently on the lower torso of the legendary ice type, patting it fondly, "Good work mate," I smiled up at him, "Let's go loot that old loser's treasure and then go make camp for the night, eh?"

Regice in reply, simply inclined its head, "Dododon."

__________

Pokemon: Ju-Jumpyou Kaisen Version

# 181: Chapter 180

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

_______

"Regirock. Gender: None. Ability: Sturdy. Height: 14'08. Weight: 2428 pounds. Attacks Known: Iron Defence, Zap Cannon, Explosion, Sandstorm, Lock On, Hyper Beam, Focus Punch, Ancient Power, Shock Wave, Stone Edge, Earthquake, Hammer Arm, Superpower, Rock Slide, Fire Punch, Ice Punch, Meteor Beam, Drain Punch. Data indicates, that much like Regice, Regirock is proficient in both long range and close range combat, but considering the multitude of fist style attacks, it may prefer close range combat."

Sitting down on a chair an hour or so after catching Regirock, within my campsite for the night, all of my pokemon out and about throughout the clearing, I stared down at Rotom Gauntlet as it detailed my newest pokemon's abilities.

'There's a lot of overlap.' I thought, leaning back and looking up, meeting the duo of gazes of both Regice and Regirock standing silently before me, not moving a muscle-

Not that they really had muscles but the point stood.

Like Regice, Regirock instantly obeyed any command I gave it, and was much the same in personality.

That being a lack there of.

As expected.

But yes, there was a lot of overlap between their attacks. Zap Cannon and Lock On for instance. And Hyper Beam, Earthquake, Hammer Arm and such.

Not to mention Explosion.

I suppose it made sense that the Regi's would have some attack overlap, considering they would have spent an excessive amount of time battling side by side before they got put to guard that oaf's treasures.

The twat.

Though interestingly, while Regice was about a half a foot taller than Regirock, Regirock had a good three hundred pounds on the ice golem.

I suppose that's because rock is more dense than ice.

I sighed, shaking my head and clenched my hand letting Rotom know to compress into his bracelet form and free my hand.

'I do like they both have Explosion though, and so will Registeel most likely.' my lips quirked up in a half smirk.

Explosion was fucking amazing.

Not only was it absurdly powerful, but it was an attack that outright ignored a lot of the targets durability. How? I have no idea, even Rotom couldn't explain it to me with his access to the internet and many a database.

I'm not going to complain though. Who would? Not to mention it was basically the ultimate fail safe.

There isn't many things out there that could shake off three Regi's exploding in their face as a group and nuking them to hell.

Five if Regidrago and Regieleki know it as well, and if they don't, I'll just teach them it. Five legendary explosions to the face. Then Golem and Exeggutor following up or with them? It would take a real monster to stay up.

What the fuck boom indeed.

Standing up, I stretched out my arms and wiggled the fingers on my right hand feeling the cool breeze on my now free fingers with Rotom in bracelet mode, and walked over to stand in front of the pair of Regi's, directly in front of Regirock and looked up into its dulled orange dotted gaze.

Charizard who had been laying down behind my chair stirred, tensing a bit I could see in my peripherals, and rolled my eyes fondly.

And I could feel Pidgeot's gaze burning into my back from where he was roosting up above in a large tree, and Milotic who was resting alongside Gyarados in a large pond I'd set up camp beside, didn't raise her head from where it lay on the bank of the pond, but her eyes did open and stare sharply.

Gyarados on the other hand, roused slightly sensing her change, before promptly scoffing through his massive mouth and closing his eyes and going to sleep. And Blastoise who was lazily floating in the pond, didn't even bat an eye.

The reactions of my pokemon were a mixed bag like that.

Tyranitar though who was lazily laying on his side leaning against a huge rock, just looked confused.

'Such worry warts.' I resisted the urge to snort as I reached up and pat Regirock's lower stomach, "Alright big guy?" I asked.

"Un." was the only sound Regirock made in response, a slight incline of its head, but that was all.

A rock of few words.

"I'm Jord," I just grinned, not bothered, "I'm your new trainer now, so good to meet you. Welcome to this big crazy family of ours. Let's get along alright mate?"

"Un." Regirock simply nodded again, while Regice looked on blankly.

These silly things.

"Feel free to do your own thing outside of training," I gestured at the expansive camp and the dozens of pokemon all around us, "And if you need anything, just come to me and let me know yeah?"

Regirock stared at me blankly for a moment, before turning its great rocky head and looking at Regice, "Un! Un! Un?" its orange eyes flashed golden.

Regirock…asked something?

"Dododon!" Regice's own eyes lit up with a golden hue and it nodded its big icy pillar spike of a head in reponse.

"Rotom?" I prompted.

"Regirock is asking Regice what you mean, it does not understand being given leave to do as it pleases, it does not even have anything it wants to do," Rotom translated, "Regice is confirming your words and explaining while it does not truly understand either, you are far different from their old master."

…Huh.

Well, that was helpful I guess. I waited as Regirock finally turned back around, and slowly dipped down to take a knee, bowing its rocky spire of a head, "Regiiiirock." it intoned, all six eyes flashing gold.

I didn't need to have Rotom translate that. I got the general gist at least, "Good to have you alongside me mate." I grinned, patting it fondly on the torso once more, smiling as brightly as I could and showing none of my simmering rage.

_______

Ten minutes later, I retreated into my tent, heaving a deep heavy sigh, shoulders slumping, tension leaving me.

I was extremely tense dealing with Regice and Regirock. Not out of fear or anything, but simply frustration and anger.

While I plan to help them, I've still got little more than hopes to help them and nothing concrete.

It really honestly did piss me off that two of my pokemon were in such a sad state despite their strength and legendary status. Who cared if I just caught them today? They're my fucking pokemon now.

If I could, I'd ask Celebi or Dialga to take me back in time so I could go beat the piss out of the King of Pokelantis.

As it was, between the catching of the two, introducing my new pokemon to our customary training methods and my anger over their state? I was exhausted.

My eyes landed on my large soft sleeping bag and giant pillows, where the slumbering form of Togepi snoozed away, Persian laying protectively beside the little tyke.

Persian opened an eye as I approached, collapsing on my side beside the great big cream furred cat with a tired huff, "I didn't expect them to be that bad you know?" I said mostly to myself as I rubbed my hand along his furry head, drawing a purr from the normal type, "I knew it wouldn't be great, but they don't even have any desires at all, hell they don't even understand what it means. I have no idea what to do." I admitted in but a whisper, not wanting to wake Togepi.

I had said as much, or thought as much at least earlier, and it wasn't going to stop me from trying, but I was just pathetically venting.

Like the big baby I was in body now, even if I'm mentally eighteen.

The best part about being de-aged to this physical age, is I could get away with some kiddy shit like whining like a baby like this.

"Mrow~" Persian purred, stretching out his legs, before rolling over and plopping his head in my lap and simply rubbing his head against my stomach.

My lips twitched a bit, relaxing a bit at the close contact, "Thanks mate." I whispered, wrapping my arms around his thick furry neck and lowering my head to press it against his, and just bask in his warm comfortable body.

Yeah…this was fine.

I'll probably rage over the state of the Regi's quite a few times in the coming days, but it'll fine.

I've got all my pokemon beside me after all.

With them by my side, I'm unstoppable.

I fell alseep not long later, hugging Persian. He was amazing like that. He may not have been my strongest, not by a long shot, but Persian had practically raised all my other Meowth himself.

He was reliable and warm.

____________

Lance stared blankly at the computer screen before him. He ran a hand down his face, massaging his temples as he closed his eyes and let out a deep breath.

Before opening them again, lips pursing as he nodded, "So I'm not seeing things at all." he said to himself simply.

On the screen, was the trainer profile of one Jord Odhar.

A now classified trainer profile that only the highest ranking of league officials and those with special permission could now look at freely outside of league tournaments Jord would participate in.

That was perhaps why it had taken until a little while ago for him to have been alerted to the newest additions to Jord Odhar's ever increasingly massive squad of monsters.

A whole flock of Fearow, and then a Tyranitar of all things. Just days after battling him, he went out and caught a Tyranitar of all things?

The absurdity of it was shocking. That made the third pseudo legendary pokemon the young prodigy had captured, even if he had given one away.

But no, it didn't end there, it got much crazier.

Because of course for Jord even catching a pseudo legendary Tyranitar wasn't enough.

No apparently the boy had captured two legendary pokemon!

'How!?' Lance resisted the urge to yell it and shake the computer screen atop his desk.

It just didn't make sense. How he came across all these rare and absurdly powerful pokemon.

One or two, three even or a small group he could see.

But…a Tyranitar, then Regice of all things, then Regirock a few hours later? Impossible.

If it was just one of the legendary golems and the Tyranitar, he might have been able to rationalize it, but Regirock, hours later, in a completely different location?

"He knows exactly where all these pokemon are." Lance nodded to himself, leaning back.

How though was the question. He'd checked his records, how could a boy registered as from Unova, missing parents listed as a father from Galar and mother from Unova, who grew up on the streets, who just arrived in Kanto a few months ago.

Know about all these rare and powerful pokemon?

'Not to mention the absurd speed of growth for his pokemon.' he threaded his fingers together as he stared at the image of Jord Odhar staring back at him, cocky grin on his face.

"Time travel?" he wondered idly. Could he have come across a Celebi perhaps?

But no that didn't make sense, Celebi physically travelled through time, they couldn't send someone back to their younger body….to his knowledge.

The only ones he could think of, at least as far as pokemon went, that could possibly have done such a thing was the legendary god of time itself, Dialga.

Which made it all the more unlikely.

'Not time travel.' he decided. That also didn't make sense, Jord was definitely a teenager. A cocky one for sure, and far more accomplished than was reasonable, but he was far too immature to be an adult who had seen all these pokemon in the future and come back.

What then? A psychic perhaps? One who could divine locations and such? They weren't exactly common, but they weren't rare either.

'No if he was a psychic he'd have used it in that viral video when his Gyarados attacked him.' he shook his head, it wouldn't make sense for him not to. He wasn't showing off for video, he was caught unaware and recorded without his knowledge when it happened.

Lance leaned back, resting his head at the head rest of his chair and stared up at the ceiling, "It doesn't matter how he knows, he just does…" he trailed off, and his lips quirked into an amused smirk, "Did losing rankle your feathers so much Jord? That you immediately went for a rock type pseudo legendary and then an ice type legendary?"

While the kid took the loss in stride and very gracefully - and why wouldn't he, the kid was amazing for his age even if he took it a little easy on him - he did seem the type that wouldn't be content with that.

So he could see him immediately going for a counter to defeat Lance, even if it was a little petty to go that far.

"What an amazing kid." he chuckled to himself, shaking his head as his blood began to rush in his veins as excitement began to build.

He already wanted to battle that kid again. After all, no doubt the boy was definitely going for Registeel next. How? Lance had no idea, but his instincts were telling him Jord would have Registeel as well soon.

And how amazing would it be to take them on?

'He's definitely going to win the Indigo Plateau this year, and with the way he's growing…he might actually make it all the way to me.' he thought, broad grin on his face.

He already couldn't wait.

Their rematch battle would be…legendary.

He stood up clenching his fists, grinning widely, "Time to go train some more." he decided, uncaring of the late hour.

# 183: Chapter 182

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

The next morning, after breakfast was out of the way, and the ever amazing morning training was taken care of, I stood before my assembled pokemon.

All of the pokemon I brought with me, barring Wartortle stood lined up together.

Well, not all of them.

"Togepri! Togepri!" Togepi sitting atop my head happily busied himself by playing around with my spiky hair.

No doubt I made for a menacing sight indeed.

My eyes swept over the gargantuan serpentine forms of Milotic and Gyarados ending at both sides, to the huge try hard birds directly in the middle, to the titanic forms of Tyranitar and the two legendary golems Regice and Regirock standing side by side.

Charizard in between Dragonite and Aerodactyl, dwarfing the orange dragon runt who despite having shot up a foot since I caught him, was multiple feet smaller.

Either way, from the titanic beasts to the tiny but calm, cool and collected Servine and Raboot, they made for a formidable line up indeed.

And today, there was a battle I wanted to start with before we left.

I dramatically pointed at the fluffy cloud winged blue dragon, "Altaria, today you'll be battling Pidgeot, I want to see that power you've grown under a champion!" I declared.

"Altaria al." the fluffy dragon trained by a champion and dragon master abruptly shook her head, denying the request and shrugging her wings.

You wot mate?

"No?" I raised a brow.

"Taria." Altaria nodded, confirming she was denying my request. Immediately, dozens of heads snapped around to glare deathly at her.

Milotic opened her wide maw as she towered over everyone there but Gyarados, levelling her head close to Altaria from where her body was over fifteen feet away, "Milo!" she bared her fangs at the dragon threateningly.

And Charizard spat a gout of flame, glaring over the head of Dragonite. While beside him?

Aerodactyl gained a massive shit eating grin, looking between Altaria and the angered others eagerly.

No doubt hoping she'd get her ass kicked.

'Weird, whenever I asked her to do something before, she obeyed pretty easily.' I thought. In fact, while I haven't bonded with her much since it's only been a few days, we got along pretty well, "Any reason why you don't want to battle girl? You seemed to quite enjoy it when you were kicking Aerodactyl around." I pointed out.

"Aero!" Aerodactyl rose up in protest, waving his wings about, shit eating grin replaced by a pout.

Altaria looked at the looming threatening form of Milotic, and glanced at Charizard at her other side not far away, before scoffing and rolling her eyes, completely unthreatened.

She simply pointed her wing at Aerodactyl, then to her stomach, "Taria altaria al!"

I blinked.

Milotic blinked.

Charizard blinked.

There were a great many blinks. Even Rotom's screen at the back of my gauntlet blinked with a flash of light, "Altaria is saying that she is carrying an egg…no two? Two eggs. Odd, such a small number for an Altaria, they usually lay clutches of four to eight on average."

"I got that more or less." I replied dumbly, and that made sense. Lance told me straight up that the medicine he'd sent me to make sure Altaria bred true for an Aerodactyl baby, would lower the amount she'd usually clutch to force one.

I suppose one was an Aerodactyl egg and the other would be a Swablu egg. No idea what I'd do with a baby Swablu, but considering it was bred from an Elite four level Altaria by Lance's estimation and my Aerodactyl, it would be a beast of a baby.

"Did you also understand that she told Milotic and Charizard to and I quote 'shut the fuck up or I will fucking eat you both.'" Rotom helpfully added.

I blinked again, "No…no I did not, thanks for telling me I guess." I added blankly.

Well…she was a glutton after all that loved eating. She was like a Snorlax trapped in the body of a fluffy beautiful dragon.

It was a testament to the happy news though, that neither Charizard nor Milotic got outraged over the threat, and instead a massive rouse of cheers erupted from many of my assembled pokemon.

I could understand the excitement.

After all, one of those eggs was going to fetch me a hundred million smackaroonies.

__________

Little more than an hour later after the joyous news I received from Altaria, that would make me an even more wealth man after our early morning training-

Registeel fell to its knees before me.

Six dull crimson red eyes slowly closed, thick vines entrapped its form, wrapping it tightly, while electricity sparked over its form, locking its limbs with paralysis.

Beside me, the titanic forms of Regice and Regirock lumbered forward from either side of me, "Hammer Arm, both of you." I ordered.

"Un! Un! Un!"

"Dododon!"

Their arms lit up with bright power as they lunged forward at absurd speeds despite their huge, heavy cumbersome bodies and delivered a pair of heavy super effective fighting attack blows that blew the sleeping entrapped legendary steel golem flying back, crashing through a thick rocky pedestal and smashing into the far side of the cavernous room we were in, crumpling within the massive crater its gleaming metal body made.

"Charizard," I said, and the huge orange winged beast standing protectively behind me, spread his wings out wide, "Flamethrower."

With a roar, Charizard opened his maw wide, and I felt the the sheer heat radiating from his attack as he unleashed a massive gout of searing flame from above me.

The gout of flame, so large and intense slammed into Registeel's prone sleeping entrapped form and spread out from the point of impact like a miniature tsunami of red, yellow and orange as my Charizard roasted the steel type legendary in his rock melting flame.

He kept it up for over twenty seconds, before having to cut off the attack to catch his breath.

And left the slumbering, scorched Registeel bare to my eyes once more, the vines sapping its energy gone, burnt to ashes, and its massive metal spherical body floating in the middle of a pool of molten rock, literal magma formed from the ground and the sheer heat of Charizard's flames.

Despite taking down a legendary, my third. I felt almost nothing but resigned pity for Registeel.

There was little to feel accomplished in taking down the legendary. Putting aside the fact I basically swat raided it like Regice and Regirock before it, it is simply in a pitiful living condition just like its brethren.

Time to get this over with.

….Though.

I eyed the pool of liquid molten rock warily, "…Regice can you cool the ground down with your Ice Beam?" I asked.

No way I was tossing a luxury ball into that, or getting near it myself. I might be a badass, but I'm not magma proof.

…Yet.

'Ash could shake off his Charizard's flamethrower after all.' I rationalized. I just need to get even more swoll, and my muscles will deflect heat from sheer awesomeness.

Or something something aura.

"Dododon!" Regice nodded and held up both hands, six jagged icy blue beams lancing out to strike at the molten ground and freeze it over.

And Registeel alongside it.

And thus, Registeel was captured.

# 184: Chapter 183

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

__________

"Registeel. Gender: None. Ability: Clear Body. Height: 14'02. Weight: 3156 pounds. Attacks Known: Iron Defence, Zap Cannon, Explosion, Lock On, Hyper Beam, Focus Punch, Ancient Power, Shock Wave, Stone Edge, Earthquake, Hammer Arm, Superpower, Metal Claw, Sandstorm, Iron Head, Shadow Claw, Thunder Punch, Ice Punch, Metal Sound, Steel Beam, Heavy Slam. Data indicates that much like Regice and Regirock, Registeel can function well in either close range or long range combat, And I cannot discern a preference when it comes to combat from the current data."

I leaned back in the saddle atop Aerodactyl's back, listening to Rotom's data on Registeel was we flew high above the ground.

Our destination?

Porta Vista. The last place I'd be visiting in Kanto before I fly the coop for a few months. Just to pay a visit to Ash, Misty and Brock, and drop off a few leaf stones for them and Wartortle with Misty.

Mmm, Misty.

'No, bad Jord.' I mentally scolded, I was planning on locking down and going serious with Hilda in the future wasn't I?

…But then she did tell me to feel free to go wild until I saw her again.

Thankfully, I didn't need to ponder on the morality of it for much longer, because just then, Rotom Gauntlet spoke up.

"Incoming call." he said, breaking me from my thoughts.

"From who?" I asked, and for a second my heart leapt thinking it was a certain fluffy haired older babe-

"Champion Lance." Rotom dashed my hopes brutally.

My disappoint was immeasurable, yet my day was not ruined.

I needed to call Lance anyway to update him on Altaria, so this saved me time. After all, pokemon pregnancies were weird. By tomorrow, she'd have laid her eggs.

"Accept the call, Rotom." I ordered.

"Affirmative." Rotom replied, and a second later, the screen at the back of my gauntlet clad fist blinked and then lit up as Lance's face appeared on the screen.

"Lance, good to see you," I grinned, "I was actually planning to call you soon anyway, good timing."

"Is that right?" Lance grinned back, "Is there something you want to tell me? Something monumental maybe?"

Huh, how did he know?

"Yeah," I nodded, "Altaria confirmed she had eggs this morning, two of them even. If you want to meet me at Porta Vista tomorrow, you should be able to pick up the egg you wanted."

Somehow Lance's grin grew even wider, "Really? That's great Jord!" he beamed, letting out a boisterous laugh, eyes lighting up. Before suddenly sobering a second later, giving me a serious look, eyebrows raised in question, "But wait, is that it? That's all you wanted to tell me?"

I raised a brow, "Yes?" I replied, confused at his reaction, "Is there something else I should be telling you about?"

Lance shook his head, grin returning, yet wry this time, "You sure? Nothing come to mind that happened to you recently? That you jumped feet first into? Something I specifically would love to know about?"

My brows furrowed as I tried to think, before it clicked. Ah right, of course he'd know! "Dratini skin boots are really sturdy, yet surprisingly comfortable," I flashed him a thumbs up with my other hand, "Worth every penny to get them made." I assusured him, glancing down at the pale scale boots adorning my feet.

He was the one to recommend the place I got them made.

I'd picked them up the night before I left Viridian City, got them made from some of the shed Dratini skin I found at the Dragon Island.

"Oh you got them already? That's great, they're amazing aren't they? I never go anywhere without mine. They're amazing for pretty much any terrain and never let any water in and-" Lance began while beaming, before pausing and pointing at me through the screen, "Stop distracting me. You know what I mean. Were you not going to tell me you captured legendary pokemon!?"

Wait.

That was what he was getting at?

"No, why would I?" I replied with a blunt snort, "You're literally monitoring my trainer page, you pretty much admitted that the other day. Mate, why the hell would I tell you something you already know?"

I got enough scolding for catching crazy pokemon from Nurse Joy and Hilda, I didn't need to add a champion to the mix thank you very much.

"Wait you're not denying any of it?" Lance looked like the wind was slurped from his sails, as if a Snorlax had a giant straw and just slurped it all up by my response and rolled his eyes, "Well that's no fun. Though, I don't suppose you'll tell me just how you knew where to find them? Or any of the other absurdly rare and powerful pokemon you've found?"

As a matter of fact, I don't mind at all.

I already devised my story with Hilda after all.

"Sure, I've got nothing to hide," I shrugged, "I came into contact with a super powerful psychic pokemon back in Unova, it let loose a ton of psychic energy at the time and I got caught in the blast. I pretty much had psychic powers, and used them to see if I could find any pokemon that stood out in Kanto since I was coming here, and it worked out. Though, I more or less used up all the power just before I landed in Kanto."

Lance's eyes widened and his jaw dropped, "Wait, you're actually telling me? And it actually was psychic powers?" he shook his head in amazement, "Well…I don't even know what to say in response to that. You're an incredible kid you know that? Anyone else would have tried to hide such a thing."

"I've got nothing to hide." I shrugged.

"Sure kid," Lance snorted, "Any other absurd pokemon your planning on capturing soon then you don't mind telling me? So I'm not as shocked in the future. Maybe you're hiding Rayquaza in your pocket?" he laughed.

I smirked, "Well maybe not Rayquaza, but I do have something about the same size in my boxers." I boasted.

Lance burst out laughing.

___________

After a long talk with Lance about the specifics of my 'psychic powers' and giving him a brief description of Mewtwo that had him reeling about the 'unknown' pokemon, we set up a time and place for me to meet him tomorrow.

And exchange Altaria's aerodactyl egg, for the hundred big ones. And, not long later Aerodactyl touched down on the far end of the beach of Porta Vista.

"Thanks for the ride mate, take a rest you big daddy to be you." I laughed, patting his jaw.

Aerodactyl just rolled his eyes before I returned him to his ball.

I turned after storing his ball in Rotom Gauntlet, taking in the city in the distance. I'd landed on the far side of the beach, so people didn't swamp me about Aerodactyl.

I walked my way down the beach, taking in the sights. The warm sand, the crystaline blue water, the pokemon out and about.

I passed a few Krabby meandering about the beach, Wingull flew above me, and even a Shellder was resting in the sand as I walked by, I was so tempted to catch it, but held back.

"Just a kid from Pallet Town, with a whole new world to see." I hummed quietly as I made my towards the city-

But as I was going I heard a shout from behind me.

"Coming through!" a shout came from behind me, and before I could react, something banged into my shoulder from behind, making me stumble a step, blur of brown and red to crumple at my side, "Ah, sorry!" they said, a boy about the same height as me, with a squeaky feminine voice that almost had me thinking he was a girl, before I got a proper look.

It was a boy, maybe around Ash's age, if not a little bit older. Dark skinned, dressed in red, with brown hair highlighted at the front with a tuft of red, "Sorry again dude!" he squeaked, bowing at me, the cap on his head almost flying off before he grabbed it and turned, rushing off before I could say anything.

I watched him go blankly for a second, "What a weirdo." I snorted, and with a shrug continued on my way.

Or tried to, as I stepped forward my foot clinked against something, and I raised a brow as I lifted my foot, to reveal…a pokeball?

Though it was different from any pokeball I'd ever seen.

It was a dark brown in coloration, with veins of golden yellow all around it, a burnt golden middle somewhat like a luxury ball, only duller.

And with four golden latches over the small button in the centre. I picked it up, looking it over. 'Did that guy drop it?' I wondered, and was about to call after him.

But as I looked in the direction he ran off, I couldn't see him at all.

"What the hell, how fast was he running?" I snorted. Well, whatever. Mine now I suppose.

Finders keepers and all that.

I gripped the odd pokeball with Rotom Gauntlet, before storing it inside it and continuing on my way.
