# 185: Chapter 184

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

____________

"Jord, hey Jord!"

I was met with quite the welcome sight when I made it to the main resort area of Porta Vista. The beach resort was filled with hundreds of people and pokemon spread throughout the vast beach area, food and item stalls, little restaurants and more lining the beach as far as the eye could see.

I'd barely sent a text letting them know I was in the area now to see them, before they were rushing over.

Misty was the one who called out to me, and I turned to see her jogging over, my eyes instinctively roaming over her body appreciatively in the tiny little white bikini she wore that hugged her curves nicely, a yellow unzipped hoodie hanging from her shoulders.

Damn she looked good.

Truly an electric tale if there ever was one.

Ash and Brock followed behind her, clad only in swim shorts, a massive grin was on Ash's face as met my eyes.

"Misty!" I grinned at her as she reached me, slowing to a stop and breathing a little heavily, which did amazing things to her scantly clad chest, "Looking great as always."

She grinned proudly, placing a hand proudly on her hip and cocking it proudly, "Don't I?" the orangette boasted, giving me a wink. Then she looked me up and down, taking in the pants I was wearing, alongside my boots and sleeveless hoodie, "You however are overdressed." she commented, just as Ash and Brock reached us.

The pair of them shared a look as I smirked at Misty. I unzipped my hoodie, leaving my torso bare, Blissey sculpted god tier musculature in full view, "There, that better?" I teased as I tied it around my waist.

"No, you're still wearing pants." Misty only smirked back as her eyes roamed over my abs of Metagrossite.

…No that didn't work.

Something to think on for later.

"Well the day's still young," I laughed with a shrug, before turning to Ash and Brock, "Guys, great to see you."

"You too buddy!" Ash flashes me a thumbs up, Pikachu on his shoulder chirping a greeting to, "You've been on fire lately, we saw your battle against Lance, that was amazing!"

"That, and the video going viral about you wrestling with your pokemon, that was incredible as well," Brock shook his head smiling wryly, "A Gyarados and a Dragonite? That was crazy to see, just as shocking as that battle against Lance. And that Blastoise you caught didn't even want to attempt it after those two. That was impressive I have to admit Jord, absurdly dangerous and reckless and not at all how someone should deal with newly caught aggressive pokemon…but impressive nonetheless."

Misty met my eyes at the mention of Blastoise, brow raised, and I gave her a simple nod. Yes, it was the same one I told her about, and she simply nodded.

"It wasn't really the plan initially, I hadn't expected Gyarados to be that aggressive and I let Dragonite out after it by mistake, my hands were all bloodied and I fumbled," I shrugged and explained, "Still, what have you guys been up to recently? I've not seen you guys since Vermillion City, and well, Misty calling me to ask about that Riolu you caught to ask if I knew anything."

"Oh yeah!" Ash snapped his fingers, "Thanks for that by the way, that was a massive help."

"It was," Misty smiled, before shrugging herself, "And well, not much. After you left, we got into that mess with Riolu and Ash caught an Onix, which he evolved into a Steelix not long later with a metal coat."

"And then we totally crushed Surge!" Ash boasted, pumping his fist with a grin.

"After he crushed you the first time you mean?" Misty snorted, making him stumble and laugh sheepishly.

I looked to Brock who laughed, "He battled Surge one on one, Pikachu against Raichu, it was pretty close actually, but Pikachu lost in the end," the formed gym leader informed me, "He spent a few days putting Pikachu through some tough training for a re-match…had a little spat with a real piece of work, and then Ash had his rematch and managed to take down Surge's Raichu with Pikachu's newly learned Volt Tackle attack."

My eyebrows raised, he knew Volt Tackle already? Pikachu wouldn't learn that originally until Battle Frontier when it was on the level of an Elite Four pokemon.

"Impressive," I nodded truthfully.

"Thanks," Ash beamed, "Not as impressive as you taking on Lance of all people. But, I caught a Gengar as well while we were doing that training with Pikachu for our rematch and he's super tough! Next time we have a re-match Jord we won't lose!"

It took considerable effort not to let my jaw drop a bit at the reveal Ash just dropped. Gengar?

And he had Riolu as well?

Was it the same Gengar he got in the future? The one on his Journeys team? That meant he had picked up two of his World Monarch team.

Already?

Is this because of me? Or was he just always fated to come across these pokemon? This Ash was a lot farther ahead than the other ones for just starting out. As far as fully evolved pokemon he has, I know he also had a Beedrill, Butterfree, Venomoth, Scizor, Quagsizre, Ludicolo, Cloyster, Starmie and now both a Steelix and a Gengar.

J-

Arceus man, if this Ash had been in the normal timeline where he started at ten, he would have dominated everything.

Unluckily for him though, this is not that timeline, and I am here.

"That's great, Gengar are amazing pokemon!" I praised him, before looking at Brock and Misty, "What about you two, catch anything new lately?"

"Nah, the last I caught was Magikarp and Oddish at that pokemon village we visited on the way to Vermillion City," Misty shrugged with a roll of her eyes, "Not everyone is as catch crazy as you and Ash."

"Same," Brock nodded in agreement with her, "The last I got were two from there as well, my Paras and Weepinbell."

I nodded, and honestly, thinking on it. Considering the helpful spores Paras could make and stuff? And that their mushrooms had medicinal properties and could be used to make powerful potions, Paras really did suit Brock as a pokemon.

That said, "Magikarp, huh?" I smiled lightly at Misty, she had confessed to me way back on our date at Pewter, that even in this timeline she had feared Gyarados as a species.

She smiled back, "Gotta get over it sometime, right?" she simply shrugged.

Good for her.

To be quite honest, there was a few variations of Misty that varied between the timelines. The worst one of all, had been the other timeline Misty that travelled with Ash, the anime one, not the manga electric tales of Misty who was simply amazing.

I had hated the anime timeline Misty. Not just because of her annoying attitude, but because of the sheer hypocrisy and self entitlement of her.

Putting Togepi and everything else aside in their journey. The defining point in Ash's journey, where the idea originated for him to use new pokemon after Johto, came from Misty herself deriding him and constantly getting on his case…for using Charizard during Johto.

As if it was unfair for him to use a pokemon he had raised and trained already when challenged to a battle, against beginners or even gym leaders. As if using a pokemon he had raised to be strong instead of weak young ones…made him a worse trainer?

It came off as pure jealousy to me.

Especially because, immediately after leaving Ash she returned to the Cerulean City gym, and got her sisters strongest pokemon, their Gyarados. A Gyarados that was far far stronger than any of her own pokemon.

And proceeded to basically barely ever use any of her own raised pokemon in battle again, only using the powerful Gyarados…raised by her sister.

After barely training her pokemon at all during the journey with Ash. And ended up getting mega evolution for Gyarados and everything.

So yes, I had absolutely loathed that Misty.

This Misty though, was an entirely different woman.

…It made me wonder if Iris in this world is more like her game or manga counterparts? Because those Iris' were amazing girls, while the anime timeline Iris was a trash heap in the same vein as that same timelines Misty.

"Speaking of," I held my gauntlet clad fist up, and had Rotom spit out three Leaf Stones, "Here's the leaf stones I had for you guys."

"Oh, is that the new Silph Co storage tech?" Brock's eyes lit up as he accepted his stone first, "And in gauntlet form huh? That's an inspired choice I gotta admit. Thanks Jord."

"That looks cool as hell, but yeah thanks Jord," Ash beamed, "Though I don't really have anything that evolves with a Leaf Stone like Brock's Weepinbell." he frowned before shrugging and beaming again, "Still good to have on hand though!"

"Considering how many pokemon you catch, it won't be long before you find one," Misty rolled her eyes, before smiling at me, "Thanks Jord, this'll be good for my Oddish when she evolves."

"No prob," I shrugged waving it off, "Speaking of gifts though, is there anywhere we can make a trade around here? I've brought Wartortle with me."

Misty's eyes lit up, "Oh! There's a small pokemon centre not far from here where we can do it."

"How come Misty gets a free pokemon too?" Ash pouted.

"Don't you already have a Squirtle?" I raised a brow.

"Well yeah, but what makes Misty special?" Ash shrugged.

"Boobs," Brock smirked, "She has them, you do-" he continued, only to double over as Misty buried an elbow in his gut.

"Oh shut up you eternal virgin chud," Misty huffed and grabbed my elbow, "You two can go play in the sand, while I spend time with a real man."

"Hey!" Ash glared pointing a scandalized finger, "What about me and Brock, are we chopped liver?!"

"A real man." Misty smugly repeated, dealing critical damage as Ash huffed, and Brock's knees gave out beneath him in defeat, before dragging me off, arm nestled between her luscious cleavage.

_________________

"Thanks for this," Misty said gratefully not long later as we stood within the trade room of the Pokemon Centre, Wartortle's gleaming black luxury ball in hand, a warm smile on her face, "I've always wanted a Blastoise you know?"

"Well it would be weird for Misty, the future Kanto master of the Water Type to not have a Blastoise, right?" I grinned as I walked over to her, "I've got something else for you."

Her orange eyebrows rose as she looked up from the luxury ball, "More? You've just given me an evolutionary stone and a Wartortle, a pretty strong one at that considering the attacks it knows," she gave him a ludicrous look, sea-green eyes incredulous, before shaking her head in amusement, "Your sense of scale is all over the place you know? Between the stone and Wartortle, they would go enough for me to buy a decently big three bedroom house back in Cerulean you know?"

Probably, considering how messed up the property rates and such were in this world. I still couldn't really wrap my head around it, and I've currently got a massive mansion home and place being built for me and my pokemon.

I just shrugged, before holding my hand out, showing off a small dark blue scale, "What's this?" Misty asked, as she took it, "It's pretty." she noted.

"It's a Dragon Scale," I told her, making her eyes go wide, one of the many I'd picked up on Dragonite Island, they were all over the place there. Considering the colours of the scale, it was probably from a Dragonair, "You'll probably want to catch a Horsea or Seadra at some point right? With this, you'll be able to evolve a Seadra into a Kingdra."

Misty stared at the dragon scale in her hand for a moment, before biting her lip and releasing a deep shuddering breath. She looked back up at me from it, "You're leaving Kanto tomorrow right?" she asked, stowing it and Wartortle's expensive black ball in her hoodie pocket.

"Yeah, there's things I need that I can't get in Kanto, I'll be back though." I assured her. There was one final stop I need to make, but honestly, it would just make my journey to Kalos quicker.

I'd originally planned on catching that Bulbasaur living in the sewers of Trovitopolis in the Orange Islands because it was way bigger than a normal Bulbasaur. But I'd been thinking on it the past few days.

That poor Bulbasaur has been living in a sewer for decades at this point. It wouldn't be in any real shape to train and become stronger. And while I don't really mind if not all my pokemon are interested in getting stronger, the situations with the Regi's made me think that perhaps I wasn't what that Bulbasaur needed.

So instead, I'll tell Lance about it. And cut down my time in the Orange Islands to just going after the Crystal Onix and perhaps one of those giant Rhydon.

And I'll go to the Bulbasaur secret garden and capture the Venusaur there instead. That thing was a beast anyway.

And perhaps if I did, it wouldn't get in the way of Ash's Bulbasaur evolving this time too, killing two Pidove with one stone.

Misty nodded simply, before stepping close to me and throwing her arms around my neck, pressing soft pillowy breasts against my chest and smiling warmly at me, "I've got a separate room from Ash and Brock here for the next few days," she said softly, "Come up with me?" she asked, pecking me softly on the lips.

My hands came down to settle on her warm, bare hips and squeezed the skin softly, making her mewl into me as I returned the kiss. Before pulling back and laying my forehead across hers, "Are you sure?" I asked, "I'm leaving soon, and the next time I see Hilda, things are probably going to get serious."

"…I figured," Misty gave me a melancholy smile, bittersweet, "Thinking on it, as much as I enjoy travelling with Ash and Brock, I should have locked you down myself back in Pewter City after our date and asked to travel with you."

She sighed, pushing her head a bit harder against mine, nuzzling her nose against mine, "I thought about it you know?" she told me, "But I chickened out. If I didn't, would it have changed things?"

"Yes," I told her bluntly, not hiding it at all, "Hilda is amazing, but so are you in your own way. If you had travelled with me, I'd have spent a lot of my time trying to get you like this, and then spend every night after that you travelled with me making you moan my name."

She chuckled, "…Just my own fault then for being a chicken," she pursed her lips before smirking, "Alright hot shot, then how about at least for tonight proving that little boast and come back to my room make me moan your name?"

I laughed a bit, hands sliding from her lovely hips to cup her large soft toned ass cheeks, sinking my hands into her doughy cheeks and lifting her into the air by them. She gasped, giggling and long toned legs wrapped around my waist, "I'll do you one better," I grinned staring straight into her eyes as I held her up, "I'll make you scream it, all night long."

# 186: Chapter 185 (R-18)

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

_____________

Of course, there was a single complication in going forward and having fun, that we both quickly realised.

"So how do we get to my room without being seen, with you carrying me like this?" Misty wiggled her ass cheeks in my hands as I held her aloft, legs wrapped around my waist and leaning down to nudge her nose against mine, before planting a quick giggling kiss on my lips and pulling back, giving me a quirked brow.

"Don't worry about that." I grinned back, squeezing her ass cheeks getting another giggle from her. I let go with one hand, shifting her by her ass to hold her in one arm and leaning her a bit more against me.

"You really are stupid strong." Misty near moaned breathily as she used her arms wrapped around my neck to pull herself flush against me, nuzzling her nose against my ear, "One of these days I'll need to learn how you managed that."

"One day," I grinned wider, holding my hand out, my gauntlet shifting from compressed bracelet form to full gauntlet with a flex of my fingers, "Give me Exeggutor."

Rotom, with surprising tact, did not say a thing, and only opened up the slot in my gauntlet, spitting out a gleaming black luxury ball into my palm, and with a single press of the button in the middle, there was a burst of bright light as Exeggutor appeared beside us.

All three heads smiled at me, even the dopey tired one, and looked around, perhaps expecting an enemy pokemon, but just blinked nonplussed…with all three sets of eyes and turned back to me when he saw none.

"Mind teleporting us somewhere?" I asked him, who agreed without issue, and then I looked to Misty, "You fine if he plucks your room location out of your head."

"You could have just let me down you know and we could walk there." she pointed out with a snort.

"Nope." I punctuated my denial my giving the single ass cheek I was holding her up by a harder squeeze.

She rolled her eyes, before shrugging, "Go for it."

I looked to Exeggutor and ordered him to get the location from Misty's mind, and seconds later, we blipped out of the pokemon centre's trading room, and appeared in a simple bedroom. Smaller than most pokemon centre rooms that usually doubled up on bunk beds so four trainers could be crammed into one room in case of overcrowding, and with a single double sized bed in the middle of the room instead of a pair bunk beds.

She totally had planned this in advance huh?

I'd barely thanked Exeggutor and returned the dual grass and psychic type to his pokeball, before Misty was grabbing my by the face and dragging me into a lip bruising kiss.

She moaned into my mouth as I slipped my tongue into her mouth and carried her towards the bed. I pulled back from the kiss as I lay her down on the bed and she looked up at me, cheeks flushed, lips bruised, gorgeous aquamarine eyes clouded with desire.

She bit her lip as I trailed my fingers gently and softly up her hips, "I've never done this before." Misty admitted, panting, chest rising and falling, her deliciously soft breasts rippling with each breath.

Somewhat surprising.

Another virgin? Not that I thought she was a slut or anything, but she had two years on Ash, who started on the same date he did in the original timeline, only five years older.

Not many people actually realised, that Ash didn't start on his tenth birthday, but rather a good nine or so months after his tenth birthday in that timeline. So it was only two or three months until his birthday.

And considering Misty had two years on him by her own admission, that would put her of age with Hilda at eighteen.

Ironically of age with me too considering I was eighteen before I ended up here and got shrunk down into a brat.

….Though perhaps for the better considering this renewed puberty saw me shooting up far quicker in height and build compared to the first.

All thanks to the power of Blissey.

Viva la Blissey.

But anyway, this world of pokemon put a lot of…rewarding on people wanting to settle down and increase the population, and take up pieces of land.

And a girl as gorgeous as her, already being a trainer for two years? With no adult supervision?

It was surprising, if she was anything like a bunch of the girls he'd gone to school with back in his own world, he'd have expected a bevy of boyfriends….

Then again, Hilda had only had the one boyfriend after being an actress for most of her life and going on a journey as well.

You know what? No use thinking on it, I don't care.

Reclining in the sand and watching the sun lazily rise up over the horizon of the ocean, I rolled the gleaming black luxury ball containing my newest…most absurd capture back and forth in my palm as I awaited Lance.

I was honestly, still a little…shocked, I suppose? I don't rightly know that shocked was the right word to describe what I'm feeling, befuddled, full of disbelief?

Discombobulated?

I still didn't feel like it was quite real. That, I just randomly bumped into some kid who dropped an ancient pokeball containing a shiny Rayquaza.

"Rotom, run its data by me again." I ordered absently.

"…This will be the third time Jord," Rotom pointed out, sounding awfully dry for having a mechanical voice, "Rayquaza. Gender: None. Ability: Air Lock. Height: 23'01. Weight: 458 pounds. Attacks Known: Dragon Pulse, Draco Meteor, Air Slash, Twister, Extreme Speed, Dragons Ascent, Ancient Power, Crunch, Flamethrower. All data indicates this pokemon is a juvenile of its species when comparing to sightings of Rayquaza in the ozone layer, and prefers to bombard opponents from afar with long range attacks, a long range combatant."

I pursed my lips, stopping in my rolling of the luxury ball, 'The height and weight line up with the games.' I thought idly. But, compared to the Rayquaza seen in the movies and anime worlds…it was like a third of the size.

Even the other shiny Rayquaza I remember from the Hoopa movie was well over fifty feet long, and that was before mega evolving.

I shook my head…shit.

I've got a shiny Rayquaza. That…just does not compute. That made zero sense. I still find it unbelievable.

I actually don't know what's more unbelievable. That I actually have a shiny Rayquaza in my possession.

Or that it's so weak compared to any other depiction of Rayquaza I'd seen. It got taken out with one shot by Regice.

A half hearted shot at that, Regice usually fired Ice Beam with both arms, but this Rayquaza was downed by just firing from one arm, in one hit.

I laughed, shaking my head, "Shit…" was all I could say, it was just…just absurd!

I leaned back and looked up at the sky, an idle thought coming to mind, "I wonder where I could get my hands on a meteor?" I mused.

Rayquaza and its ability to mega evolve, was different from normal pokemon. Rayquaza had a specific organ, called the Mikado Organ, and it basically functioned as a mega stone.

But, to power it, a Rayquaza had to devour a meteor. How that worked, I have no idea, I just know it does.

So as long as I get my hands on a meteor and feed it to Rayquaza, I'll be able to mega evolve it into Mega Rayquaza.

…Though that will have to wait. Rayquaza didn't need mega evolution to be frank, what it needed more than anything else, was going through a course of Jord's School of Hard Knocks.

Usually I wouldn't deride any pokemon for being weak. I trained a freaking Ratata, Pidgey and even Spearow into monsters.

But it was a freaking Rayquaza. Whoever caught it in that ancient pokeball, must have either ganked it when it was fresh out of the nest and locked it up without ever letting it out.

Or was a massive incompetent as a pokemon trainer. Like so incompetent, he'd make the main timeline anime Ash…from Unova, look like a paragon of genius and unstoppability.

A true juggernaut none could ever hope to match.

Unova Ash!

That was how incompetently bad this things old trainer must have been, if he hadn't keep poor Rayquaza locked up all the time prior to whatever led to things now.

"If I remember, right, doesn't Mikado mean emperor?" I mused, and snorted. What an arrogant name for a freaking organ, giving the ability to mega evolve or not.

I'd say this much. There was nothing emperor like about my Rayquaza. Nor kingly. He was like, a freshly anointed Baron at most!

"Baroooo!" a loud cheerful cry split the air above me, and I looked up just in time to see a massive Dragonite that had to easily be twenty feet tall, spee down from the clouds, a familiar red haired caped figured on its back.

"Store this guy for now." I said to Rotom, holding the luxury ball in my palm, and a moment later, it was sucked inside the red gauntlet and disappeared out of sight.

Just in time as Lance's massive dragonite landed in the sand before me, yet despite the speed it came down at, and its massive height and bulk, it didn't kick up any sand at all.

My eyebrows raised, a little impressed, 'That's some control.' I resisted the urge to whistle. I looked up at the massive beast, that beamed down at me. It was over twice the size of my Dragonite, and its whole vibe was entirely different.

Much more warm and friendly looking.

And vastly more powerful.

This big guy, this was Lance's ace huh? Not the Dragonite me and my time faced before, but his true strongest Dragonite.

"Jord, good morning!" Lance grinned at me from the orange scaled dragons back and slid off, landing behind it and walking around to stand in front of me, beaming as widely as his Dragonite.

I held my gauntlet clad hand out, palm up, "Rotom." I said simply, no need for anything else, he already knew what to do.

One second, the palm of my gauntlet was empty, the second there was a sturdy large egg incubation container with a velvety cushion at the bottom, and sitting atop it?

A large stark grey egg.

"One Aerodactyl egg, that'll be a hundred million please." I cheekily grinned back at him.

Lance's eyes widened as soon as the incubated egg came into view, his jaw dropping and for a moment he just stared, gaping at it.

"Droo." the huge dragonite behind him, warbled and gently pushed him from behind making the champion of both Kanto and Johto take a step closer to me.

He laughed a little shakily, before lifting his hands and gently, slowly, ever so gently took the egg from my grasp, "I can't believe It's really happened." Lance licked lips, before tearing his eyes away from the egg in his grasp, to grin happily at me, "A hundred million is a cheap price to pay for this, thank you Jord, truly."

He lowered the incubated egg down after staring at it a few more moments with awe and disbelief, happiness and more, before pressing a button on the red belt holding up his black and red bodysuit, and in a blink the egg disappeared.

"Huh, you've got storage tech in your belt?" I asked with interest. That, was actually a good idea, I hadn't thought of that.

I prefer my gauntlet though.

"Indeed, useful stuff isn't it?" he eyed my gauntlet knowingly, as he pressed a button on his belt, and pulled out a phone from it a moment later, "Not quite as imaginative as your way of doing things though, a rotom in a gauntlet. I might have to look into such a thing myself."

"I've patented the idea." I jokingly smirked.

His eyebrows rose, "Well, fair enough I suppose, a little greedy but a good idea on your part." Lance nodded approvingly, making me blink.

…Wait, I actually could?

Something to do look into later then when I was travelling between regions of try hard bird back.

He looked me up and down as he typed away on his phone, "You know Jord, you told me you've got quite a lot of shed skin from Dratini and Dragonair right?" he said, before pointing to himself, "You should think about getting some clothing made from it besides the boots, like my bodysuit. I don't want to tell you what to do, but considering you've got involved with legendary pokemon now, the danger has spiked up for you considerably. It would protect your body, the skin is highly resistant to most pokemon attacks and durable enough to block bullets, and or such projectiles."

"Took a lot of bullets have you?" I asked, amused.

"A few," Lance shrugged and casually confirmed, making me blink, "Gun violence used to be a lot more prevalent when I was your age. But even now, you'll still find the odd pirate group who'll fire guns on you alongside attacking you with pokemon."

…Huh.

"Alright, I'll look into it." I took his advice.

Lance nodded in approval, before pressing a button on his phone, "There we go, that's the money transferred to you Jord." he said.

And just as he finished saying so, Rotom beeped, "Confirmation of one hundred million pokedollars being deposited into your account Jord. Current balance: two hundred and thirty million, seven hundred and eighty three thousand, six hundred and eighty seven pokedollars."

I swallowed at the massive number.

It was so beautiful to hear. Was that the language of love? Was it developed in Kalos?

If I meet Serena should I get her to whisper such beautiful numerical worlds into my ears as sweet nothings?

"Thanks, pleasure doing business with you old Lancy boy." I laughed in delight.

Lance snorted, "It was indeed," he nodded, before crossing his arms and raising an eyebrow at me, "So legendary pokemon huh? Regice, Regirock and Registeel. I have to admit, coming into possession of potent psychic power for a time or not, I'm impressed."

I snorted, "If you're impressed by that, wait until you see what I caught earlier, and this time it really was a complete accident and random encounter."

"Something more impressive than three legendary pokemon?" Lance's second red brow joined the first, "Now I'm very curious."

"You'll have to wait to see it on my profile, I'm keeping this guy a secret for now otherwise," I laughed, but…since he was here already, I'd love to battle him again, "How about a battle? You can meet Regice." I cast an excited grin at the towering Dragonite behind him.

"Tempting, oh so tempting," Lance replied, "I would love to test the might of the legendary Regice, not just it, but Regirock and Registeel as well, it would be glorious…but no, as much as I would love to do it. Considering how much commotion we'd cause in such a battle, we'd have people pouring into the beach and crowding around us."

He pointed a finger at me, "When you return to Kanto after your little jaunt to pick up whatever absurd things you foresaw with those psychic powers, I'm going to be waiting for you at the end of the road," he told me, "Claim the rest of the gym badges, crush every other competitor during the Indigo League, friend and foe alike. Fight your way through my Kanto Elite Four, and come before me Jord and challenge me for the championship title of Kanto. That, is what I expect of you, I have high hopes for you after everything I've seen so far."

"You think I can get that far?" I asked, lump forming in my throat for some reason I couldn't quite place.

"Not a doubt," Lance gave a booming boisterous laugh, before giving me a conspirative joking wink, "And honestly, between you and me, being the champion of both Kanto and Johto, and a member the of the G-men? It's so much work. I'd welcome you surpassing me Jord."

"Oh I see how it is, you just want to use me to get more free time to be lazy." I snorted.

"Exactly." he flashed me a thumbs up.

That did remind me of something though.

"On the topic of you being a G-man," I switched the topic, "I was originally holding off because I wanted to capture it for myself. But, after thinking on it, the pokemon I was going to go after would be in no shape to really be a battler."

"Oh?" Lance's eyebrows lowered and the joking atmosphere left him as he turned serious.

"A huge bulbasaur, easily twice the size of a normal one, if not three times the size, living in the sewers of Trovitopolis in the Orange Islands," I revealed, "It's been living down there for thirty or forty years I'm pretty sure, after it was cast away, abandoned and tossed down there by its trainer. Its trainer, is now the current mayor of Trovitopolis."

"…I see…" Lance nodded simply, the skin around his eyes tightening as his eyes narrowed into a glare, "Understood, I'll deal with it."

He cast a glance at me, "Is there anything else like that you've figured out using those psychic powers?"

"A lot," i confirmed, before shrugging, "It was a ton of info though, and it kinda lingers in the back of my head? I'll just suddenly remember stuff I found out sometimes, a lot of it I just subconsciously processed if you get what I mean?"

Lance nodded, "I've heard similar things from other psychics, like Will for instance, one of my Johto Elite Four," he replied, reaching over to pat me on the shoulder, "Well then, if you do happen to remember any other things like what happened with this poor Bulbasaur, make sure to contact me and let me know okay? People like this mayor, need to face justice, treating pokemon like that can never be allowed." he bit out angrily.

"For sure!" I agreed with him totally.

---

---

---

# 190: Chapter 189

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

After parting ways with Lance, my new destination before leaving Kanto altogether was the Bulbasaur's Mysterious Garden.

Luckily, I already knew the rough area it was in.

I let Exeggutor back out of his pokeball, and had him teleport me halfway across Kanto. Specifically, to the forest where I caught him, his overly large friend group of other Exeggutor, and where I mined all my leaf stones.

From there, I opted for speed, and mounted Pidgeot. Soaring through the skies towards Grandpa Canyon, and beyond.

'I should have just done this before.' I mused, high in the air and holding Togepi in my arms, allowing the little guy to take in the sights.

Today, would be his first official battle. While he'd taken part in spars so far with my other pokemon, right from the second day after he'd hatched, he'd yet to fight an official one.

But yes, putting that aside, if I hadn't been dead set on capturing that giant bulbasaur and had a little more common sense, I could have swung this way right after catching Aerodactyl, Kabutops and the other fossil pokemon.

'Oh well.' I sighed, putting it out of mind. No point griping over it. I was in a slightly different place mentally then, before my talk with Surge that put a few things in perspective.

All in all, it didn't actually take me very long to find the vast forest. It actually stood out quite a bit if you knew what you were looking for.

I directed Pidgeot over the forest, speeding across the vast land it covered, taking in the huge dense foliage of trees that left little space to traverse, and anywhere not covered by trees, had what appeared to be gargantuan roots blocking the way in.

'Now that I think about it, back then it never made sense how the Venusaur did that.' I mused. It was never explained either. But, nowadays I had a good idea what those massive roots were.

It could only be Frenzy Plant.

Interesting.

For it to have learned how to do that alone, was quite impressive indeed. It must have been way stronger than I thought it was.

Odd though that Ash's Bulbasaur at the time was easily tanking hits from it and smacking back.

…Maybe it went easy on Bulbasaur?

I shrugged, 'Doesn't matter now I suppose.' I dismissed it, directed Pidgeot deeper and deeper into the forest.

Before finally we came upon an enormous clearing covered in grass land and a wide range of beautiful flowers.

And right smack dab in the middle, was a towering hollowed out tree.

I directed Pidgeot to land down a few dozen feet away from the large hollowed out tree. The residence of the Venusaur that ruled this land.

Once we landed, I hopped down off of Pidgeot's back, carrying Togepi in my arms, and returned the towering try hard bird back to his ever so comfortable and expensive luxury ball.

"Alright little guy," I held the tiny little baby pokemon up and smiled at him, "You ready for this?"

"Toge toge…" Togepi giggled and beamed back, before-

"Togepri!" his little voice deepened, and a vicious thuggish almost evil looking expression appeared on his tiny egg like face. Eyes narrowed into a vicious glare, tiny mouth in a fiendish smirk, his gaze dark and terrible indeed.

And ever so cute.

He even licked his lips in desire for violence!

Just as I taught him.

"That's a lad!" I laughed ruffling his spiky head, and setting him down at my feet. He might have only been a week old, but he had fast learned the basics I'd installed in all my pokemon.

And now in spirit of that-

"Alright Togepi, before we start use Nasty Plot." I ordered.

Togepi grinned up at me, and nodded, "Toge!" he replied, spreading his stubby little arms wide, and a moment later he was enveloped in a crackling red and black aura.

Now he was ready.

"Venusaur, come on out!" I shouted, "I know you're there, and I know you already know we're here as well."

For a moment, there was silence in response to my challenge.

But then-

"ZAAAAAAAAAAAAUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUUURRRRR!" a ground shaking roar erupted and echoed forth from the the large hollowed out tree, and the ground actually began to shake in real, as huge, heavy foot steps echoed.

And a second later, the gigantic form of a Venusaur stepped forth from tree and glared at me.

I whistled, 'Damn, this thing is way bigger than I thought.' I mused absently. It made the Venusaur that appeared in the later series of the anime, seem puny in comparison.

It had to be well over ten feet in height, and length wise, I could only estimate from this distance to be at least three of me in height, probably around twenty feet long?

With a head like the front of a car, and thick powerful muscular legs that were wider than I was.

This thing was a beast!

…Well I wanted a giant bulbasaur, but a giant Venusaur is even better. How did I forget this guy was so fucking massive?

"Let's do this!" I pointed my gauntlet clad fist at the giant grass and poison type, "Togepi, Flamethrower!"

"Toge!" Togepi chirped, before opening his tiny little mouth.

And spitting out a large gout of flame towards the Venusaur. Who in reply, opened its massive maw wide open.

"ZAUR!" it roared, and-

My eyes widened as it spat out a literal fucking massive vertical hurricane of gale force winds towards us.

It was so powerful it tore a massive trench in the grassy ground as it tore towards us, slamming into Togepi's flamethrower and scattering it like flimsy embers.

I rolled to one side avoiding it, while Togepi jumped to the other side. I rolled to my feet, just in time to see it tear through our position and leave a ten foot deep trench.

---

---

---

# 191: Chapter 190

I think I'll address something here. I keep getting a lot of complaints about this story. Well, there's always whiny complainers over every story, especially because my mc's aren't the ching chong slop edgy losers that hide every little facet of their personalities and abilities and have less personality than Plank from Ed Edd n' Eddy.

Specifically though, I want to address the complaints over Ash Ketchum in this story, and how well he's doing.

First, I'll go over, ugh, the scaling of pokemon series. As a rule, I hate doing this shit, cuz power scaling is such virgin behaviour, and I know a bunch of Red fanboys are going to seethe and cry because their precious silent, red-eyed dot dot dot self insert won't be made out as powerful as they want. And there are a lot of you consistently wanking off Red in the comments of this story. Seriously, wash Red's cum from your hands before you type these comments please.

Anyway, there's a hierarchy among the pokemon series strength wise. Of the main series, Power wise, the most powerful pokemon series, is the main anime, followed by the Pokemon Adventure/Specials manga, Then Pokemon Electric Tales of Pikachu, then Pokemon Horizons, then Pokemon Origins and the Games are about on equal level give or take.

To be frank, Ash and Red from the Adventures manga, by time they get their 8th badges respectively, could solo all of Horizons, Origins and and the games without much issue at all. That's how big of a gulf there are, and at those points Ash and Manga Red are far from their peaks.

Now putting that side, let's focus on the pokemon leagues of the anime. In the Adventures Manga, the league isn't even properly made until much later in the series, there isn't even an a proper League Elite Four and Champion when Red gets his badges and it's just an open tournament anyone can compete in. It isn't until post Hoenn if I remember right, proper leagues are established, and it's just the same as the game ones.

Now the anime. It's very different. Putting aside the eight badges, The pokemon leagues are yearly things in each region, at different times for each. Where the best of the best turn up. Anyone of these guys could Solo Origins, Horizons or the games. And to become a champion in the anime, you need to go through a tournament full of these guys. Only when you win this, do you get the chance to take on the Elite Four and then possibly the Champion.

Easier said than done however. The Elite Four, are on a completely different level from normal trainers. Let's just use Flint as an example from Sinnoh. When Ash fought him, it beat Pikachu, Infernape and Buizel without issue. It flat out took Pikachu's Volt Tackle to the chest, and didn't even flinch or move back. This same Pikachu beat Regice remember, commanded by Brandon at that.

If an 8th Gym Leader was say, Saiyan Saga Vegeta level, an Elite Four member would be more akin to first form Frieza from the Saiyan Saga. The gulf is truly that massive. Well, before Sun and Moon Journeys nerfed the fuck out of them. And a champions ace like Cynthia, could outright while immobilzed, face tank the Frenzy Plant of a Torterra at very least, on the 8th gym leader tier of power.

Now, why is this important. Frankly, despite his sheer idiocy at times, and the writer leaks outright admitting they refused to let Ash win and gave him anti-plot armour. Ash was without a doubt, a prodigy.

It's established very early in the anime, that most trainers can ever hope to win a few badges, never mind to make it to the leagues consistently. Just liken Pokemon Battling, to sports of our world, like Football, Basketball etc. Your run of the mill joe ain't gonna go far there. And it ain't like the leagues only have people from one region, no, these people come from all over the world for each league. The best of the best from the entire world turn up at these leagues. And Ash consistently, from his very league, scored incredibly high. Just his first three were top 16, top 8 and top 8 again. And everyone knows, if Ash used his older pokemon in the Hoenn League, he would have won. So at that point, in canon, where he would have been 13 at most, he was already good enough to win a league.

But putting aside main Ash's abilities for a moment. I want to focus more, on the fact that early on, Ash was very catch crazy. He wanted to catch every pokemon he saw. He just, didn't get the chance because of literally, Team Rocket, Misty and Brock always getting in his way. Oh and Samurai that one time.

Seriously, Brock and Misty were horrible fucking friends early on in the series.

Anyway why is this important? Because this was never just a pokemon anime story. It's a fusion of the Pokemon Anime, Electric Tale of Pikachu, and Pokemon I Choose You anime AU-verse. Why is this important? Because the characters are a fusion as well. So Brock and Misty are a lot more mature here.

And this goes double for Ash, if I said main Anime Ash was was a prodigy, then I Choose You Ash is like, a super prodigy or something. That Ash caught only a few pokemon first starting out - Pikachu, Butterfree and Charizard. By time he had four badges, he had a fully evolved Charizard. Not only that, but Pikachu went on to 1v1 Marshadow, then right after, fought Ho-oh. And it's implied at the end, that Pikachu actually won that fight. Even if he didn't though. Four badges in, and this Ash is already capable of raising pokemon to fight high-tier legends like Ho-oh.

So the Ash of this story, is a fusion of his anime self that is much more catch happy with nobody to get in his way this time, the prodigal ability of I Choose You Ash, and the maturity of Electric Tales of Pikachu Ash. And a reminder that, Electric Tales of Pikachu Ash, was literally banging Misty and sent a letter telling his mother all about it, even how he and Misty were banging in a bath. And that's just after the Orange League, when he's about 12. In Electric Tales, he travelled the Orange Islands with Misty, Gary and Ritchie. And even caught an enormous alpha slowpoke, to trade to Gary for a picture of his sister and advice on her. Yes, not only was he banging Misty, Electric Tales Ash wanted to bang Gary's sister as well.

God this drew on way longer than I intended. But yes, that's why the Ash of this story, is doing so well.

Now I'm sure some of you will be thinking. But wait Big Papa Ding Dong Jord, how come Jord could hurt Lance's pokemon then and take two down if Champions are such a big deal. Well, putting aside Poke-Jords pokemon are generally a lot bigger, stronger and more durable than they usually would be at their level because of the intense gravity training they do. Lance and his pokemon were taking it easy on him. Because it was being televised, Lance didn't want to absolutely demolish an up and coming rookie with such promise. And he wanted to see just what all Poke-Jords pokemon were capable of, and if there was anything he could take from it.. If he went full force, none of Poke-Jords pokemon would have gotten much of any hits in, if any.

Keep in mind, Lance and the other champions, really are like Shonen Mc's of their own story, that already completed their main story.

You'll understand in a few chapters I guess.

...And yeah, sorry about the yapping that went on way longer than I intended.

___________

"Ancient Power!" I shouted to Togepi.

Togepi wasted no time, thrusting his little hands towards the huge grass type, dozens of thick rocky boulder materialising in the air around him before shooting towards the huge dual type like a rain of bullets.

The Venusaur scoffed, and from its massive flowery back, tens of dozens of massive vines erupted out and swept through the Ancient Power, pulverizing it into dust.

It cast its gaze at me, before scoffing and slamming those vines into the ground, and throwing itself into the air towards me, where it rolled mid-air, picking up speed and came down like a falling meteor with Body Slam.

This thing did not play around.

"Bad choice, big guy!" I smirked, instead of dodging I thrust my arm up, "Thunder Wave!"

Since it involved me in the battle, it could not complain about me hitting back right?

I saw it's eyes widen above me, as a blindingly fast blue bolt erupted from my Rotom Gauntlet and pierced into it.

It roared as its body spasmed and became paralyzed, leaving it open.

It abruptly stopped mid-air, and my smirk only widened as I look towards Togepi, his little eyes glowing a bright blue with psychic power.

"Togepri!" Togepi cried out, yanking his tiny arms down and the Venusaur caught within his Psychic attack?

Was smashed face first into the ground from dozens of feet in the air, so hard a crater was formed.

"Confuse Ray!" I snapped quickly, opening my palm wide open. Rotom, obeyed instantly, a flare of black energy erupting from my palm and washing over the Venusaur, making it groan.

It shook its head, growling at the wall of the crater it was in, before smashing its face into it with a devastating Headbutt attack.

Probably thinking it was either me or Togepi.

"Now Togepi, Yawn!" I ordered quickly as I rushed over towards the crater and jumped in, slamming an axe kick into Venusaur's huge head.

It barely did anything beyond make it groan in annoyance, but it forced its head deeper into the wall, and kept it even busier in case it possibly snapped out of the confusion quickly.

A moment later, Togepi followed me in, landing atop the flower on Venusaur's back, and yawned.

A bright pink bubble spawned from his mouth, and popped against the Venusaur's back.

"..Saur….?" it teetered on its legs, before yawning itself and slumping to the ground asleep.

"Way to go little guy!" I thrust my hand out up towards the flower, and Togepi happily giggled, meeting me for a high five, before hopping on my shoulder.

"Time to finish this," I smirked, holding my gauntlet clad hand out again, "Rotom, let's hit it with Shadow Ball, full power. And Togepi? You get it with Dream Eater."

"Affirmative!" Rotom replied, screen on the back of my fist flashing, while Togepi chirped in agreement.

"Togepri!" his eyes flashed with dark power as he thrust his little stubby arms out once more, and a translucent copy of himself erupted from his body, and speared straight inside the Venusaur's body.

It groaned in pain, body spasming as the super effective attack enhanced by Nasty Plot devoured it from the inside out.

And following that, a pitch black crackling orb of black power formed from my palm, and was unleashed, slamming into the back of the Venusaur's head and exploding.

The Venusaur groaned again. But to my surprise, it was still quite lively.

So we repeated it. Another volley of Shadow Ball from Rotom, and enhanced Dream Eater from Togepi.

Still it did not fall fully.

Only on the third volley, did the sleeping form of the Venusaur we stood atop, truly go limp.

"What a tough big bastard," I laughed, before turning to Togepi and holding my arms out, "There's a lad, you did amazing!"

"Togepri!" Togepi happily chirped as he jumped into my arms and nuzzled me.

"You little juggernaut you!" I gushed, nuzzling him back.

Celebration aside, I cradled Togepi in the crook of my left arm, before opening my other hand up, "Rotom, luxury ball me."

A moment later, the shrunken form of a gleaming back luxury ball appeared in my palm. I pressed the golden middle of it, enlarging the ball, before kneeling down and tapping it to the back of the huge Venusaur's head.

It cracked open, and a second later, the huge dual grass and poison type, became red energy as it was sucked inside. The ball in my hand, snapping closed as it began to shake in my palm.

I dropped a few feet to the ground, in the middle of the crater as it took. Once, twice, thrice it shook in my palm, before slowing to a stop.

And with a ping, the red light died away as Venusaur was successfully captured.

"Capture complete." I grinned, looking at the ball for a second, "Rotom, what are we working with here? What was the first attack it used against us, it couldn't have been Hurricane, right?"

"Oddly you are incorrect, somehow this Venusaur does indeed know the attack Hurricane. How…peculiar, I was not aware this pokemon line was capable of learning such an attack," Rotom hummed, before regurgitating the data of the pokemon I just caught, "Venusaur. Gender: Male. Ability: Chlorophyll. Height: 12'06. Weight: 848 pounds. Attacks Known: Vine Whip, Body Slam, Razor Leaf, Hurricane, Frenzy Plant, Sunny Day, Solar Beam, Sleep Powder, Poison Powder, Leech Seed, Synthesis, Headbutt, Sludge Bomb, Weather Ball, Grassy Terrain, Earth Power, Ancient Power. Data indicates a fondness and preference for long range combat, but battle data during the encounter and confrontation with this Venusaur, indicates, it has no problem engaging in close combat either using its vast bulk to its advantage against smaller foes."

…Huh.

'Well I suppose Ash's Bulbasaur did learn Whirlwind and Dig somehow.' I mused. Still, that was a massive amount of attacks it knew. This thing, was really powerful.

If it focused fully on Togepi instead of trying to jump me as well, it most likely would have soundly defeated the little guy. But, when it attacked me, it wasn't expecting me to be able to fire back, and that left it open to Togepi. And once it was asleep and confused, it was game over no matter the vast difference in strength.

Still, I couldn't help but grin, "Charizard, Blastoise and now Venusaur, I have them all." I laughed giddily.

All three fully evolved Kanto starters. Truly a milestone if there ever was one.

---

---

---

# 192: Chapter 191

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

I was about to release Pidgeot and leave, had just turned around in fact cradling Togepi in one arm, fingers flexed for Rotom to deposite a pokeball into my palm, when I paused.

A thought occurred, looking over my shoulder at the massive tree my new Venusaur had dwelled within.

…What if?

What if there was a mega stone here? So far, the two I'd found, had appeared in spots where a certain species had congregated in large numbers, or had been there for a long time.

This place counted as both.

I set Togepi atop my head as I turned around fully and made my way over, climbing up the large tree base platform at the front of it, and made my way inside.

It was quite dark, and surprisingly big from what I could see.

"Rotom, light." I said, holding my hand up. A moment later, a torch light lit up from the gauntlet and illuminated the area.

The inside was fully dug out I noted, looking up. Or rather, hollowed out I mused looking up that had been cut away to give more room, up a good thirty feet, and the walls of it, were roughly hewn as if sheared by a blade.

Most likely by Razor Leaf.

Beyond that, there was nothing else much to see beyond a massive bed of leaves, grass and flowers in the centre of the room.

And there, sitting innocently right in the centre of the bed, was a small, glittering spherical gem. It glowed a light teal, while within it, a twisting helix of dark green and red.

I practically felt my eyes light up, "Knew it!" I pumped my fist, jostling Togepi and making him fist my hair to keep balance with a chirping giggle.

Walking over, I picked it up and held it up, "Did you not say these were supposed to be incredibly rare?" Rotom hummed, "As in, people dedicate years just to finding one of them to use, and you have come across three in a month. And each one would be worth a fortune?"

"Yep," I licked my lips, holding the mega stone above my head, admiring the beautiful stone, "There's a bunch of theories where they come from. Ranging from the ancient weapon used in Kalos three thousand years ago and brought about by the legendary pokemon Xerneas blessing evolution stones, or that they came from space, falling as meteors."

"So nobody really knows then?" Rotom snorted, "Just a bunch of silly conjecture?"

Not quite, because I knew both were technically correct. Going by the games. But, taking Rayquaza's mikado organ into consideration, I had a theory.

And as I am the awesometacular Jord of chadly destiny, it is probably correct.

Mega stones were simply rock formations, a lot like evolution stones, that contained excess energy, that when exposed to the aura of a species of pokemon over time, or an absurdly large amount of it in a short time, the power within the stone would be influenced and warp and mutate, becoming a mega stone compatible with that species of pokemon.

"Isn't that what all scientific theories are to start with?" I hummed, having Rotom store it away in the gauntlet storage space, and turning away, hands clasped behind my head as I walked out, hopping off the ledge onto the grass once more.

"I suppose…" Rotom huffed.

I shook my head. They were on the right track either way, and it wasn't really the scientists fault, that they didn't know the specifics of aura, few people did. Most just thought it was something only the likes of a Lucario could manipulate, when that was factually wrong.

…Maybe I'll bring it up when I visit Kalos and meet Professor Sycamore. 'I'm pretty sure he's got a key stone and a Charizardite Y that he gave to that Trevor kid.' I mused.

Nothing else made sense on how he got it. Granted, how Sawyer got a Sceptilite didn't make sense either. He just turned up randomly with it. Even Brock and Misty just randomly had them, even though they were both utter scrubs in the main timeline.

Though I had a good idea where I could find one.

Well, whatever, it was time to go now.

'Goodbye Kanto, I'll see you later.' I smirked as I had Pidgeot's luxury ball deposited into my palm and released him, climbing onto his back, and taking off into the air moments later.

Leaving Kanto behind, for the foreseeable future.

I'll be back though.

My sweet ass pad is being built in Kanto after all.

_________

There was a cheerful hum on Lance's lips as he entered his office. In his hands, he cradled the incubation pod, containing the grey egg of a pokemon he'd dreamed about for so long.

'Jord really should learn to haggle better, I'd have given ten times that for one of these, if it came to it.' Lance thought as he set it on his desk, running his hand gently over the top of the incubation pod, almost as if it would break at the slightest touch.

Even though he knew pokemon eggs were far from that fragile, he'd seen dragon types knocking their eggs off of twenty foot drops onto pure ground and they'd been absolutely fine.

"Good thing he didn't though, I'd have been cleared out." Lance laughed a little, shaking his head as he let go of the pod and collapsed onto his huge, plush comfy office chair.

Well, not quite, but it would have drained a massive chunk of his funds. Last he checked, his savings were just a little over three billion currently.

He felt a little guilty to be honest, but he comforted himself with the thought that Jord was vastly more talented and richer now than he was at his age.

"Which reminds me," Lance swirled around in his chair to face the multitude of computer screens on his desk, "Jord did say he caught something earlier I wouldn't believe didn't he?"

That made him curious. The boy had literally caught three legendary pokemon, and he believed that.

What could he have captured that truly that shocking.

Booting up his computer, it wasn't long before he was bringing up Jord's specific trainer page profile.

He scrolled down to his latest catch and blinked, "Huh a Venusaur?" he noted the latest capture.

Well, he did say he knew where a Venusaur was now that he'd given up his plans for that Bulbasaur he told him about 'Which reminds me, I'm going to the orange islands after I have some breakfast.' Lance nodded to himself.

He had a pokemon abusing cunt of a mayor to deal with.

Still, that was incredibly quick of him, they hadn't parted ways that long ago. shaking his head, Lance brought up Jord's second latest capture, the one he'd have been talking about then.

The picture of a towering serpentine black scaled dragon pokemon stared back at him.

Lance blinked, "…Eh?" he uttered, head tilting to the side.

No…that couldn't be right.

He rubbed his eyes and looked again.

"No…it's still there." Lance licked his lips, nodding to himself, "That really is a Rayquaza…a black one."

Alternative coloured pokemon were not unheard of, while they weren't exactly common, they popped up here and there, scientists were of the opinion that every one in ten thousand or so pokemon had the chance of being born with an alternate colour.

And Jord now had one of those pokemon.

A Rayquaza at that.

Lance nodded to himself, "Well…that sure is something." he mused.

Then his eyes rolled up into the back of his head, and he passed out from sheer shock.

____________

"Looking at this, it should only be about another hour and a half, maybe two hours before we get there." I mused, looking down at the map displayed on Rotom's screen.

High above the clouds, riding atop Pidgeot's back, we'd already bypassed the island where I caught my Blastoise, and all my squirtle and wartortle, and further, passing by Cinnabar Island a good fifteen minutes ago.

We were out in the vast open ocean waters between regions now. Generally speaking, beyond heading towards the Orange Islands, the only land between me and it now, were some of the Delacour Islands and some completely un-inhabited islands nobody had documented anything about in decades.

'Hmm…' I pressed two fingers on the screen and used them to pull down on the map, zooming in on one of the last Delacour islands.

Specifically the one where I knew a shiny Druddigon resided on. One that would eventually be captured by Lance's cousin Claire.

Tempting.

But….no.

The only real draw of it for me, was that it was shiny. And, I'd already decided to try and distance myself a bit from going manic with catching and collecting pokemon.

I'd come to love it regardless, but it wasn't fair to Druddigon itself that I only wanted it because it was different colored from a normal Druddigon.

I'm trying to be a better guy.

….Hypocritical I know considering I'm going after an Onix because it was made of crystal. But, I'd made up my mind to catch that guy way back.

Plus, an onix made up entirely of crystal, is entirely different from a pokemon simply being a different colour. The thing may as well have been an entirely different species of pokemon considering what I assumed of its typing and abilities.

Abruptly, Pidgeot let out a sharp cry and flared his wings, jolting me out of my thoughts. I tightened my grip and made sure Togepi was secure just as a blinding flash lit up the horizon, followed by a thunderous explosion that shook the air around us.

We were miles out from it, but I could still feel it. The blast wave rolled over the sea, and then came the aftermath: a colossal plume of black smoke billowing into the sky, thick and fast, swallowing the island beneath it until it all but vanished behind the swirling dark cloud.

"What…the fuck?" I gaped, arm tight around Togepi cradling the little guy into my chest as he chirped excitedly at the sight of it.

I am totally a bad influence.

"Return little man," I returned the baby pokemon to his luxury ball and stowed it away in my gauntlet storage space, before tightening my grip with both hands now on Pidgeot's saddle, "Pidgeot, go let's check that out."

"Geot!" Pidgeot barked with a nod, spreading his wings wide and levelling out once more, before with a powerful beat of his wings that resounded through the air, he took off again, speeding up much faster than his travel speed and soaring towards the island.

It was but seconds before we arrived, Pidgeot circling around the massive cloud of smoke that blotted out the island below us, keeping just out of range of it so we didn't breathe it in.

As he circled the island though, bit by bit I began to make out what seemed to be destroyed remnants of a huge building, waves of fire flickering through the smoke and all around at the bottom.

Something caught my way, a figure.

A person?

"Pidgeot, blow some of this smoke away." I ordered quickly, whoever it was couldn't be left down there for long.

Their lungs were probably already being cooked by that smoke as it was.

"Pidgeot!" he reared back at my order, and began to rapidly beat his wings, unleashing a devastatingly massive gust attack that unleashed a small tornado and blew away much of the smoke, dispersing it off into the distance.

And the figure came into view fully.

And my jaw dropped as a tall, grey humanoid pokemon with a long purple tail looked up at me, an expressionless look upon its face.

Oh….oh shit.

'No, no no!' my eyes widened this couldn't be happening! A hundred and one emotions blazed through me in an instant. Shock, disbelief, awe, excitement, terror. So many I couldn't keep track of them.

….Mewtwo.

___________

Poke-Jord: "I'm in danger."

---

---

# 193: Chapter 192

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

I held Mewtwo's gaze, breathe caught in my chest. I don't know how long it lasted, but..

It was only broken when Mewtwo raised his arms and with a simple sweep of them, the massive inferno raging around him was quelled.

"Behold human, my psychic powers!" Mewtwo declared straight up at me, all but ignoring Pidgeot, "I am the strongest pokemon in the world! Even more powerful than Mew!"

I suppose it was supposed to sound very grand. Intimidating. A declaration of absolute, unquestionable power.

Yet-

"Which one?" I replied, gesturing for Pidgeot to lower down, to land in front of the pokemon. My starter and partner did so, completely unbothered by Mewtwo's showing.

To him after all, he had no idea just what kind of beast of a pokemon Mewtwo truly was.

Though, even as I slid out of the saddle atop Pidgeot's back to face Mewtwo, my mind was racing, 'What the fuck am I doing?!' I panicked, and cursed myself for my big mouth.

I should be running for the hills. Bringing out Exeggutor and teleporting the fuck away, as far away as possible.

Yet instead, I'm standing here facing Mewtwo of all fucking pokemon!?

"…What?" Mewtwo narrowed deep blue eyes at me, head cocking slightly to the side, "What do you mean?"

"You said you're stronger than Mew," I forced myself to raise a brow questioningly, forcing all terror and panic deep beneath a veneer of casual confidence, "What one? I know of at least three.

Mewtwo blinked ever so slowly, and was silent. I had to resist the urge to smirk at catching him off guard. "I…I am stronger than all of them." the clone finally responded.

"Most likely," I nodded, agreeing, "Out of all the Mew's I know about, only one of them is a good fighter."

"Then…it is true then, I am the strongest." Mewtwo concluded.

"Eh," I shrugged, and decided to go for broke, "No you're not. Don't get me wrong. Your absurdly powerful. One of the strongest pokemon ever for sure. But, far from the strongest."

Mewtwo's eyes narrowed, "You dare, human? You have the gall to question my might?"

"No, I just told you, you're definitely one of the strongest," I grinned at him and 'casually' walked towards him, doing my level best to hide any tremors of fear, "But….You're not as strong as Dialga, Palkia or Giratina. And you're definitely not as strong as Arceus."

Mewtwo cocked his head to the other side, "I do not understand. Those scientists claimed Mew was the rarest and most powerful pokemon. Yet, what are Dailga, Palkia and Giratina? What is an Arceus?"

"They're gods," I said simply, "Arceus, is the first ever pokemon. The original one that created this world, this very universe. It created Dialga the god of time, Palkia the god of space and Giratina the god of anti-matter to give form and oversee this world."

Mewtwo reared back as if I struck him, "I….."

I pounced on that sign of weakness, "And beyond that, there is the likes of the original Rayquaza, and Groudon and Kyogre. Not to mention Zygarde," I added, "They're all legendary pokemon that are stronger than you. Well, I'd say you could give Groudon or Kyogre a run for their money. At least until they used Primal Reversion and entered their more powerful Primal forms, then you'd be vastly overpowered."

"I…I…" Mewtwo started and stopped, "I do not understand. It is my destiny to be the strongest, is it not? Then...how…"

"It's not your destiny," I said bluntly, crossing my arms, "You don't have a destiny."

As soon as the words left my lips, the air pulsed. Blue light flashed, and I found myself forced to my knees, as if the very sky itself was pressing down on me.

"GEOT!?" Pidgeot barked angrily, flaring his wings wide and preparing to fight, before I held up my hand to stop him.

"You mock me human?" Mewtwo all but hissed, towering over me, eyes glowing a bright blue with power, "You speak nonsense to confuse me. No doubt, you cannot stand it. A clone with such a destiny! I tell you human, I may be a clone, but what is my purpose then, if not to be the most powerful!"

I grit my teeth and forced my neck up against the weight to look up into Mewtwo's eyes, before clenching my fists and pushing up against the weight forcing me down. My legs trembled, but slowly I rose against the psychic power holding me down, "You don't have one," I repeated, lifting a trembling hand to point straight between his eyes, "Beyond a few legendary pokemon, nobody does!"

All of a sudden, the power pressing down on me sputtered and died. Mewtwo's arms fell limp at his side and he stared at me wide eyed, "Then…what is the point of me?"

I palmed my forehead, "Fucking scientist morons," I huffed. Then I did the crazy. I spread my arms wide and wrapped them around the much taller cloned pokemon, hugging him, "You've had it rough, huh?"

Mewtwo stiffened, "Unhand me human! Do you think your feeble strength could harm me?"

"It's just a hug you moron," I squeezed my arms slightly around him, "I'm trying to comfort you here."

"…I….why?" Mewtwo gave the most confused look I'd seen in my life. Which was quite the grand feat considering I'd looked at myself in the mirror when trying to remembering something with my Goldeen memory.

"Cuz you clearly need it," I said. I stayed like that for a moment more, before letting go and sighing, running a hand through my hair, "You've practically just been born. It's no wonder you're so lost and confused despite all your power and intelligence."

"I am a clone, I was not birthed," Mewtwo instantly replied, yet stepped back from me, almost as if scared. Which would be hilarious, to have Mewtwo scared of me, if it were not so sad, "I was cloned from the DNA of Mew."

"And?" I crossed my arms and raised my brow challengingly, "It shows how little you know of reproduction. You might have come from a test tube and not womb or an egg, but you were born into this world like anyone else. You realise, everyone is a clone practically? The genes of the parents are what are used at the basis to create life. You're not some copy or fake just because you were born a different way. You're simply the child of Mew and human science."

---

---
