# 194: Chapter 193

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

___________

Mewtwo just stared at me, silent. Yet, I watched as his eyes moved from side to side, as if avoiding my gaze.

He was clearly confused, and uncomfortable.

And I don't blame him.

I sighed, rubbing my face in agitation, "…You know, I so wasn't expecting to meet you today," I huffed, collapsing onto my ass and staring up at the sky, "Just because there's some pokemon stronger than you, doesn't mean you're not one of the most powerful beings in existence. You're way above my pay grade, but I suppose it's better you hear this from me before you get found by the idiots behind your creation in the first place."

"What do you mean? I killed the scientists." Mewtwo replied bluntly.

I resisted the urge to wince.

"They were the ones that made you, but they were put up to it by a guy called Giovanni," I shrugged and informed him, "He's the leader of a group called Team Rocket. Their goal is to steal rare and powerful pokemon and conquer the world. Giovanni had them make you, to be his weapon to do and accomplish all that."

Mewtwo's eyes flashed with disdain and anger, pulsing with bright blue light once more, "You are saying…my actual purpose…is a slave and weapon for this giovanni?"

"No, I already told you, you don't have a purpose," I rolled my eyes, "Just because a parent has a child for some stupid goal, doesn't mean they have to agree with it. The likes of Dialga was born connected to time itself, an inborn purpose. But you, just like me, weren't born with one ingrained in your very existence."

"I…see…" Mewtwo hummed quietly, and followed my gaze to stare up at the sky, "How…does one attain a purpose then?"

I shrugged again, "You live," I answered simply, "It really isn't all that complicated."

"Live?" Mewtwo tasted the words, echoing them, yet looked as confused as ever, "I don't understand, aren't I already living then?"

"You're alive, that's different from living," I replied, "Live your life. There's a whole wide world out there. Go out and learn. Find something you enjoy. Find something you want to accomplish, a dream and that will become your purpose. You find your own purpose."

"What do you mean?" Mewtwo cocked his head to the side once more in interest, paying rapt attention now.

"Maybe you'll find yourself enjoying cooking and want to be the best pokemon chef ever. Or maybe, you'll find you actually want to be the strongest pokemon, so you'll pursue strength and make it your purpose to surpass Arceus and be the strongest," I mused, "It could be anything."

"…I see, no, I do not, but I think I understand what you are trying to say, somewhat," Mewtwo crossed his arms and peered down at me, "What of you human? Do you have a purpose?"

My lips quirked up and I grinned, "Sure," I replied leaning my chin on my fist as I looked up at him, "I've got a bunch."

"A bunch…" Mewtwo trailed off, "You, have multiple purposes?" his eyes widened in what might have been shock.

I couldn't quite tell with this guy. But, I know I surprised him at least.

"It is possible to have more than one?" Mewtwo asked after a moment.

"You can have as many as you want," I laughed, pushing up to my feet and patting him on the chest, and my grin widened a bit as he did not stiffen at my touch, "I'm going to become the strongest pokemon trainer in the world. I'm going to master how to use aura. I'm going to marry a girl called Hilda. I'm going to become a champion of an entire region. I'm going to overcome my human life span. I'm going to help a few of my pokemon get over their issues. I'm going to get super rich and wealthy. I'm going to be the physically strongest human in the entire world. And no doubt, I'll come across more I want to do in the future after all. The world is a big place after all."

"….Amazing…" Mewtwo uttered, staring at me.

"I like to think I am," I flashed him a thumbs up humbly, "But just like for me, there's an entire world out there for you to explore and find your own purpose or purposes. You can do anything you want to do."

"I see…." Mewtwo nodded slightly, "Yet…I do not know where to start. You have proven it, I know so little of this world."

"That's the same for me, and everyone really. There's so much in this world, it's impossible to know it all," I laughed, "But, if you want some advice. In your place, I'd work on learning to control your power. Your absurdly powerful, but you've got no experience with it. You'll need to learn how to hone it, so you don't end up accidentally destroying everything around you. It wouldn't do for you to find something you truly love and want, only for your own awesome power to rip it apart by mistake."

"Understood," Mewtwo nodded, "But, how do I go about that?"

"I can give you some advice if you want." I offered, "I've got a few psychic pokemon like you I'm training, so I can definitely give you some tips on it."

"Ah yes, you did say you wanted to become the most powerful pokemon trainer," Mewtwo nodded slightly, "Then, would it be possible for me to accompany you and partake in your knowledge and experience as a trainer?"

…Eh?

---

---

---

# 195: Chapter 194 (Season 1: End)

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

Alright lads, with this chapter we've officially reached the point of where Season 1 of this story ends, and going forward will be a time skip. At the end of the chapter, there will be a list of the level equivalent Poke-Jord's pokemon are at, and an image of him with all the pokemon to give an overall look at what it looks like.

___________

I stared at Mewtwo.

He stared back.

"Come again?" I asked, sticking my pinkie in my ear and wiggling it about, to dislodge whatever was clearly blocking my hearing. Because…no fucking way! "I don't think I heard that right. Because….there's no way."

"What do you mean?" Mewtwo frowned, "Do you disagree and not want to agree to my request."

"No, not that," I waved his words off, pulling my pinkie from my ear, and lamenting that nothing was on it at all, "It's just, I have to be hearing wrong. There's no way you just offered to be my pokemon."

"Why not?" Mewtwo somehow frowned even more, "Is there a reason I cannot? I was under the impression, that matter of my birth or not, I am still a pokemon, and you are a pokemon trainer."

"No, the logic works," I agreed, and was a little pleased at the sudden shift in how he referred to his birth and not being a clone, "But, like I said, you really are one of the most powerful pokemon in the world. If I capture you, and you become my pokemon. As soon as I help you learn to control your power, I'll already be the strongest pokemon trainer in the world. There's no pokemon beyond the top tier legendary pokemon I mentioned before, that are more powerful than you."

Or well, close to there at least. Maybe an absurd guy like Leon could manage to beat Mewtwo, but I'd still be instantly thrust to the top regardless.

"Is that not a good thing?" Mewtwo cocked his head to the side again, he had a habit of that I noticed, looking confused.

"Yes, and no," I sighed, "It's amazing. But, it takes the fun out of it. You'll find out soon with how powerful you are Mewtwo, so let me tell you it straight. When something is so easy you can accomplish it without effort, there's nothing to be proud of. It's not an accomplishment. It's boring."

"I see," Mewtwo nodded slowly, reaching up to cup his chin, "It is akin to breathing, then? That works as a metaphor does it not? For why would you be proud of being able to breathe? Yes, I understand."

And there was the arrogance still that came from knowing just how absurdly overpowered he truly was.

He was not wrong though.

"You're not wrong," I clicked my tongue, before shrugging, "Well whatever. It's just an example. You're that amazing of a pokemon, anyone you followed would instantly have the ability to become a champion if they used you alone to battle. So you just offering to become my pokemon, is quite shocking."

"Ah, understandable," Mewtwo mused, "So you are not opposed to training me then?"

"Not a single bit," I admitted with a laugh, "If you want to become my pokemon? I'll happily capture you. Though eventually, it'll get out your with me, and we'll need to deal with Giovanni and Team Rocket."

That, was going to be a pain in the ass. But whatever, I was going to end up having to deal with them at some point.

Lest I end up dealing with something crazy in the future, like Neo Rainbow Rocket or something.

…Actually, considering they came from a different dimension, even if I nipped this worlds Team Rocket in the bud, I might just have to deal with them anyway. And that Giovanni had a mega evolving Mewtwo of his own.

You know what?

Yeah okay, I am definitely not refusing.

"I do not fear this fool Giovanni. You said he needed me. Thus without me, he is no threat," Mewtwo waved my words off, "As it is, I ask of you human. Train me, teach me, and clear my ignorance of this world. Help me find my purpose in life."

"Alright then Mewtwo," I nodded, and held my hand out. Without a word from me, Rotom opened the slot on the palm of my gauntlet and deposited an empty gleaming black and gold luxury ball into my palm. I enlarged it and held it out towards Mewtwo, "My name is Jord Odhar, glad to have you along for the ride then Mewtwo."

"A pleasure, Jord Odhar." Mewtwo inclined his head, before reaching out and touching one of his nubbed spherical fingers to the luxury ball. It popped open, and red light enveloped the legendary psychic pokemon.

A moment later, he was broken down by that red light and sucked inside the luxury ball. It clacked, echoingly as it shut a moment later and the golden middle lit up a crimson red.

….Doot…..Doot…..

It shook slightly back and forth in my palm, before-

Doot….!

It pinged loudly as it stopped shaking, the red light fading as the capture was completed.

And I just stared at the gleaming black expensive luxury ball.

Mewtwo…was mine.

'What the absolute fuck just happened?' I couldn't help but gape in disbelief at the ball.

No seriously.

What the fuck!?

For a long, long…very long minute or however long it was, I just stood there and stared at the luxury ball within my palm.

Did not compute.

'I have Mewtwo.' I thought blankly. Like, there were many pokemon out there I foresaw myself capturing, and even some legendaries hadn't been out of the realm of possibility.

Like, Regice, Regirock and Registeel were picture proof of that. But, there was a world of difference between those three, and Mewtwo.

Mewtwo, when having mastered his abilities was capable of creating region wide storms. While reclining in a chair.

With a single twirl of his hand.

It wasn't quite Kyogre's ability to create a planet wide rain storm, or in Groudon's case a drought. But, it was beyond absurd regardless.

Mewtwo wasn't like the legendaries the likes of Tobias captured, that while supremely overpowered, could still be brought down by a powerful elite tier trainer.

Mewtwo, was pretty much a god. Or at the very least, was bordering on the levels of the actual godly pokemon. Like the original Kyogre and Groudon. The first Rayquaza who protected the planet. Zygarde who drew power from all of nature the world over. Yveltal and Xerneas the literal personifications of life and death. He could probably even put up a small fight against one of Arceus' shards.

Because, there truly only was one Arceus. One that existed at the very centre of the Pokemon Multiverse. And each Arceus saw throughout the series, like the anime, movies, games and manga and such, were all just lesser clones whom the original was connected to and saw through. Lesser fragments or not though, one of them was capable of taking on Dialga, Palkia and Giratina all at the same time and dominating.

I hadn't even considered capturing any of them, or Mewtwo for that matter. I was content to leave those kinds of beastly powers up to Ash.

….Yet here I am.

"Rotom….can you read Mewtwo's data from the pokeball?" I finally spoke up after who knows how long.

"….Somewhat…" Rotom replied after a moment, "I can read the basic biometric information, and determine Mewtwo's height, weight and known attacks. But, all pokedex data beyond that comes up as an error, even the name. I cannot even determine a gender."

I frowned, thinking. I stayed silent for a few moments as I pondered on that information. After a minute, I nodded, "Well, he is a totally new pokemon so no pokedex will have information on him," I said, "Let's keep it that way Rotom. Don't add anything to Mewtwo's data. It's better that way, the less who know about Mewtwo the better, even if only Lance has access to my data."

"Affirmative, albeit disappointing," Rotom made a clicking sound, as if tutting a tongue in annoyance, "Alas it seems, I will not be known as the Rotom that documented an all new breed of legendary pokemon and have my name go down in history."

"You're gonna be famous enough in the future, you're the personal aid, partner and trusted weapon of the future World Monarch," I snorted, "Anway, let's hear the data you have on Mewtwo then."

"Hmph, very well," Rotom's eyes at the back of the gauntlet rolled in exasperation, "Mewtwo. Gender: Unknown. Ability: Pressure. Height: 6'07. Weight: 271 pounds. Attacks Known: Confusion, Psybeam, Psychic, Barrier, Light Screen, Reflect, Recover, Hyper Beam, Swift, Counter, Shadow Ball, Disable, Psywave, Hypnosis, Teleport. Data indicates a leaning towards long range combat, but considering the subject Mewtwo is a newly born pokemon, this is inconclusive."

Quite a large starting point of attacks for a guy who basically just came out of the womb a half hour ago at most. But, as expected of Mewtwo I guess.

…No physical attacks though, barring maybe counter depending on how it was used. 'Definitely something to work on then.' I nodded.

I was always more of a Mega Mewtwo X kind of guy than Mega Mewtwo Y. 'Not to mention, Mewtwo gets access to Aura Sphere as well, meaning Mewtwo can learn to harness aura.' I mused.

In that case, I could probably look into aura myself through training with Mewtwo.

I shook my head, that was something to think on later. I raised my hand, and let Mewtwo out of the luxury ball.

In a burst of light, Mewtwo appeared before me shaking his head, "That…was interesting," he commented, "So that is a pokeball then?"

"A luxury ball actually," I corrected, spinning the expensive black pokeball atop my finger, "A more expensive version of pokeball. They're much more comfortable for pokemon."

"I see, well it was rather pleasant I do admit," Mewtwo nodded, "Albeit, I did not have long to explore it before you brought me back out. Is there a reason for that?"

"No," I shook my head, "I was just checking to make sure you were okay in it." it would not do to have a pokemon like him cramped and resentful.

Mewtwo tilted his head, before his lips quirked, "Would you have done so with any other pokemon?" he asked, and I raised a brow, because yes I would, albeit not to see if they were cramped in the ball or something though, "Do not feel the need to give me special treatment beyond the other pokemon. Matter of my birth aside, by your own words, I am a pokemon like any other, so treat me as you would any other pokemon."

I sighed, and ran a hand through my hair, "Alright then, I'll do that," I said, before nodding my head behind him at the ruins of the massive laboratory he had torn apart, "Before we get a move on though and leave. We need to completely destroy the rest of this lab and leave no trace. Otherwise, they'll just use the remaining data to create another Mewtwo."

"Yes, that would be the prudent thing to do."

I grinned, "Alright then, let's take this chance for me to get a look at what you're capable of," I said, pointed at the lab, "Let's see your Hyper Beam attack."

"Very well, let me show you my power." Mewtwo grinned and turned around facing towards the lab and brought bought hands up, spreading them wide, twin orbs of crackling black power erupting into existence. My eyes went wide as a massive pressure spread over the island, sparks of black power like bolts of lightning leaping from the spheres, tearing great chunks out of the ground where they struck, and the entire island began to shake. Then Mewtwo brought both palms together, heels of his palms touching together, while his nub like fingers splayed outwards, and merged both spheres of black power together into a much larger one.

Wait-

Something about that pose seemed dreadfully familiar….!

_________

"…Well that's one way to do it." I mused blankly.

High in the air atop Pidgeot's back, I stared down below at what would have been New Island in another world.

Or lack thereof.

It was just gone.

The entirety of the island, was nowhere to be seen. The titanic black Hyper Beam Mewtwo had unleashed. Hadn't just destroyed the remnants of the lab.

It had obliterated much of the island, and the rest had sunk deep into the ocean.

Mewtwo floating in the air beside us, a blue glow around his body, actually looked a bit sheepish as he stared down at roiling waves below, "…I see what you mean now by needing to control my power."

"…You think?" I deadpan snorted, rolling my eyes, "Well whatever, we're done here now at least."

It was time to head for Sunburst Island. Then the island of giant pokemon. And after that, it was time to head for an entirely new region.

_______________

# 198: Chapter 195 (Season 2 Start)

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

_______

Calem!"

Serena barely held back the annoyed scowl from twisting her lips as she banged her childhood friends bedroom door with one hand, while balancing a tray of food precariously in the other, "Open up already, don't make me use my key!" she demanded.

Silence.

No response at all.

She couldn't stop the scowl now, reaching into the back pocket of her skirt to dig out the key. She was already sore and tired from her mother making her train all morning riding their Rhyhorn, she was so not in the mood for this today!

The tray wobbled in her grip, but reflexes born from doing this damn near every day for years at this point, just barely allowed her to keep it upright and not spill all the food everywhere.

Finally she pulled the key free and jammed it in the bedroom door lock and twisted it open. A rank smell hit her first as she stepped into a dark, gloomy room.

The musty smell of a room that hadn't had the windows opened in god knows how long.

Her eyes went to her childhood friend Calem sitting in the middle of the room, knees drawn to his chin as he stared blankly at the wall.

"Seriously?" Serena rolled her eyes and stepped over a mess of covers, empty bottles and one crusty slipper, "When was the last time you showered?"

Walking over, she placed the tray in front of him on the floor, before walking over and yanking the curtains open letting some sunlight in.

Calem hissed looking away, "Can you not do that?" he grumbled in annoyance.

"Don't be such a baby," Serena groaned in annoyance as she opened the window, "And you didn't answer my question, when did you last shower?"

She'd been away for a week. She'd wanted to try her hand at becoming a sky trainer, it had seemed so interesting.

…But, while she'd spent a week at the sky trainer academy, it hadn't amounted to much. It was decently fun, but not something she'd found wanting to dedicate her life to.

Her mother was out of town as well as a judge for a rhyhorn race and only got back last night. And Calem's parents as always were away on business.

So he'd been left to himself a week.

'Not that an eighteen year old should need to be supervised just to take a shower.' she huffed internally, turning towards him and arching a brow.

"…The night you left." Calem said under his breath, looking away.

She took a deep, steady breath and released. She wasn't the type that liked to hit people…but god, she just wanted to clobber this idiot NEET.

"Eat your breakfast, and then go take a shower," Serena settled for ordering him, eyes narrowed, "I'm going to meet up with Shauna and the guys. If I come back and you haven't showered, I'm getting Tierno to throw you in, and he'll join you in it as well."

Calem shivered, head whipping to her, "Fine!" he relented, and huffed himself, "You're so annoyingly bossy."

"Trust me, I wish I didn't need to but, if I don't, within a month you'd give a Grimer a run for its money in smell." Serena fired back, glaring.

Before her shoulders slumped and she just gave up, "…Just, stop being so difficult, what are you gonna do when I eventually find something I wanna do and leave this town?" Serena said, making her way towards the door.

He didn't reply to that.

At least until she got to the door, only then did he speak up, "…You look good today by the way." Calem said.

She looked over her shoulder, to see Calem's eyes settled on her lower back, specifically her short red waist high skirt, before quickly looking away, "I always look good." the honey blonde replied with a laugh, and left his bedroom behind.

She put the bad mood he put her in as always, out of mind. Today after all, was going to be exciting. Vaniville Town was ripe with excitement today. Professor Sycamore himself had come, and was hosting a pokemon battle tournament!

She left Calem's house behind, out into the sunny morning and made her through the streets to the small fountain in the middle of town where she was to meet her friends.

Vaniville was a lovely, gorgeous town. But it wasn't exactly big, so it didn't actually take her long.

Her friends were already there. Trevor was sitting at the fountain bench, engrossed in his pokedex, while Shauna sat beside him kicking her legs back and forth.

The lumbering bulky Tierno, was spinning in front of them like a spinning top that could give a Hitmontop a run for its money as he danced.

He did love doing that.

Shauna noticed her first, "Serena, over here!" the short, mocha skinned girl grinned and waved her over.

The mocha skinned girl hopped up as Serena approached, doing the impossible and drawing Trevor from his pokedex as her large breasts bounced in the motion. Clad in only a tiny pair of jean shorts, and an unzipped pink hoodie with a black crop top underneath, she was definitely feeling the heat.

And Trevor's crush was plain for anyone who paid attention.

"Hey." Serena grinned back as she reached her, her much smaller friend sweeping her up into a tight hug.

Or well, would have if her head came above her breasts. As it was, Shauna was an inch or two shy of five feet, where as Serena was five-nine.

"Can you believe it, an actual tournament happening here in Vaniville!" Shauna squealed in excitement, "It's been years since something this interesting happened here."

She snorted, "That's for sure." Serena agreed. As beautiful as Vaniville was, it was a bit boring. It was a small place without much to attract people beyond its inherent countryside beauty.

"Serena!" Tierno spun over, rolling to a stop spread out laying on his side, propping his hand on his chin and winked up at her, "Glad you're back, how was the sky trainer school? Find what you wanted?"

She stepped back a step, smoothing her skirt. She'd long since shot Tierno down for his crush on her, but he was bold and flirty as heck, and wasn't shy about checking her out. Though, despite that he was a good friend, "It was fun, but not really for me."

---

# 199: Chapter 196

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

And with this, we hit 200 chapters on here more or less.

_______

"Ah I'm sorry Serena," Shauna winced, patting her side comfortingly, "You'll find what you wanna do, don't worry!" she assured her.

'Will I?' Serena couldn't help but think She'd tried to many things. Rhyhorn racing, gardening, part time work, art, soccer, she'd even tried her hand at becoming a nurse. But, nothing had ever sparked a desire for her to pursue.

And now even being a sky trainer had flopped too.

A shame too since she could have used their family Fletchling. And even worse, it was cementing more to her mother, that she was 'destined' to become a Rhyhorn racer.

She'd ever transferred the ownership of their family Rhyhorn to her. Without asking her opinion on it either.

She wasn't even a registered trainer…..technically.

She'd passed the exam for it ages ago, she'd just never actually pursued the route of a proper trainer, or even picked a starter pokemon yet.

'Here I am, eighteen and still no idea what I want to do with my life.' Serena bit back a sigh, eyeing her friend in front of her. She was the oldest of her friend group, a whole two years ahead of Shauna, Trevor and Tierno.

…Well not counting Calem who was a couple months older than her and would be turning nineteen soon.

"Hopefully," she forced a smile on her face for Shauna, "Actually, you texted me while I was at that school you said you had found out what you wanted to do right, when you left on your journey?"

Shauna, Tierno and Trevor had got their starter pokemon together a year ago. And planned on leaving on a journey together. They'd basically hung back an entire year…because of well, her and Calem.

Trying to convince her to go with them, and help with Calem. Her hand went to her skirt pocket, fingering one of the shrunken pokeballs within it.

Another pokemon forcibly transferred to her without her consent. Khangaskhan. Calem's pokemon.

Originally at least.

…Well, it was technically his parents pokemon on paper, but they'd bought it for him as a child, and he'd went on to win a tournament with it even. Back then, he'd seemed so cool and strong.

His name was on everyone's lips. A child prodigy people called him. Claimed he was a champion in the making.

Only, not even a couple weeks later he basically abandoned Khangaskhan and retreated to his bedroom and refused to come out.

All because some paparazzi had accidentally gave him a fright when calling out to him from behind and trying to ask him some questions. Ever since then, he'd given up pokemon training, given up leaving his room and dedicated his life to being a NEET.

…It had been over eight years now since that day.

"Oh yeah, totally!" Shauna broke her from those morose thoughts and bounced on her feet excitedly, "See, when I went to Lumiose with my parents a few days ago, there was one of those showc-"

She never got to finish as a massive, piercing shriek thundered through the air, and a giant shadow blotted out the sun from above.

As one, the four of them looked up on instinct just in time to see an absolutely gargantuan bird pokemon fly over them. Absolutely massive, with beautiful feathers of cream and brown, and a majestic mane of blonde and pink flowing in the wind behind it.

"Holy crap!" Tierno exclaimed, jumping to his feet, gaping at the massive pokemon flying off and disappearing beyond the buildings.

"What…what the heck was that?!" Serena couldn't help but ask herself, that pokemon was absolutely titanic! And those wings! They were longer than her rhyhorn!

"I…think that was a Pidgeot!" Trevor uttered in awe, "Fascinating, I had no idea they could even get that big. This is my first time seeing one too! Oh if only it was flying slower I could have taken a picture!"

"Pidgeot?" Shauna cocked her head to the side.

"The fully evolved form of Pidgey and Pidgeotto," Trevor whirled to her excitedly, eyes glinting with his fascination. It was his dream after all, to photograph all types of pokemon throughout the world, "They're super rare. While Pidgey and Pidgeotto are super common, few will ever evolve into a Pidgeot. It's said only the most talented trainers will be able to raise a Pidgey to a Pidgeot."

Huh, was that so?

Serena's blue eyes roamed to the empty air where the giant bird had flown past, "There was somebody on its back too, I think I saw a saddle as well." she mused.

Shauna clapped her hands together before pumping her fist, "Do you think that means they're here for the tournament?" she wondered.

"Most likely," Tierno turned back to them and nodded sagely, crossing his arms, "There's a decent prize money for the winner, but the real prize is that Professor Sycamore will let the winner pick any of the starter pokemon he has."

"Then it looks like the tournament will be really exciting huh?" Serena looked back at them, "I wonder if that trainer will use that huge Pidgeot in the tournament?"

"I hope not!" Tierno grimaced, "I'm in the tournament remember. I've got a Pikachu, but…I don't think type advantage is gonna help much with a Pidgeot that big."

Shauna gave him a shit eating smirk, "Better luck next time Tiernie!" she mock consoled him, patting his shoulder.

"Hey, don't count me out just yet!" he complained, "My Wartortle has totally mastered all my moves and knows Ice Beam!" he shot finger guns her way and gave both girls a roguish wink.

"Good luck with that." Serena laughed, turning away and looking to the cafe across the street, "Let's go get something to eat, and maybe a coffee, we've got some time left before the tournament starts anyway."

# 200: Chapter 197

If you wanna read ahead of my stories, you can check out the advanced chapters on my subcribestar, at the same username - 0Jordinio0

__________

"Fletchling, get back here!"

Shouting in annoyance, Serena chased the flying type through the streets of Vaniville, "Why do you always do this?" she growled, sliding into a skid as her technical pokemon ascended over a row of shops, and beyond into a small park.

In his beak, her pink purse dangled tauntingly.

He just loved to lead her on a merry chase and do anything he could to annoy her. More the fool was her for letting him out in public like this.

With so many people around and visiting their quaint little town, no way he could have resisted getting up to mischief.

And his favourite brand of mischief was causing her problems!

Like stealing her purse when she was about to pay for her meal. She had to leave Shauna to handle the bill. Tierno and Trevor had left ahead of them, but had left their share at least.

Unlike her because of this little twerp of a bird!

Tierno wanted to get some last minute training in before the tournament started. Where as, Trevor had come across some news article from Kanto, about some guy named Roy who had been washed out to sea and tragically passed away.

She hadn't even got the chance to feel bad for said person because of Fletchling!

Panting, face flushed and sweating noticeably she was in a right foul mood when she made it into the park.

'Where are you, you little menace!?' she growled inwardly.

As she searched through the forest, finding no sign or feather of the little menace - and for a moment thought Fletchling had faked her out and returned to their home, she heard a voice.

"You're a right cheeky one aren't you?"

A boys voice, thick with an accent that reminded her of some people from Galar. She quickly looked around the tree out of slight curiosity-

And her eyes widened as she saw a young boy, a few years younger than her going by his face. Cute, with spiky sideswept brown hair and pale blue eyes, dressed in a sleeveless white hoodie, baggy black pants tucked into stylish black boots with faint dark blue markings that looked just lovely.

The most stand out things about him though, that drew are eyes. Were the thick red metallic bracelet on his wrist, and a gleaming black shiny pokeball hanging on a chain around his neck.

Oh, that and how muscly he was. Wow.

But more importantly, and the reason why her eyes widened. Was the annoying Fletchling she was looking for, resting quite the thing on the boys wrist. Acting all innocent as the boy gently ruffled the back of his evil little feathered neck!

Something about him was oddly familiar, but that wasn't important! The demon bird was.

"I don't think this is yours though, is it?" the boy snorted in amusement as he stopped petting Fletchling and gently pulled the purse from his beak.

And Fletchling just let him!

She'd have to wrestle with him to get it back even after catching him usually. Her eye almost twitched as she watched Fletchling give a sheepish barking chuckle, rubbing the back of his head with one wing.

As if to say 'aw shucks'.

Where was that attitude with her!? Where was the smug look? The laughing squawk of mockery?

She knew it wasn't just her, even if she was Fletchling's favourite target to annoy. He did it to everyone, besides her mother at least.

Who was this kid?

"That's cuz it's mine, as is the menace roosting on your arm," Serena said, making her way around the tree, "That little annoyance stole it from me to lead me on a chase."

The boy turned to her at her voice, and his eyes widened a bit as he looked at her for some reason, a startled looking expression appearing on his face, "Ah sorry, didn't mean to startle you." she raised a hand.

"Ah…" the boy gaped at her for a moment, before closing his mouth and shaking his head. An easy grin appearing on his face, "Don't worry about it. No harm done."

He held the purse up to her, "You've got a bit of a hellion on your hands with this one, huh?" he chuckled, inclining his head at Fletchling who had the nerve to preen proudly.

As if it were a compliment!

Serena took the purse gratefully, eyeing Fletchling in disbelief, "That's underselling it, he's never like this. You wouldn't believe how bad he is sometimes," she shook her head, "How can you even tell with how good he's being for you?"

The boy's chuckle turned into full laugh, "Let's just say I'm a bit of an expert on smug birds," he gave her a wink, his eyes drifting to Fletchling a moment later, "And this one definitely fits the bill."

"You're not wrong there," Serena rolled her eyes as she put her purse away in her skirt pocket, and while her hand was in there, settled on a certain pokeball. She brought her hand back out, pokeball aimed, and with a press of a button, red light washed over Fletchling who gave her a squawk of annoyance, before he was returned, "Yeah well, you deserve it." she stuck her tongue out.

The boy snorted in amusement.

"So you're a trainer then?" she asked as she stuffed the pokeball in her pocket, eyes lingering on the expensive looking pokeball hanging from the chain around his neck, "You're awfully young." she noted.

And was impressed. So young, and he looked like he already well knew what he was doing with his life, huh? She was a little jealous.

"Yeah, I got my license a few years early, it's a bit of a thing," the boy smiled easily, and held his hand out, "I'm Jord by the way, nice to meet you."

She smiled back, placed her hand in his, "I'm Serena, nice to meet you to…" she trailed off for a second as he brought her hand up and pressed his lips against the back of, "Oh!" she uttered, in surprise.

He gave her a cheeky wink as he let go of her hand, "Sorry, couldn't resist. I heard that was a popular greeting in Kalos, and I just got here, so figured I'd try it out."

Her cheeks heated a bit, and she couldn't help but giggle a little, "You're bold huh?"
